update() {
  cat >&2 <<'MSG'
The old `update` alias was removed because it could run broad config/mount/bootstrap work.
Use one of:
  config help    Usage info.
  config pull    Will sync from /mnt/distrohome/.configfiles/$WSLUSER
  config push    Will push to /mnt/egress/$WSLUSER
MSG
  return 2
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Quiet Conda and shell aliases. Safe to source from shell startup.

if [[ -x "$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" ]]; then
  __conda_setup="$("$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" shell.bash hook 2>/dev/null)"
  if [[ $? -eq 0 && -n "$__conda_setup" ]]; then
    eval "$__conda_setup"
  else
    export PATH="$HOME/anaconda3/bin:$PATH"
  fi
  unset __conda_setup
fi

Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/config.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# set -euo pipefail

# =============================================================================
# config / conda / lv helper script
#
# Execution model
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# - System-level installs:
#     apt packages, Docker engine, Terraform, kubectl, minikube,
#     SQL ODBC/sqlcmd tools, mount helpers, /mnt cleanup, repo files, keyrings.
#
# - TARGET_USER-level installs:
#     Anaconda under TARGET_HOME, pyenv under TARGET_HOME,
#     dotnet global tools under TARGET_HOME, pip --user packages,
#     shell init snippets in TARGET_HOME dotfiles.
#
# Access model
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# - System packages are installed once and are usable by all users if PATH and
#   permissions allow it.
# - TARGET_USER-specific tools/config live under TARGET_HOME and are installed
#   by running commands as TARGET_USER with HOME forced to TARGET_HOME.
# - Docker is system-level software, but TARGET_USER is added to the docker
#   group so they can use it without sudo after re-login/newgrp.
# - Terraform, kubectl, minikube, sqlcmd are installed system-wide and are
#   directly usable by TARGET_USER from normal shell PATH locations.
#
# Important use contract
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# - Source this file when you want the helper functions and aliases:
#     source ~/.local/bin/config.sh
# - Execute this file when you want the installer / mount workflow:
#     bash ~/.local/bin/config.sh
# =============================================================================

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Target and session context
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
if [[ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]]; then
  CONFIG_INITIAL_TARGET_USER="${TARGET_USER:-$(id -un)}"
else
  CONFIG_INITIAL_TARGET_USER="${TARGET_USER:-${SUDO_USER:-vmuser}}"
fi

CONFIG_SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd -P)"
CONFIG_LIB_DIR="${CONFIG_LIB_DIR:-/home/vmuser/.local/lib/config-sh}"
CONFIG_INSTALLERS_LIB="${CONFIG_INSTALLERS_LIB:-$CONFIG_LIB_DIR/installers.sh}"
CONFIG_LOADED_FILES=""

config_target_env_storage_dir() {
  printf '%s\n' "$(config_global_config_dir)/targets"
}

config_target_env_storage_owner_group() {
  local path owner group

  for path in "$(config_target_env_storage_dir)" "$(config_global_config_dir)"; do
    [[ -e "$path" ]] || continue
    owner="$(stat -c '%U' "$path" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    group="$(stat -c '%G' "$path" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [[ -n "$owner" && -n "$group" && "$owner" != "UNKNOWN" && "$group" != "UNKNOWN" ]] || continue
    printf '%s:%s\n' "$owner" "$group"
    return 0
  done

  echo "[ERROR] Could not derive target env storage owner/group from $(config_global_config_dir)." >&2
  return 1
}

config_trim_whitespace() {
  printf '%s' "$1" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//; s/[[:space:]]*$//'
}

  unset CONFIG_DEFAULT_SMB_USER_VMUSER CONFIG_DEFAULT_SMB_USER_LABUSER

  unset PYTHON_ENV_PROFILE PYTHON_ENV PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER

config_record_loaded_file() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  [[ -n "$file" ]] || return 0
  if [[ -n "$CONFIG_LOADED_FILES" ]]; then
    CONFIG_LOADED_FILES+=$'\n'
  fi
  CONFIG_LOADED_FILES+="$file"
}

config_load_env_file() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local raw line key value lineno=0

  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 0

  while IFS= read -r raw || [[ -n "$raw" ]]; do
    lineno=$((lineno + 1))
    line="$(config_trim_whitespace "$raw")"
    [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue
    [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue

    if [[ ! "$line" =~ ^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)=(.*)$ ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Ignoring invalid config line at $file:$lineno" >&2
      continue
    fi

    key="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    value="$(config_trim_whitespace "${BASH_REMATCH[2]}")"

    if [[ "$value" == *'$('* || "$value" == *'`'* || "$value" == *'|'* || "$value" == *'&'* || "$value" == *'<'* || "$value" == *'>'* || "$value" == *'('* || "$value" == *')'* ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Ignoring unsafe config line at $file:$lineno for $key" >&2
      continue
    fi

    if [[ "$value" =~ ^\"(.*)\"$ ]]; then
      value="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    elif [[ "$value" =~ ^\'(.*)\'$ ]]; then
      value="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    fi

    export "$key=$value"
  done < "$file"

  config_record_loaded_file "$file"
}

config_load_config_files() {
  local base
  base="$(config_global_config_dir)"
  CONFIG_LOADED_FILES=""
  config_clear_loaded_policy_vars
  config_load_env_file "$base/config.env"
  config_load_env_file "$(config_mounts_config_dir)/mounts.env"
  config_load_env_file "$base/targets/${TARGET_USER}.env"
  config_load_env_file "$(config_install_config_dir)/packages.env"
  config_load_env_file "$(config_install_config_dir)/versions.env"
  config_load_env_file "$(config_install_config_dir)/repos.env"
}

config_tsv_data_rows() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local raw line

  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 0

  while IFS= read -r raw || [[ -n "$raw" ]]; do
    line="$(config_trim_whitespace "${raw%%$'\r'}")"
    [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue
    [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    printf '%s\n' "$line"
  done < "$file"
}

config_account_profile_rows() {
  config_tsv_data_rows "$(config_account_profiles_file)"
}

config_smb_profile_rows() {
  config_tsv_data_rows "$(config_smb_profiles_file)"
}

config_mount_profile_rows() {
  config_tsv_data_rows "$(config_mount_profiles_file)"
}

config_python_env_profile_rows() {
  local row profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra

  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra <<< "$row"
    if [[ -n "${extra:-}" || -z "$profile_name" || -z "$target_role" || -z "$python_env" || -z "$env_manager" || -z "$create_missing" || -z "$allow_system" || -z "$description" ]]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Invalid Python env profile row: $row" >&2
      echo "[ERROR] Expected columns: profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description" >&2
      return 1
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$row"
  done < <(config_tsv_data_rows "$(config_python_env_profiles_file)")
}

config_find_tsv_row_by_first_column() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local key="${2:-}"
  local raw row first rest

  [[ -n "$file" && -n "$key" ]] || return 2

  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 1
  while IFS= read -r raw || [[ -n "$raw" ]]; do
    row="$(config_trim_whitespace "${raw%%$'\r'}")"
    [[ -n "$row" ]] || continue
    [[ "$row" == \#* ]] && continue
    IFS=$'\t' read -r first rest <<< "$row"
    [[ "$first" == "$key" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$row"
    return 0
  done < "$file"

  return 1
}

config_find_account_profile() {
  config_find_tsv_row_by_first_column "$(config_account_profiles_file)" "${1:-}"
}

config_find_smb_profile() {
  config_find_tsv_row_by_first_column "$(config_smb_profiles_file)" "${1:-}"
}

config_find_mount_profile() {
  config_find_tsv_row_by_first_column "$(config_mount_profiles_file)" "${1:-}"
}

config_find_python_env_profile() {
  local key="${1:-}"
  local row profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra

  [[ -n "$key" ]] || return 2

  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra <<< "$row"
    [[ "$profile_name" == "$key" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$row"
    return 0
  done < <(config_python_env_profile_rows)

  return 1
}

config_resolve_python_env_profile_for_account_profile() {
  local account_profile="${1:-}"
  local target_role="${2:-}"
  local row profile_name row_target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra

  if [[ -n "$account_profile" ]]; then
    row="$(config_find_python_env_profile "$account_profile" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [[ -n "$row" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$row"
      return 0
    fi
  fi

  [[ -n "$target_role" ]] || return 1

  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r profile_name row_target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra <<< "$row"
    [[ "$row_target_role" == "$target_role" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$row"
    return 0
  done < <(config_python_env_profile_rows)

  return 1
}

config_known_profile_for_name() {
  local kind="${1:-}"
  local name="${2:-}"

  case "$name" in
    vmuser) printf '%s\n' "DefaultOperator" ;;
    labuser) printf '%s\n' "DefaultTarget" ;;
    AIEngineer|aiengineer) printf '%s\n' "AIEngineer" ;;
    ResearchScientist|researchscientist) printf '%s\n' "ResearchScientist" ;;
    Publisher|publisher) printf '%s\n' "Publisher" ;;
    *)
      case "$kind" in
        operator) printf '%s\n' "DefaultOperator" ;;
        *) printf '%s\n' "DefaultTarget" ;;
      esac
      ;;
  esac
}

config_resolve_account_profile_name() {
  local kind="${1:-target}"
  local requested_profile="${2:-}"
  local requested_name="${3:-}"

  if [[ -n "$requested_profile" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$requested_profile"
  else
    config_known_profile_for_name "$kind" "$requested_name"
  fi
}

config_bool_is_true() {
  case "${1:-0}" in
    1|yes|true|on|Y|y) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

config_group_exists() {
  local name="${1:-}"
  [[ -n "$name" ]] || return 1
  getent group "$name" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

config_prompt_research_role_group() {
  local preferred="${1:-researchscientist}"
  local mode="${2:-missing}"
  local answer=""

  if [[ "$mode" == "both" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] Both Research Scientist role groups exist:" >&2
    echo "  1) researchscientist  (preferred default)" >&2
    echo "  2) researchassistent  (existing compatibility group)" >&2
  else
    echo "[INFO] No Research Scientist role group exists yet." >&2
    echo "  1) researchscientist  (preferred default)" >&2
    echo "  2) researchassistent  (compatibility spelling)" >&2
  fi

  while true; do
    read -r -p "Select Research Scientist role group [default=${preferred}]: " answer
    case "$answer" in
      ""|1|researchscientist)
        printf '%s\n' "researchscientist"
        return 0
        ;;
      2|researchassistent)
        printf '%s\n' "researchassistent"
        return 0
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Invalid Research Scientist role group selection: $answer" >&2
        ;;
    esac
  done
}

config_resolve_research_scientist_role_group() {
  local interactive="${1:-0}"
  local explicit_role_group="${2:-}"
  local has_preferred=0 has_compat=0

  if [[ -n "$explicit_role_group" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$explicit_role_group"
    return 0
  fi

  config_group_exists researchscientist && has_preferred=1
  config_group_exists researchassistent && has_compat=1

  if (( has_preferred )) && (( !has_compat )); then
    printf '%s\n' "researchscientist"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( !has_preferred )) && (( has_compat )); then
    printf '%s\n' "researchassistent"
    return 0
  fi
  if (( has_preferred )) && (( has_compat )); then
    if (( interactive )); then
      config_prompt_research_role_group "researchscientist" "both"
      return 0
    fi
    echo "[ERROR] Both researchscientist and researchassistent exist." >&2
    echo "[ERROR] Re-run with --role-group researchscientist or --role-group researchassistent." >&2
    return 1
  fi

  if (( interactive )); then
    config_prompt_research_role_group "researchscientist" "missing"
  else
    printf '%s\n' "researchscientist"
  fi
}

config_account_plan_clear() {
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_KIND ACCOUNT_PLAN_DEFAULT_USERNAME
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE_LABEL ACCOUNT_PLAN_BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_PROFILE ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PROFILE ACCOUNT_PLAN_SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_CREATE_LINUX_USER ACCOUNT_PLAN_ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_DESCRIPTION ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_DEFAULT ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_INGRESS
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_EGRESS ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_RESEARCH ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_PUBLISH ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_DISTROHOME ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_SCRIPTING
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_INGRESS ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_EGRESS ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_RESEARCH ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PUBLISH ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ENV_FILE
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_PROFILE ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER
  unset ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_CREATE_MISSING ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM
}

config_resolve_account_profile() {
  local profile_name="${1:-}"
  local requested_name="${2:-}"
  local smb_override="${3:-}"
  local mount_override="${4:-}"
  local role_group_override="${5:-}"
  local interactive_mode="${6:-0}"
  local row smb_row mount_row
  local account_kind default_username primary_group role_group home_mode target_role target_role_label
  local bootstrap_profile smb_profile mount_profile sync_system_items create_linux_user add_operator_to_role_group description extra
  local smb_user_default smb_user_ingress smb_user_egress smb_user_research smb_user_publish
  local mount_distrohome mount_scripting mount_ingress mount_egress mount_research mount_publish
  local python_env_row python_env_profile python_env_target_role python_env python_env_manager python_env_create_missing python_env_allow_system python_env_description
  local smb_description mount_description

  [[ -n "$profile_name" ]] || return 2
  row="$(config_find_account_profile "$profile_name")" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown account profile: $profile_name" >&2
    return 1
  }

  IFS=$'\t' read -r ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME account_kind default_username primary_group role_group home_mode \
    target_role target_role_label bootstrap_profile smb_profile mount_profile sync_system_items \
    create_linux_user add_operator_to_role_group description extra <<< "$row"
  [[ -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid account profile row: $profile_name" >&2
    return 1
  }

  [[ -n "$requested_name" ]] || requested_name="$default_username"
  [[ -n "$requested_name" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve account name for profile: $profile_name" >&2
    return 1
  }

  if [[ -n "$smb_override" ]]; then
    smb_profile="$smb_override"
  fi
  if [[ -n "$mount_override" ]]; then
    mount_profile="$mount_override"
  fi

  if [[ "$profile_name" == "ResearchScientist" ]]; then
    role_group="$(config_resolve_research_scientist_role_group "$interactive_mode" "$role_group_override")" || return 1
  elif [[ -n "$role_group_override" ]]; then
    role_group="$role_group_override"
  fi

  smb_row="$(config_find_smb_profile "$smb_profile")" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown SMB profile: $smb_profile" >&2
    return 1
  }
  IFS=$'\t' read -r _ smb_user_default smb_user_ingress smb_user_egress smb_user_research smb_user_publish mount_distrohome mount_scripting mount_ingress mount_egress mount_research mount_publish smb_description extra <<< "$smb_row"
  [[ -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid SMB profile row: $smb_profile" >&2
    return 1
  }

  mount_row="$(config_find_mount_profile "$mount_profile")" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown mount profile: $mount_profile" >&2
    return 1
  }
  IFS=$'\t' read -r _ mount_distrohome mount_scripting mount_ingress mount_egress mount_research mount_publish mount_description extra <<< "$mount_row"
  [[ -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid mount profile row: $mount_profile" >&2
    return 1
  }

  python_env_row="$(config_resolve_python_env_profile_for_account_profile "$profile_name" "$target_role")" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve Python env profile for account profile=$profile_name target_role=$target_role" >&2
    return 1
  }
  IFS=$'\t' read -r python_env_profile python_env_target_role python_env python_env_manager python_env_create_missing python_env_allow_system python_env_description extra <<< "$python_env_row"
  [[ -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid Python env profile row resolved for: $profile_name" >&2
    return 1
  }

  config_account_plan_clear
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME="$profile_name"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_KIND="$account_kind"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_DEFAULT_USERNAME="$default_username"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP="$primary_group"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP="$role_group"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE="$home_mode"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE="$target_role"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE_LABEL="$target_role_label"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE="$bootstrap_profile"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_PROFILE="$smb_profile"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PROFILE="$mount_profile"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS="$sync_system_items"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_CREATE_LINUX_USER="$create_linux_user"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP="$add_operator_to_role_group"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_DESCRIPTION="$description"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_DEFAULT="$smb_user_default"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_INGRESS="$smb_user_ingress"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_EGRESS="$smb_user_egress"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_RESEARCH="$smb_user_research"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_PUBLISH="$smb_user_publish"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_DISTROHOME="$mount_distrohome"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_SCRIPTING="$mount_scripting"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_INGRESS="$mount_ingress"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_EGRESS="$mount_egress"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_RESEARCH="$mount_research"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PUBLISH="$mount_publish"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_PROFILE="$python_env_profile"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV="$python_env"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER="$python_env_manager"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_CREATE_MISSING="$python_env_create_missing"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM="$python_env_allow_system"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME="$requested_name"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME="/home/$requested_name"
  ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ENV_FILE="$(config_target_config_file_for_user "$requested_name")"
}

config_print_account_plan_summary() {
  cat <<EOF
You selected ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME}:
  Linux user: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}
  primary group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}
  role group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}
  home mode: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE}
  target role: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE}
  label: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE_LABEL}
  bootstrap profile: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE}
  SMB profile: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_PROFILE}
  mount profile: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PROFILE}
  mounts: ingress=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_INGRESS}, egress=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_EGRESS}, research=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_RESEARCH}, publish=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PUBLISH}, distrohome=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_DISTROHOME}, scripting=${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_SCRIPTING}
  sync system items: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS}
  create linux user: $(config_bool_is_true "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_CREATE_LINUX_USER}" && printf yes || printf no)
  add Operator (vmuser) to role group: $(config_bool_is_true "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP}" && printf yes || printf no)
EOF
}

config_account_profile_to_target_env() {
  cat <<EOF
# >>> config account profile managed >>>
ACCOUNT_KIND="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_KIND}"
ACCOUNT_PROFILE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME}"
PRIMARY_GROUP="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}"
ROLE_GROUP="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}"
HOME_MODE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE}"
TARGET_ROLE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE}"
TARGET_ROLE_LABEL="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ROLE_LABEL}"
BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE}"
SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS}"
SMB_PROFILE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_PROFILE}"
SMB_USER_DEFAULT="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_DEFAULT}"
SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_INGRESS}"
SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_EGRESS}"
SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_RESEARCH}"
SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_USER_PUBLISH}"
MOUNT_PROFILE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PROFILE}"
MOUNT_DISTROHOME="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_DISTROHOME}"
MOUNT_SCRIPTING="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_SCRIPTING}"
MOUNT_INGRESS="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_INGRESS}"
MOUNT_EGRESS="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_EGRESS}"
MOUNT_RESEARCH="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_RESEARCH}"
MOUNT_PUBLISH="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PUBLISH}"
PYTHON_ENV_PROFILE="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_PROFILE}"
PYTHON_ENV="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV}"
PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER}"
PYTHON_ENV_CREATE_MISSING="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_CREATE_MISSING}"
PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM}"
CREATE_LINUX_USER="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_CREATE_LINUX_USER}"
ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP}"
# <<< config account profile managed <<<
EOF
}

config_strip_managed_block() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  python3 - "$file" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys

path = Path(sys.argv[1])
if not path.exists():
    sys.exit(0)

lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
inside = False
for line in lines:
    if line.strip() == "# >>> config account profile managed >>>":
        inside = True
        continue
    if line.strip() == "# <<< config account profile managed <<<":
        inside = False
        continue
    if not inside:
        print(line)
PY
}

config_reconcile_target_env_file() {
  local target_user="${1:-}"
  local file="${2:-}"
  local dry_run="${3:-0}"
  local preserved managed_block targets_dir owner_group config_owner config_group

  [[ -n "$target_user" && -n "$file" ]] || return 2

  targets_dir="$(dirname "$file")"
  owner_group="$(config_target_env_storage_owner_group)" || return 1
  config_owner="${owner_group%%:*}"
  config_group="${owner_group##*:}"
  preserved="$(config_strip_managed_block "$file")"
  managed_block="$(config_account_profile_to_target_env)"

  if [[ "$dry_run" == "1" ]]; then
    echo "Would ensure target env directory: $targets_dir"
    echo "  final owner/group: ${config_owner}:${config_group}"
    echo "  final mode: 700"
    echo "Would write target env file: $file"
    echo "  final owner/group: ${config_owner}:${config_group}"
    echo "  final mode: 600"
    if [[ -n "$preserved" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$preserved"
      [[ "$preserved" == *$'\n' ]] || printf '\n'
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$managed_block"
    return 0
  fi

  install -d -m 700 "$targets_dir"
  chown "${config_owner}:${config_group}" "$targets_dir"
  chmod 700 "$targets_dir"
  {
    if [[ -n "$preserved" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$preserved"
      [[ "$preserved" == *$'\n' ]] || printf '\n'
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$managed_block"
  } > "$file"
  chown "${config_owner}:${config_group}" "$file"
  chmod 600 "$file"
}

config_list_account_profiles() {
  local row name kind default_username primary_group role_group home_mode target_role target_role_label bootstrap_profile smb_profile mount_profile sync_system_items create_linux_user add_operator_to_role_group description extra
  local n=0
  echo "Available account profiles:"
  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r name kind default_username primary_group role_group home_mode target_role target_role_label bootstrap_profile smb_profile mount_profile sync_system_items create_linux_user add_operator_to_role_group description extra <<< "$row"
    n=$((n + 1))
    printf "  %d) %-18s kind=%-8s user=%-18s role-group=%-18s label=%-20s smb=%-18s mount=%-10s sync=%s  %s\n" \
      "$n" "$name" "$kind" "$default_username" "$role_group" "$target_role_label" "$smb_profile" "$mount_profile" "$sync_system_items" "$description"
  done < <(config_account_profile_rows)
}

config_account_profiles_by_kind() {
  local wanted_kind="${1:-}"
  local row name account_kind rest

  [[ -n "$wanted_kind" ]] || return 2

  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r name account_kind rest <<< "$row"
    [[ "$account_kind" == "$wanted_kind" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$name"
  done < <(config_account_profile_rows)
}

config_print_account_profiles_by_kind() {
  local wanted_kind="${1:-}"
  local profile
  local found=0

  while IFS= read -r profile; do
    [[ -n "$profile" ]] || continue
    printf '  - %s\n' "$profile" >&2
    found=1
  done < <(config_account_profiles_by_kind "$wanted_kind")

  (( found )) || printf '  (none found)\n' >&2
}

config_list_smb_profiles() {
  local row name smb_user_default smb_user_ingress smb_user_egress smb_user_research smb_user_publish a b c d e f description extra
  local n=0
  echo "Available SMB profiles:"
  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r name smb_user_default smb_user_ingress smb_user_egress smb_user_research smb_user_publish a b c d e f description extra <<< "$row"
    n=$((n + 1))
    printf "  %d) %-18s default=%-12s ingress=%-12s egress=%-12s research=%-12s publish=%-12s  %s\n" \
      "$n" "$name" "$smb_user_default" "$smb_user_ingress" "$smb_user_egress" "$smb_user_research" "$smb_user_publish" "$description"
  done < <(config_smb_profile_rows)
}

config_list_mount_profiles() {
  local row name mount_distrohome mount_scripting mount_ingress mount_egress mount_research mount_publish description extra
  local n=0
  echo "Available mount profiles:"
  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r name mount_distrohome mount_scripting mount_ingress mount_egress mount_research mount_publish description extra <<< "$row"
    n=$((n + 1))
    printf "  %d) %-18s distrohome=%s scripting=%s ingress=%s egress=%s research=%s publish=%s  %s\n" \
      "$n" "$name" "$mount_distrohome" "$mount_scripting" "$mount_ingress" "$mount_egress" "$mount_research" "$mount_publish" "$description"
  done < <(config_mount_profile_rows)
}

config_list_python_env_profiles() {
  local row profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra
  local n=0
  echo "Available Python env profiles:"
  while IFS= read -r row; do
    IFS=$'\t' read -r profile_name target_role python_env env_manager create_missing allow_system description extra <<< "$row"
    n=$((n + 1))
    printf "  %d) %-18s role=%-20s env=%-18s manager=%-6s create_missing=%s allow_system=%s  %s\n" \
      "$n" "$profile_name" "$target_role" "$python_env" "$env_manager" "$create_missing" "$allow_system" "$description"
  done < <(config_python_env_profile_rows)
}

config_profile_name_by_index() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local index="${2:-}"
  local raw row current=0 name extra
  [[ "$index" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return 2
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 1
  while IFS= read -r raw || [[ -n "$raw" ]]; do
    row="$(config_trim_whitespace "${raw%%$'\r'}")"
    [[ -n "$row" ]] || continue
    [[ "$row" == \#* ]] && continue
    current=$((current + 1))
    [[ $current -eq $index ]] || continue
    IFS=$'\t' read -r name extra <<< "$row"
    printf '%s\n' "$name"
    return 0
  done < "$file"
  return 1
}

config_prompt_for_profile_selection() {
  local label="${1:-profile}"
  local file="${2:-}"
  local default_name="${3:-}"
  local allow_blank_keep="${4:-0}"
  local prompt answer resolved

  while true; do
    if [[ "$allow_blank_keep" == "1" ]]; then
      prompt="Select ${label} number or press Enter to keep ${default_name}: "
    else
      prompt="Select ${label} number [default=${default_name}]: "
    fi
    read -r -p "$prompt" answer
    if [[ -z "$answer" ]]; then
      if [[ "$allow_blank_keep" == "1" || -n "$default_name" ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "$default_name"
        return 0
      fi
      continue
    fi
    if [[ "$answer" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
      resolved="$(config_profile_name_by_index "$file" "$answer" 2>/dev/null || true)"
      if [[ -n "$resolved" ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "$resolved"
        return 0
      fi
    fi
    if config_find_tsv_row_by_first_column "$file" "$answer" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      printf '%s\n' "$answer"
      return 0
    fi
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid ${label} selection: ${answer}" >&2
  done
}

config_write_example_config_file() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local body="${2:-}"
  local label="${3:-config file}"
  local force="${4:-0}"

  if [[ -f "$file" && "$force" != "1" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] Existing file kept: $file"
    return 0
  fi

  printf '%s' "$body" > "$file"
  chmod 600 "$file"
  echo "[INFO] Created $label: $file"
}

config_init_example_files() {
  local force=0
  local config_dir targets_dir bootstrap_dir profile_dir install_dir mounts_dir accounts_dir
  local global_file vmuser_file labuser_file steps_file default_plan admin_plan lab_plan
  local operator_plan default_target_plan ai_engineer_plan research_scientist_plan publisher_plan
  local packages_file versions_file repos_file python_env_profiles_file mounts_file account_profiles_file smb_profiles_file mount_profiles_file
  local global_body vmuser_body labuser_body steps_body default_plan_body admin_plan_body lab_plan_body
  local operator_plan_body default_target_plan_body ai_engineer_plan_body research_scientist_plan_body publisher_plan_body
  local packages_body versions_body repos_body python_env_profiles_body mounts_body account_profiles_body smb_profiles_body mount_profiles_body

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --force) force=1 ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unsupported config-init option: $1" >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  config_dir="$(config_global_config_dir)"
  targets_dir="$config_dir/targets"
  bootstrap_dir="$config_dir/bootstrap"
  profile_dir="$bootstrap_dir/profiles"
  install_dir="$config_dir/install"
  mounts_dir="$config_dir/mounts"
  accounts_dir="$config_dir/accounts"
  global_file="$config_dir/config.env"
  vmuser_file="$targets_dir/vmuser.env"
  labuser_file="$targets_dir/labuser.env"
  steps_file="$bootstrap_dir/steps.tsv"
  default_plan="$profile_dir/default.plan"
  admin_plan="$profile_dir/admin.plan"
  lab_plan="$profile_dir/lab.plan"
  operator_plan="$profile_dir/operator.plan"
  default_target_plan="$profile_dir/default-target.plan"
  ai_engineer_plan="$profile_dir/ai-engineer.plan"
  research_scientist_plan="$profile_dir/research-scientist.plan"
  publisher_plan="$profile_dir/publisher.plan"
  packages_file="$install_dir/packages.env"
  versions_file="$install_dir/versions.env"
  repos_file="$install_dir/repos.env"
  python_env_profiles_file="$install_dir/python-env-profiles.tsv"
  mounts_file="$mounts_dir/mounts.env"
  account_profiles_file="$accounts_dir/profiles.tsv"
  smb_profiles_file="$accounts_dir/smb-profiles.tsv"
  mount_profiles_file="$accounts_dir/mount-profiles.tsv"

  install -d -m 700 "$config_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $config_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$targets_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $targets_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$bootstrap_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $bootstrap_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$profile_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $profile_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$install_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $install_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$mounts_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $mounts_dir"
  install -d -m 700 "$accounts_dir"
  echo "[INFO] Created config directory: $accounts_dir"

  global_body='# config-sh global defaults
#
# This file controls defaults shared by all target users.
# It is safe to edit these values.
#
# Load order:
#   1. built-in script defaults
#   2. this global config file
#   3. targets/USER.env
#   4. environment variables
#   5. command-line options
#
# Do not put passwords or SMB secrets in this file.
# Use credentials files under ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts instead.

# Users that are allowed to sync system-level files during pull/push.
# Space-separated list.
CONFIG_ADMIN_TARGETS="vmuser"

# Default behavior for syncing /etc/resolv.conf.
# 0 = exclude unless explicitly requested
# 1 = include by default
CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF_DEFAULT="0"

# Default SMB username mapping.
# Per-target files may override these with SMB_USER_DEFAULT.
CONFIG_DEFAULT_SMB_USER_VMUSER="hector"
CONFIG_DEFAULT_SMB_USER_LABUSER="labuser"

# Default ingress/egress SMB identities.
SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT="labuser"
SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT="labuser"
SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT="Researcher"
SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT="labuser"

# Default WSL distro name used by mount templates if DISTRONAME is unset.
MOUNTS_DISTRONAME_DEFAULT="jepabio-Ubuntu-22.04"

# Default bootstrap profile when target config does not override it.
BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE_DEFAULT="default"
'
  vmuser_body='# Target config for vmuser
#
# This file controls defaults used when running:
#   config --target vmuser ...
#
# Safe to edit:
#   TARGET_ROLE
#   SMB_USER_DEFAULT
#   BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE
#   SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS
#
# Do not put passwords here.

TARGET_ROLE="operator"
TARGET_ROLE_LABEL="Operator"
SMB_USER_DEFAULT="hector"
SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT="labuser"
SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT="labuser"
SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT="hector"
SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT="hector"
SMB_PROFILE="operator"
MOUNT_PROFILE="all"
MOUNT_DISTROHOME="1"
MOUNT_SCRIPTING="1"
MOUNT_INGRESS="1"
MOUNT_EGRESS="1"
MOUNT_RESEARCH="1"
MOUNT_PUBLISH="1"
BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE="operator"
SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS="1"
'
  labuser_body='# Target config for labuser
#
# This file controls defaults used when running:
#   config --target labuser ...
#
# Safe to edit:
#   TARGET_ROLE
#   SMB_USER_DEFAULT
#   BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE
#   SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS

config_resolve_home_for_user() {
  local user="${1:-}"
  [[ -n "$user" ]] || return 2
  getent passwd "$user" | cut -d: -f6
}

config_default_smb_user_for_target() {
  local user="${1:-}"
  local user_upper=""
  local scoped_var=""

  [[ -n "$user" ]] || return 2

  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$SMB_USER_DEFAULT"
    return 0
  fi

  user_upper="$(printf '%s' "$user" | tr '[:lower:]-' '[:upper:]_')"
  scoped_var="CONFIG_DEFAULT_SMB_USER_${user_upper}"

  if [[ -n "${!scoped_var:-}" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "${!scoped_var}"
    return 0
  fi

  case "$user" in
    vmuser) printf '%s\n' 'hector' ;;
    labuser) printf '%s\n' 'labuser' ;;
    *) printf '%s\n' "$user" ;;
  esac
}

config_refresh_session_context() {
  CURRENT_HOME="$TARGET_HOME"
  BASEDIR="${CURRENT_HOME}/.local/wsl-mounts"

  CONFIG_STATE_DIR="${TARGET_HOME}/.local/state/config-sh"
  STATE_DIR="$CONFIG_STATE_DIR"

  WSL_USER="$TARGET_USER"
  SMB_USER="${SMB_USER_OVERRIDE:-$(config_default_smb_user_for_target "$TARGET_USER")}"
  SMB_USER_INGRESS="${SMB_USER_INGRESS_OVERRIDE:-${SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
  SMB_USER_EGRESS="${SMB_USER_EGRESS_OVERRIDE:-${SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
  SMB_USER_RESEARCH="${SMB_USER_RESEARCH_OVERRIDE:-${SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT:-$SMB_USER}}"
  SMB_USER_PUBLISH="${SMB_USER_PUBLISH_OVERRIDE:-${SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT:-$SMB_USER}}"

  export CURRENT_HOME BASEDIR
  export CONFIG_STATE_DIR STATE_DIR
  export WSL_USER SMB_USER SMB_USER_INGRESS SMB_USER_EGRESS SMB_USER_RESEARCH SMB_USER_PUBLISH
}

config_set_target_user() {
  local user="${1:-}"
  local home=""

  [[ -n "$user" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Missing target user" >&2
    return 2
  }

  [[ "$user" =~ ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*[$]?$ ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid target user: $user" >&2
    return 2
  }

  home="$(config_resolve_home_for_user "$user")"
  [[ -n "$home" && -d "$home" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve home for target user: $user" >&2
    return 1
  }

  TARGET_USER="$user"
  TARGET_HOME="$home"
  export TARGET_USER TARGET_HOME

  config_load_config_files
  config_refresh_session_context
}

config_set_target_user "$CONFIG_INITIAL_TARGET_USER" || {
  rc=$?
  return "$rc" 2>/dev/null || exit "$rc"
}

LV_HELPER_FILE="$CONFIG_SCRIPT_DIR/lv.sh"
MOUNTS_HELPER_FILE="$CONFIG_SCRIPT_DIR/mounts.sh"
if [[ -f "$LV_HELPER_FILE" ]]; then
  # shellcheck source=/home/vmuser/.local/bin/lv.sh
  source "$LV_HELPER_FILE"
fi
if [[ -f "$MOUNTS_HELPER_FILE" ]]; then
  # shellcheck source=/home/vmuser/.local/bin/mounts.sh
  source "$MOUNTS_HELPER_FILE"
fi

# mounts.sh is source-safe, but config owns the canonical target/session context.
config_refresh_session_context

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers for running commands as TARGET_USER
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
target_sudo() {
  (($#)) || { echo "[ERROR] target_sudo requires a command" >&2; return 2; }
  sudo -u "${TARGET_USER}" -H env HOME="${TARGET_HOME}" "$@"
}

run_as_target() {
  (($#)) || { echo "[ERROR] run_as_target requires a command" >&2; return 2; }
  target_sudo "$@"
}

run_as_target_shell() {
  local script="${1:-}"
  [[ -n "$script" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] run_as_target_shell requires a script string" >&2; return 2; }
  target_sudo bash -lc "$script"
}

append_once_target() {
  local file="$1" line="$2"

  target_sudo bash -s -- "$file" "$line" <<'BASH'
set -euo pipefail
file="$1"
line="$2"
mkdir -p -- "$(dirname -- "$file")"
touch -- "$file"
grep -qxF -- "$line" "$file" 2>/dev/null || printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$file"
BASH
}

append_block_once_target() {
  local file="$1" marker="$2" block="$3"

  printf '%s\n' "$block" | target_sudo bash -c '
set -euo pipefail
file="$1"
marker="$2"
block="$(cat)"
mkdir -p -- "$(dirname -- "$file")"
touch -- "$file"
if ! grep -qF -- "$marker" "$file" 2>/dev/null; then
  printf "%s\n" "$block" >> "$file"
fi
' bash "$file" "$marker"
}

if [[ -f "$CONFIG_INSTALLERS_LIB" ]]; then
  # shellcheck source=/home/vmuser/.local/lib/config-sh/installers.sh
  source "$CONFIG_INSTALLERS_LIB"
else
  echo "[ERROR] Missing installers library: $CONFIG_INSTALLERS_LIB" >&2
  echo "[INFO] Restore it or reinstall the config tool." >&2
  return 1 2>/dev/null || exit 1
fi

# =============================================================================
# Config file copy helpers
# =============================================================================
config_sync_is_admin_target() {
  local admin_targets="${CONFIG_ADMIN_TARGETS:-vmuser}"

  if [[ "${SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS:-}" == "1" ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "${SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS:-}" == "0" ]]; then
    return 1
  fi

  case " $admin_targets " in
    *" ${TARGET_USER} "*) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

config_sync_include_system_items() {
  config_sync_is_admin_target
}

config_sync_include_resolv_conf() {
  local value="${CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF:-${CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF_DEFAULT:-0}}"
  [[ "$value" == "1" ]]
}

config_push_user_items() {
  local egress_root="${1:-/mnt/egress}"
  local target_root="$egress_root/$TARGET_USER"

  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "tree" "${TARGET_HOME}/.local/bin" "$target_root/bin"
  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "file" "${TARGET_HOME}/.bashrc" "$target_root/home/.bashrc"
}

config_push_system_items() {
  local egress_root="${1:-/mnt/egress}"
  local target_root="$egress_root/$TARGET_USER"

  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "tree" "/etc/netplan" "$target_root/etc/netplan"
  if config_sync_include_resolv_conf; then
    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "file" "/etc/resolv.conf" "$target_root/etc/resolv.conf/resolv.conf"
  fi
}

config_pull_user_items() {
  local distrohome="${1:-${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}}"
  local user_root="$distrohome/.configfiles/$TARGET_USER"

  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "tree" "$user_root/bin" "${TARGET_HOME}/.local/bin" "target"
  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "file" "$user_root/home/.bashrc" "${TARGET_HOME}/.bashrc" "target"
}

config_pull_system_items() {
  local distrohome="${1:-${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}}"
  local user_root="$distrohome/.configfiles/$TARGET_USER"

  printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "tree" "$user_root/etc/netplan" "/etc/netplan" "root"
  if config_sync_include_resolv_conf; then
    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "file" "$user_root/etc/resolv.conf/resolv.conf" "/etc/resolv.conf" "root"
  fi
}

CopyConfigFiles() {
  local distrohome="${1:-/mnt/distrohome}"
  local success=0
  local root_home target_user target_group target_bin_dir
  local manifest_output kind src dest owner_scope owner group mode
  local include_system=0

  root_home="${TARGET_HOME}"
  target_user="${TARGET_USER}"
  target_group="$(id -gn "$target_user" 2>/dev/null || id -gn)"
  target_bin_dir="${root_home}/.local/bin"

  copy_tree_contents() {
    local from="$1" to="$2" owner="${3:-}" group="${4:-}"
    if [[ ! -d "$from" ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Missing directory: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    if ! compgen -G "$from/*" > /dev/null; then
      echo "[WARN] Directory is empty: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    sudo mkdir -p "$to" || return 1
    sudo cp -a "$from"/. "$to"/ || return 1
    if [[ -n "$owner" && -n "$group" ]]; then
      sudo chown -R "$owner:$group" "$to" || return 1
    fi
    return 0
  }

  copy_file() {
    local from="$1" to="$2" owner="${3:-}" group="${4:-}" mode="${5:-}"
    if [[ ! -f "$from" ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Missing file: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    sudo mkdir -p "$(dirname "$to")" || return 1
    sudo cp -a "$from" "$to" || return 1
    if [[ -n "$owner" && -n "$group" ]]; then
      sudo chown "$owner:$group" "$to" || return 1
    fi
    if [[ -n "$mode" ]]; then
      sudo chmod "$mode" "$to" || return 1
    fi
    return 0
  }

  manifest_output="$(config_pull_user_items "$distrohome")"
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r kind src dest owner_scope; do
    [[ -n "${kind:-}" ]] || continue
    case "$owner_scope" in
      target)
        owner="$target_user"
        group="$target_group"
        ;;
      root)
        owner="root"
        group="root"
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[WARN] Unknown pull owner scope: $owner_scope" >&2
        success=1
        continue
        ;;
    esac

    case "$kind" in
      tree)
        if copy_tree_contents "$src" "$dest" "$owner" "$group"; then
          echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
        else
          success=1
        fi
        ;;
      file)
        mode="644"
        if copy_file "$src" "$dest" "$owner" "$group" "$mode"; then
          echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
        else
          success=1
        fi
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[WARN] Unknown pull item kind: $kind" >&2
        success=1
        ;;
    esac
  done <<< "$manifest_output"

  if config_sync_include_system_items; then
    include_system=1
    manifest_output="$(config_pull_system_items "$distrohome")"
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r kind src dest owner_scope; do
      [[ -n "${kind:-}" ]] || continue
      case "$owner_scope" in
        target)
          owner="$target_user"
          group="$target_group"
          ;;
        root)
          owner="root"
          group="root"
          ;;
        *)
          echo "[WARN] Unknown pull owner scope: $owner_scope" >&2
          success=1
          continue
          ;;
      esac

      case "$kind" in
        tree)
          if copy_tree_contents "$src" "$dest" "$owner" "$group"; then
            echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
          else
            success=1
          fi
          ;;
        file)
          mode="644"
          if copy_file "$src" "$dest" "$owner" "$group" "$mode"; then
            echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
          else
            success=1
          fi
          ;;
        *)
          echo "[WARN] Unknown pull item kind: $kind" >&2
          success=1
          ;;
      esac
    done <<< "$manifest_output"
  fi

  if (( include_system == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Skipping system sync items for non-admin target: $TARGET_USER"
  fi

  normalize_bin_modes() {
    local executable_files=(
      "$target_bin_dir/config"
      "$target_bin_dir/config.sh"
      "$target_bin_dir/pip"
      "$target_bin_dir/pip3"
      "$target_bin_dir/pip3.10"
      "$target_bin_dir/virtualenv"
    )
    local regular_files=(
      "$target_bin_dir/conda.sh"
      "$target_bin_dir/lv.sh"
      "$target_bin_dir/mounts.sh"
      "$target_bin_dir/shell-loader.sh"
    )
    local f

    for f in "${executable_files[@]}"; do
      [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
      sudo chmod 755 "$f" || return 1
    done

    for f in "${regular_files[@]}"; do
      [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
      sudo chmod 644 "$f" || return 1
    done

    return 0
  }

  if normalize_bin_modes; then
    echo "[INFO] Normalized executable modes under $target_bin_dir"
  else
    success=1
  fi

  if (( success == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Configuration files updated successfully."
  else
    echo "[WARN] Some configuration files were not updated."
    return 1
  fi
}

PushConfigFiles() {
  local egress_root="${1:-/mnt/egress}"
  local success=0
  local root_home target_root
  local manifest_output kind src dest
  local include_system=0

  root_home="${TARGET_HOME}"
  target_root="$egress_root/$TARGET_USER"

  push_tree_contents() {
    local from="$1" to="$2"
    if [[ ! -d "$from" ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Missing directory: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    if ! compgen -G "$from/*" > /dev/null; then
      echo "[WARN] Directory is empty: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    sudo mkdir -p "$to" || return 1
    sudo cp -a "$from"/. "$to"/ || return 1
    return 0
  }

  push_file() {
    local from="$1" to="$2" mode
    if [[ ! -f "$from" ]]; then
      echo "[WARN] Missing file: $from"
      return 1
    fi
    mode="$(stat -c '%a' "$from" 2>/dev/null)" || mode="644"
    sudo mkdir -p "$(dirname "$to")" || return 1
    sudo rm -f "$to" || return 1
    sudo tee "$to" >/dev/null < "$from" || return 1
    sudo chmod "$mode" "$to" || return 1
    return 0
  }

  manifest_output="$(config_push_user_items "$egress_root")"
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r kind src dest; do
    [[ -n "${kind:-}" ]] || continue
    case "$kind" in
      tree)
        if push_tree_contents "$src" "$dest"; then
          echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
        else
          success=1
        fi
        ;;
      file)
        if push_file "$src" "$dest"; then
          echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
        else
          success=1
        fi
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[WARN] Unknown push item kind: $kind" >&2
        success=1
        ;;
    esac
  done <<< "$manifest_output"

  if config_sync_include_system_items; then
    include_system=1
    manifest_output="$(config_push_system_items "$egress_root")"
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r kind src dest; do
      [[ -n "${kind:-}" ]] || continue
      case "$kind" in
        tree)
          if push_tree_contents "$src" "$dest"; then
            echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
          else
            success=1
          fi
          ;;
        file)
          if push_file "$src" "$dest"; then
            echo "[INFO] Updated $dest from $src"
          else
            success=1
          fi
          ;;
        *)
          echo "[WARN] Unknown push item kind: $kind" >&2
          success=1
          ;;
      esac
    done <<< "$manifest_output"
  fi

  if (( include_system == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Skipping system sync items for non-admin target: $TARGET_USER"
  fi

  if (( success == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Egress configuration files updated successfully."
  else
    echo "[WARN] Some egress configuration files were not updated."
    return 1
  fi
}

# =============================================================================
# Mount/fstab helpers
# =============================================================================
run_once() {
  local skipped="$STATE_DIR/$1.skipped"
  if [[ -f "$skipped" ]]; then
    echo "[SKIP] $1 skipped for now"
    return 0
  fi
  local name="${1:-}"
  shift || true

  [[ -n "$name" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] run_once requires a step name" >&2; return 2; }
  [[ "$name" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$ ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Invalid run_once step name: $name" >&2; return 2; }
  (($#)) || { echo "[ERROR] run_once requires a command for step: $name" >&2; return 2; }

  config_runtime_init || return 1

  local marker="$STATE_DIR/$name.done"
  local failed="$STATE_DIR/$name.failed"
  local running="$STATE_DIR/$name.running"
  local lockdir="$STATE_DIR/$name.lock"
  local tmp=""
  local step_log="$STATE_DIR/$name.last.log"
  local reason=""
  local start="" end="" rc=0

  if [[ -f "$marker" ]]; then
    echo "[SKIP] $name already done"
    return 0
  fi

  if config_bootstrap_step_plan_skipped "$name"; then
    echo "[SKIP] $name skipped by bootstrap.plan"
    echo "[INFO] To allow it: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} unskip ${name}"
    return 0
  fi

  if ! mkdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "[ERROR] Step already running or stale lock exists: $name ($lockdir)" >&2
    echo "[INFO] If no config/bootstrap process is active, remove the stale lock manually." >&2
    return 1
  fi
  config_chown_target_dir "$lockdir" || {
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true
    return 1
  }

  start="$(date -Is 2>/dev/null || date)"
  tmp="$(mktemp "$STATE_DIR/.${name}.done.XXXXXX")" || {
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true
    return 1
  }

  {
    printf 'name=%s\n' "$name"
    printf 'status=running\n'
    printf 'target_user=%s\n' "$TARGET_USER"
    printf 'target_home=%s\n' "$TARGET_HOME"
    printf 'started_at=%s\n' "$start"
    printf 'pid=%s\n' "$$"
  } > "$running"
  config_chown_target_file "$running" || {
    rm -f -- "$running" "$tmp"
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true
    return 1
  }

  rm -f -- "$failed"

  : > "$step_log"
  config_chown_target_file "$step_log" || {
    rm -f -- "$running" "$tmp" "$step_log"
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true
    return 1
  }

  if "$@" > >(tee "$step_log") 2> >(tee -a "$step_log" >&2); then
    rc=0
    end="$(date -Is 2>/dev/null || date)"
    {
      printf 'name=%s\n' "$name"
      printf 'status=done\n'
      printf 'target_user=%s\n' "$TARGET_USER"
      printf 'target_home=%s\n' "$TARGET_HOME"
      printf 'started_at=%s\n' "$start"
      printf 'finished_at=%s\n' "$end"
      printf 'exit_code=0\n'
    } > "$tmp" && mv -f -- "$tmp" "$marker" || rc=$?
    if ((rc == 0)); then
      config_chown_target_file "$marker" || rc=$?
    fi

    rm -f -- "$running" "$tmp" "$step_log"
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true

    if ((rc != 0)); then
      echo "[ERROR] Step succeeded but marker write failed: $name" >&2
      return "$rc"
    fi

    echo "[DONE] $name"
    return 0
  else
    rc=$?
    end="$(date -Is 2>/dev/null || date)"
    reason="$(grep -v '^[[:space:]]*$' "$step_log" 2>/dev/null | tail -n 1 | tr '\t' ' ' | tr -s ' ')"
    {
      printf 'name=%s\n' "$name"
      printf 'status=failed\n'
      printf 'target_user=%s\n' "$TARGET_USER"
      printf 'target_home=%s\n' "$TARGET_HOME"
      printf 'started_at=%s\n' "$start"
      printf 'finished_at=%s\n' "$end"
      printf 'exit_code=%s\n' "$rc"
      [[ -n "$reason" ]] && printf 'reason=%s\n' "$reason"
    } > "$failed" 2>/dev/null || true
    config_chown_target_file "$failed" 2>/dev/null || true

    rm -f -- "$running" "$tmp"
    rmdir -- "$lockdir" 2>/dev/null || true

    echo "[FAIL] Step failed: $name (exit $rc)" >&2
    return "$rc"
  fi
}

config_state_dir_refresh() {
  config_refresh_session_context
}

config_target_group() {
  id -gn "$TARGET_USER" 2>/dev/null
}

config_chown_target_path() {
  local path="${1:-}"
  local group=""

  [[ -n "$path" ]] || return 2
  [[ -e "$path" ]] || return 0

  group="$(config_target_group)" || return 1
  chown -R "${TARGET_USER}:${group}" "$path" 2>/dev/null || sudo chown -R "${TARGET_USER}:${group}" "$path"
}

config_ensure_target_dir() {
  local dir="${1:-}"
  local group=""

  [[ -n "$dir" ]] || return 2

  group="$(config_target_group)" || return 1

  install -d -m 700 -o "$TARGET_USER" -g "$group" "$dir" 2>/dev/null || {
    mkdir -p -- "$dir" || return 1
    chmod 700 "$dir" 2>/dev/null || true
    chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$dir" 2>/dev/null || sudo chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$dir"
  }
}

config_chown_target_file() {
  local file="${1:-}"
  local group=""

  [[ -n "$file" ]] || return 2
  [[ -e "$file" ]] || return 0

  group="$(config_target_group)" || return 1
  chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$file" 2>/dev/null || sudo chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$file"
  chmod 600 "$file" 2>/dev/null || true
}

config_chown_target_dir() {
  local dir="${1:-}"
  local group=""

  [[ -n "$dir" ]] || return 2
  [[ -d "$dir" ]] || return 0

  group="$(config_target_group)" || return 1
  chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$dir" 2>/dev/null || sudo chown "$TARGET_USER:$group" "$dir"
  chmod 700 "$dir" 2>/dev/null || true
}

config_runtime_init() {
  config_state_dir_refresh
  umask 077
  config_ensure_target_dir "$STATE_DIR" || return 1
}


CONFIG_COMMAND=""
CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS=()
CONFIG_TARGET_USER=""

config_apply_target_user() {
  config_set_target_user "$@"
}

config_parse_global_args() {
  CONFIG_COMMAND=""
  CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS=()
  CONFIG_TARGET_USER=""

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --target|-t|--user)
        local option_name="$1"
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || {
          echo "[ERROR] Missing value after ${option_name}" >&2
          return 2
        }
        CONFIG_TARGET_USER="$1"
        ;;
      --)
        shift
        break
        ;;
      -*)
        break
        ;;
      *)
        break
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  if [[ -n "$CONFIG_TARGET_USER" ]]; then
    config_set_target_user "$CONFIG_TARGET_USER" || return $?
  fi

  CONFIG_COMMAND="${1:-help}"
  shift || true
  CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS=("$@")
}

config_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: config.sh [global options] [command]

Global options:
  --target USER, -t USER, --user USER
                       Configure USER instead of the default TARGET_USER

Commands:
  help [topic|all]   Show usage or topic help
  -h, --help         Show this usage text
  howto              Show common task-oriented workflows
  menu               Show a compact command menu
  profiles           Show available account, SMB, and mount profiles
  config-init        Create commented config and manifest templates
  config-show        Show effective config without secrets
  status             Show local config state and done markers
  preflight          Run bootstrap preflight checks only
  rm STEP_NAME       Remove saved state/cleanup, then mark the plan row skipped
  skip STEP_NAME     Block a step and mark its plan row skipped
  unskip STEP_NAME   Allow a step by removing its skip marker and setting plan pending
  mounts, mount      Run mount workflow; prints target/mount identity first
  bootstrap, install Run or inspect install/bootstrap workflow;
                    see `config bootstrap help` for plan-file / plan-apply details
  pull               Clear copy_config_files.done and rerun CopyConfigFiles
  push               Copy local config files to /mnt/egress/$TARGET_USER
  credentials        Run the SMB credential helper as TARGET_USER
  run, user          Run a command or shell file as TARGET_USER; see `config run help`
  --create-operator  Create/reconcile the operator account from a profile
  --create-target    Create/reconcile a target account from a profile
  --remove-target    Remove a target account conservatively; keeps home/config by default
  --remove-operator  Remove an operator account with extra guardrails
  create-operator    Same as `--create-operator`
  create-target      Same as `--create-target`
  remove-target      Same as `--remove-target`
  remove-operator    Same as `--remove-operator`
  all, legacy        Run mounts workflow, then bootstrap workflow

Help topics:
  config help howto     Common workflows
  config help menu      Compact command menu
  config help account   Account lifecycle commands
  config help bootstrap Bootstrap/install plan workflow
  config help config    Config files and policy defaults
  config help profiles  Account/SMB/mount profile model
  config help credentials
  config help create-operator
  config help create-target
  config help remove-target
  config help remove-operator
  config help mount     Mount workflow
  config help pull      Pull sync
  config help push      Push sync
  config help run       Run command as target user

Examples:
  config help all
  config help howto
  config help menu
  config help account
  config help bootstrap
  config help config
  config config-init
  config --target labuser config-show
  sudo config --target labuser status
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap status
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only
  sudo config --target labuser install plan
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap
  sudo config -t labuser skip install_dev_env_dotnet_tools
  sudo config --create-operator --profile DefaultOperator --name vmuser --dry-run
  sudo config --create-target --profile AIEngineer --name aiengineer --dry-run --expire-password
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-operator --name oldoperator --dry-run
EOF
}

config_help_howto() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Common workflows:

  Initialize commented config and manifest files:
    config config-init

  Inspect effective config without secrets:
    config --target labuser config-show

  See who config will affect:
    config status
    sudo config --target labuser status

  Open the bootstrap checklist:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file
    sudoedit "$(sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file)"

  Apply the checklist safely:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only

  Check what bootstrap will do:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan

  Show known bootstrap steps from the manifest:
    config --target labuser bootstrap steps

  Run allowed bootstrap steps:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap

  Run one bootstrap step:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap step STEP_NAME

  Skip a step for now:
    sudo config --target labuser skip STEP_NAME

  Allow a skipped step to run again:
    sudo config --target labuser unskip STEP_NAME

  Remove a step's saved state and block it from running:
    sudo config --target labuser rm STEP_NAME

  Allow it again later:
    sudo config --target labuser unskip STEP_NAME

  Make the checklist match saved history:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --markers-win

  Make saved state follow the checklist:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins

  Re-run completed steps from the checklist:
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins --reset-done
    sudo config --target labuser bootstrap

  Mount required shares:
    sudo config --target labuser mount
    sudo config --target labuser mount --all

  Pull config files for a target user:
    sudo config --target labuser pull

  Push config files for a target user:
    sudo config --target labuser push

  Run a command as the target user:
    config --target labuser run --shell-command 'echo $HOME'

Alias-to-managed-step migration:
  old aliases are now managed bootstrap steps.
  Python stacks install into the resolved target PYTHON_ENV.
  Use `config bootstrap steps` and `config bootstrap status` before running work.
  Run one step at a time.
  `lv` remains an inspection/dashboard helper, not the package install control plane.

Managed step examples:
  config --target aiengineer bootstrap step install_ml_base_python_stack
  config --target researchscientist bootstrap step install_grn_research_python_stack
  config --target publisher bootstrap step install_publisher_base_tools

Safe order for bootstrap:
  1. edit bootstrap.plan
  2. run plan-apply
  3. check bootstrap plan
  4. run bootstrap

Important:
  plan-apply never installs anything.
  bootstrap/install runs the allowed steps.
  --reset-done can make completed steps run again.
  rm STEP clears saved state and blocks the step until you unskip it.
  Edit config files to change policy.
  Edit shell scripts only to change execution logic.
EOF
}

config_help_config() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Config files:

  Global defaults:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/config.env

  Per-target defaults:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/targets/USER.env

  Account creation profiles:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/profiles.tsv
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/smb-profiles.tsv
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/mount-profiles.tsv

  Initialize examples:
    config config-init

  Inspect effective config:
  config --target labuser config-show

  Later layers override earlier ones:
    built-in defaults < global config < target config < environment < CLI

  Account profiles are creation templates.
  Target env files are generated or reconciled per target from those templates.

Generated files are commented and intended to be edited.
Edit config files to change policy.
Edit shell scripts only to change execution logic.

Architecture:
  config.sh is the CLI/state engine.
  lib/config-sh/installers.sh contains trusted installer functions.
  etc/config-sh contains editable policy and manifests.
  accounts/*.tsv are creation templates for target env reconciliation.

To add a new install step:
  1. add the trusted shell function in lib/config-sh/installers.sh
  2. add it to the allowlist in config.sh
  3. add a row to etc/config-sh/bootstrap/steps.tsv
  4. add default states to bootstrap profiles if needed
  5. test with config bootstrap steps and bootstrap step STEP

Safe format:
  KEY=value
  KEY="value"
  KEY='value'
  comments and blank lines are allowed
EOF
}

config_help_menu() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Command menu:

  help TOPIC        Show help. Topics: howto, menu, config, account, profiles, bootstrap, mount, pull, push, run, remove-target, remove-operator
  profiles          Show available account, SMB, and mount profiles
  config-init       Create commented config and manifest templates
  config-show       Show effective config without secrets
  status            Show current target, paths, and saved state
  credentials       Run the SMB credential helper as TARGET_USER
  --create-operator Create/reconcile operator account from a profile
  --create-target   Create/reconcile target account from a profile
  --remove-target   Remove a target account conservatively
  --remove-operator Remove an operator account with extra guardrails
  bootstrap         Run or inspect bootstrap/install workflow
  install           Alias for bootstrap
  bootstrap step STEP
                    Run one selected bootstrap step
                    Managed role installs replace old alias-driven workflows
  mount             Run mount workflow
  pull              Pull config files into target home
  push              Push target config files to /mnt/egress
  skip STEP         Block a bootstrap step from running
  unskip STEP       Allow a skipped bootstrap step to run again
  rm STEP           Remove saved state/cleanup, then mark the plan row skipped
  run               Run a command or shell file as TARGET_USER
  --create-operator Create/reconcile the operator account from a profile
  --create-target   Create/reconcile a target account from a profile
  --remove-target   Remove a target account conservatively
  --remove-operator Remove an operator account with extra guardrails

Managed role guidance:
  Python stacks install into resolved PYTHON_ENV for the selected role.
  Use bootstrap steps/status first, then run one managed bootstrap step at a time.
  lv remains an inspection/dashboard helper, not the package install control plane.

Lifecycle examples:

  sudo config --create-target --profile AIEngineer --name aiengineer --dry-run --expire-password
  sudo config --create-operator --profile DefaultOperator --name vmuser --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-operator --name oldoperator --dry-run

Target selection:

  --target USER     Configure USER instead of the current/default user
  -t USER           Short form of --target
  --user USER       Alias of --target

Bootstrap plan commands:

  bootstrap plan-file
      Print the checklist path.

  bootstrap plan-init
      Create the checklist if missing.

  bootstrap plan
      Show the current step table.

  bootstrap status
      Same current step table as plan.

  bootstrap steps
      Show known bootstrap steps from manifest metadata.

  bootstrap plan-apply
      Compare checklist with saved history and reconcile.

  bootstrap step STEP_NAME
      Run one bootstrap step through the normal target-scoped run_once path.
      Honors .done, .skipped, and bootstrap.plan skipped state.

  bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only
      Safest apply mode. Only sync skipped/not-skipped state.

  bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins
      Make saved skip state follow bootstrap.plan.

  bootstrap plan-apply --markers-win
      Rewrite bootstrap.plan from saved marker history.

  bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins --reset-done
      Allow completed steps to be reset and run again.

Examples:

  sudo config --target labuser status
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap
  sudo config --target labuser skip install_docker
  sudo config --target labuser unskip install_docker
  config --target labuser run --shell-command 'echo $HOME'
  config --target aiengineer bootstrap step install_ml_base_python_stack
  config --target researchscientist bootstrap step install_grn_research_python_stack
  config --target publisher bootstrap step install_publisher_base_tools
EOF
}

config_pull_push_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config pull [--sync-resolv-conf]
  config push [--sync-resolv-conf]

Options:
  --sync-resolv-conf  Include `/etc/resolv.conf` in sync scope
  --with-resolv-conf  Alias for `--sync-resolv-conf`

Notes:
  `/etc/resolv.conf` is excluded from default pull/push sync.
  System sync still requires an admin target such as `vmuser`.
EOF
}

config_help() {
  local topic="${1:-main}"

  case "$topic" in
    main)
      config_usage
      ;;
    all)
      config_help_all
      ;;
    howto)
      config_help_howto
      ;;
    config|settings)
      config_help_config
      ;;
    menu|commands)
      config_help_menu
      ;;
    pull|push|sync)
      config_pull_push_usage
      ;;
    credentials|credential|secrets)
      config_credentials_usage
      ;;
    account|accounts)
      config_account_usage
      ;;
    profiles)
      config_help_profiles
      ;;
    create-operator|operator)
      config_create_operator_usage
      ;;
    create-target|target)
      config_create_target_usage
      ;;
    remove-target)
      config_remove_target_usage
      ;;
    remove-operator)
      config_remove_operator_usage
      ;;
    bootstrap|install)
      config_bootstrap_usage
      ;;
    run|user)
      config_user_usage
      ;;
    mount|mounts)
      mounts_usage
      ;;
    help|-h|--help)
      config_usage
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown help topic: $topic" >&2
      echo "[INFO] Available help topics: all, howto, menu, config, account, profiles, create-operator, create-target, remove-target, remove-operator, bootstrap, install, pull, push, run, mount" >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac
}

config_user_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: config.sh run [options]

Target selection:
  Prefer `config --target USER run ...` for normal target-scoped usage.
  Use `run --as USER` only as a one-off override for this run command.

Options:
  --as USER            Override TARGET_USER for this run command only
  --shell-command CMD  Run CMD with bash -c as TARGET_USER
  --shell-file PATH    Run PATH with bash as TARGET_USER
  --init               Load shell init files for --shell-command or --shell-file
  help, -h, --help     Show this help

Examples:
  config run --shell-command 'echo $HOME'
  config --target labuser run --shell-command 'echo $HOME'
  config --target labuser run --init --shell-command 'echo $HOME'
  config run --as labuser --shell-command 'echo $HOME'
  config skip install_dev_env_dotnet_tools
  config unskip install_dev_env_dotnet_tools
  config --target labuser run --shell-command 'python3 -m pip --version'
  config --target labuser run --shell-file "$HOME/.local/scripts/example.sh"
  config run --as labuser --init --shell-file "$HOME/.local/scripts/example.sh"
EOF
}

config_status() {
  local file
  config_state_dir_refresh

  printf "TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "CURRENT_HOME=%s\n" "$CURRENT_HOME"
  printf "BASEDIR=%s\n" "$BASEDIR"
  printf "STATE_DIR=%s\n" "$STATE_DIR"
  printf "DISTROHOME=%s\n" "$DISTROHOME"
  printf "CONFIG_DIR=%s\n" "$(config_global_config_dir)"
  printf "WSL_USER=%s\n" "$WSL_USER"
  printf "SMB_USER=%s\n" "$SMB_USER"
  printf "BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE=%s\n" "${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE:-default}"
  if [[ -n "${TARGET_ROLE_LABEL:-}" ]]; then
    printf "TARGET_ROLE_LABEL=%s\n" "$TARGET_ROLE_LABEL"
  fi
  printf "SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS=%s\n" "${SYNC_SYSTEM_ITEMS:-"(auto)"}"
  if [[ -n "${SMB_PROFILE:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_PROFILE=%s\n" "$SMB_PROFILE"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${MOUNT_PROFILE:-}" ]]; then
    printf "MOUNT_PROFILE=%s\n" "$MOUNT_PROFILE"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_USER_DEFAULT=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_DEFAULT"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT:-}" ]]; then
    printf "SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT"
  fi
  echo "CONFIG_FILES_LOADED:"
  if [[ -n "$CONFIG_LOADED_FILES" ]]; then
    while IFS= read -r file; do
      [[ -n "$file" ]] || continue
      printf "  %s\n" "$file"
    done <<< "$CONFIG_LOADED_FILES"
  else
    echo "  (none)"
  fi

  if [[ -f /etc/os-release ]]; then
    printf "OS_RELEASE=%s\n" "$(awk -F= '/^PRETTY_NAME=/{gsub(/"/, "", $2); print $2}' /etc/os-release)"
  fi

  config_status_list_markers "Done" "done"
  config_status_list_markers "Failed" "failed"
  config_status_list_markers "Running" "running"
}
