  printf "  DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER=%s\n" "${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER:-Anaconda3-2025.12-2-Linux-x86_64.sh}"
}

config_status_list_markers() {
  local label="$1" suffix="$2" marker found=0
  local line started exit_code reason step_base step_log

  echo "$label markers:"
  shopt -s nullglob
  for marker in "$STATE_DIR"/*."$suffix"; do
    if [[ "$(basename "$marker")" == "install_dev_env.done" ]] && compgen -G "$STATE_DIR/install_dev_env_*.*" > /dev/null; then
      continue
    fi
    started=""
    exit_code=""
    reason=""
    if [[ -s "$marker" ]]; then
      while IFS= read -r line; do
        case "$line" in
          started_at=*) started="${line#started_at=}" ;;
          exit_code=*) exit_code="${line#exit_code=}" ;;
          reason=*) reason="${line#reason=}" ;;
        esac
      done < "$marker"
    fi
    case "$suffix" in
      done)
        printf "  %s\n" "$(basename "$marker" .done)"
        ;;
      failed)
        step_base="$(basename "$marker" .failed)"
        step_log="$STATE_DIR/$step_base.last.log"
        if [[ -z "$reason" && -f "$step_log" ]]; then
          reason="$(grep -v '^[[:space:]]*$' "$step_log" 2>/dev/null | tail -n 1 | tr '\t' ' ' | tr -s ' ')"
        fi
        printf "  %s" "$step_base"
        [[ -n "$exit_code" ]] && printf " exit=%s" "$exit_code"
        [[ -n "$reason" ]] && printf " reason=%s" "$reason"
        printf "\n"
        ;;
      running)
        printf "  %s" "$(basename "$marker" .running)"
        [[ -n "$started" ]] && printf " started_at=%s" "$started"
        printf "\n"
        ;;
      *)
        printf "  %s\n" "$(basename "$marker")"
        ;;
    esac
    found=1
  done
  shopt -u nullglob

  ((found)) || echo "  (none)"
}

config_skip_step() {
  local name="$1"
  local path="$STATE_DIR/$name.skipped"
  local plan_updated=0

  config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$name" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $name" >&2
    echo "[INFO] See: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan" >&2
    return 2
  }

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  cat >"$path" <<EOF
name=$name
status=skipped
target_user=$TARGET_USER
target_home=$TARGET_HOME
skipped_at=$(date -Is)
EOF
  config_chown_target_file "$path" || return 1
  rm -f "$STATE_DIR/$name.running" "$STATE_DIR/$name.failed"
  echo "[INFO] Marked step as skipped for now: $name"

  if config_bootstrap_plan_set_step_state "$name" skipped; then
    echo "[INFO] Updated bootstrap.plan: set $name to skipped"
    plan_updated=1
  elif [[ $? -eq 3 ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] No bootstrap.plan found for target: $TARGET_USER"
    echo "[INFO] Create one if needed with: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan-init"
  else
    return 1
  fi
}

config_unskip_step() {
  local name="$1"
  local path="$STATE_DIR/$name.skipped"
  local marker_removed=0
  local plan_status=0
  local plan_state=""

  config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$name" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $name" >&2
    echo "[INFO] See: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan" >&2
    return 2
  }

  config_runtime_init || return 1

  if [[ -f "$path" ]]; then
    rm -f "$path"
    echo "[INFO] Removed skipped marker: $name"
    marker_removed=1
  else
    echo "[INFO] Step had no skipped marker: $name"
  fi

  plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  if config_bootstrap_plan_set_step_state "$name" pending; then
    if [[ "$plan_state" == "skipped" ]]; then
      echo "[INFO] Updated bootstrap.plan: skipped -> pending for $name"
    else
      if (( marker_removed == 0 )); then
        echo "[INFO] Step is already allowed by marker and plan: $name"
      else
        echo "[INFO] bootstrap.plan already allowed step: $name"
      fi
    fi
  else
    plan_status=$?
    if [[ $plan_status -eq 3 ]]; then
      echo "[INFO] No bootstrap.plan found for target: $TARGET_USER"
      echo "[INFO] Create one if needed with: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan-init"
    else
      return 1
    fi
  fi
}

config_rm_marker() {
  local name="$1"
  local removed=0
  local path

  for path in \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.done" \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.failed" \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.running" \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.skipped" \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.last.log" \
    "$STATE_DIR/$name.lock"; do
    if [[ -e "$path" ]]; then
      rm -rf "$path"
      removed=1
      echo "[INFO] Removed marker: $(basename "$path")"
    fi
  done

  if [[ $removed -eq 1 ]]; then
    return 0
  else
    echo "[INFO] No marker state found for: $name"
    return 0
  fi
}

config_rm_step() {
  local name="$1"
  config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$name" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $name" >&2
    echo "[INFO] See: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan" >&2
    return 2
  }
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  echo "Remove step state:"
  printf "  TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "  TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  STATE_DIR=%s\n" "$STATE_DIR"
  printf "  STEP_NAME=%s\n" "$name"
  config_uninstall_step "$name" || return 1
  echo "[INFO] Cleanup/uninstall completed or not required: $name"
  config_rm_marker "$name"
  if config_bootstrap_plan_set_step_state "$name" skipped; then
    echo "[INFO] Updated bootstrap.plan: $name -> skipped"
  elif [[ $? -eq 3 ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] No bootstrap.plan found for target: $TARGET_USER"
    echo "[INFO] Create one if needed with: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan-init"
  else
    return 1
  fi
  echo "[INFO] Step is now blocked from running until you unskip it."
}

config_preflight_host_resolves() {
  local host="$1"
  getent hosts "$host" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

config_bootstrap_step_needs_network() {
  local step="$1"
  local step_name function_name scope network description

  if IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description < <(config_bootstrap_step_metadata "$step" 2>/dev/null); then
    [[ "$network" == "1" ]]
    return
  fi

  case "$step" in
    update_apt|standard_apps|install_networking|install_dev_env_system_packages|install_dev_env_dotnet_tools|install_dev_env_python_user_tools|install_dev_env_azure_cli|install_gui_support|install_docker|install_terraform|install_kubernets|install_minikube|install_sqlserver_support_2004|install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

config_bootstrap_has_pending_network_step() {
  local step
  for step in \
    update_apt \
    standard_apps \
    install_networking \
    install_dev_env_system_packages \
    install_dev_env_shell_init \
    install_dev_env_dotnet_tools \
    install_dev_env_python_user_tools \
    install_dev_env_pyenv \
    install_dev_env_anaconda \
    install_dev_env_azure_cli \
    install_dev_env_verify \
    install_gui_support \
    install_docker \
    install_terraform \
    install_kubernets \
    install_minikube \
    install_sqlserver_support_2004 \
    install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204; do
    if ! config_bootstrap_step_needs_network "$step"; then
      continue
    fi
    case "$(config_bootstrap_step_status "$step")" in
      done|skipped)
        ;;
      *)
        return 0
        ;;
    esac
  done
  return 1
}

config_run_preflight_checks() {
  local failed=0
  local host

  echo "Preflight checks:"

  if [[ ! -e /etc/resolv.conf ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] /etc/resolv.conf is missing"
    failed=1
  elif ! grep -Eq '^[[:space:]]*nameserver[[:space:]]+' /etc/resolv.conf 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "[FAIL] /etc/resolv.conf has no nameserver entries"
    failed=1
  else
    echo "[OK] /etc/resolv.conf has nameserver entries"
  fi

  if config_bootstrap_has_pending_network_step; then
    for host in \
      archive.ubuntu.com \
      security.ubuntu.com \
      packages.microsoft.com \
      download.docker.com \
      apt.releases.hashicorp.com \
      api.nuget.org; do
      if config_preflight_host_resolves "$host"; then
        echo "[OK] DNS resolves: $host"
      else
        echo "[FAIL] DNS does not resolve: $host"
        failed=1
      fi
    done
  else
    echo "[OK] No pending network-dependent bootstrap steps"
  fi

  if [[ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.list && -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.sources ]]; then
    echo "[WARN] Duplicate Azure CLI apt sources detected"
  fi

  if [[ $failed -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] Preflight checks failed"
    return 1
  fi

  echo "[OK] Preflight checks passed"
  return 0
}

config_bootstrap_function_allowed() {
  case "$1" in
    UpdateAPT|StandardApps|InstallNetworking|InstallDevEnvSystemPackages|InstallDevEnvShellInit|InstallDevEnvDotnetTools|InstallDevEnvPythonUserTools|InstallDevEnvPyenv|InstallDevEnvAnaconda|InstallDevEnvAzureCLI|InstallDevEnvVerify|InstallGUISupport|InstallDocker|InstallTerraform|InstallKubernetes|InstallMinikube|InstallSQLServerSupport2004|InstallSQLServerCLITool2204|InstallMLBasePythonStack|InstallPytorchStack|InstallLLMStack|InstallAPIStack|InstallMLDevTools|CheckGPUAvailability|CheckPytorchCuda|InstallResearchNumericPythonStack|InstallResearchOptimizationStack|InstallResearchNotebookStack|InstallGRNResearchPythonStack|InstallParameterSearchTooling|InstallPublisherBaseTools|InstallPublisherNotebookStack|InstallPandocObsidianTools|CheckOpenClawBaseRequirements|InstallOpenClawFullStack|CheckOpenClawAgentWorkspace|CheckDockerAccess|CheckTerraformInstallation|CheckKubernetesAccess)
      return 0
      ;;
    *)
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

config_bootstrap_builtin_step_rows() {
  cat <<'EOF'
update_apt	UpdateAPT	system	1	Refresh apt metadata and upgrade packages
standard_apps	StandardApps	system	1	Install common system tools
install_networking	InstallNetworking	system	1	Install network and CIFS tools
install_dev_env_system_packages	InstallDevEnvSystemPackages	system	1	Install system packages for developer tools
install_dev_env_shell_init	InstallDevEnvShellInit	target	0	Update target shell init files

}

config_bootstrap_step_manifest_rows() {
  local file line lineno=0 step_name function_name scope network description

  file="$(config_bootstrap_steps_file)"
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 1

  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    lineno=$((lineno + 1))
    [[ -n "${line//[[:space:]]/}" ]] || continue
    [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description extra <<< "$line"

    [[ -n "${step_name:-}" && -n "${function_name:-}" && -n "${scope:-}" && -n "${network:-}" && -n "${description:-}" && -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
      echo "[WARN] Invalid bootstrap steps manifest row at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
    }
    [[ "$step_name" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$ ]] || {
      echo "[WARN] Invalid bootstrap step name at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
    }
    [[ "$function_name" =~ ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$ ]] || {
      echo "[WARN] Invalid bootstrap function name at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
    }
    case "$scope" in system|target|mixed) ;; *)
      echo "[WARN] Invalid bootstrap scope at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
    esac
    case "$network" in 0|1) ;; *)
      echo "[WARN] Invalid bootstrap network flag at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
    esac
    config_bootstrap_function_allowed "$function_name" || {
      echo "[WARN] Disallowed bootstrap function at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
      return 1
    }
    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "$step_name" "$function_name" "$scope" "$network" "$description"
  done < "$file"
}

config_bootstrap_steps_source() {
  if config_bootstrap_step_manifest_rows >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf '%s\n' "manifest"
  else
    printf '%s\n' "fallback"
  fi
}

config_bootstrap_step_rows() {
  config_bootstrap_step_manifest_rows || config_bootstrap_builtin_step_rows
}

config_bootstrap_steps() {
  local step_name function_name scope network description
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description; do
    [[ -n "$step_name" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$step_name"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_step_rows)
}

config_bootstrap_function_for_step() {
  local wanted="${1:-}"
  local step_name function_name scope network description

  [[ -n "$wanted" ]] || return 2
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description; do
    [[ "$step_name" == "$wanted" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$function_name"
    return 0
  done < <(config_bootstrap_step_rows)
  return 1
}

config_bootstrap_step_metadata() {
  local wanted="${1:-}"
  local step_name function_name scope network description

  [[ -n "$wanted" ]] || return 2
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description; do
    [[ "$step_name" == "$wanted" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "$step_name" "$function_name" "$scope" "$network" "$description"
    return 0
  done < <(config_bootstrap_step_rows)
  return 1
}

config_bootstrap_plan_file() {
  printf '%s\n' "$STATE_DIR/bootstrap.plan"
}

config_bootstrap_step_is_known() {
  local step="${1:-}"
  [[ -n "$step" ]] || return 1
  config_bootstrap_steps | grep -qxF -- "$step"
}

config_bootstrap_plan_rows() {
  local file
  local line state step extra lineno=0

  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 0

  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    lineno=$((lineno + 1))

    line="${line%%#*}"
    line="$(printf '%s' "$line" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//; s/[[:space:]]*$//')"
    [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue

    read -r state step extra <<< "$line"

    case "$state" in
      next|pending|skipped) ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Invalid bootstrap plan state at $file:$lineno: $state" >&2
        return 1
        ;;
    esac

    [[ -n "${step:-}" && -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] Invalid bootstrap plan row at $file:$lineno: $line" >&2
      return 1
    }

    [[ "$step" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$ ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] Invalid bootstrap step name at $file:$lineno: $step" >&2
      return 1
    }

    config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$step" || {
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step in plan at $file:$lineno: $step" >&2
      return 1
    }

    printf '%s\t%s\n' "$state" "$step"
  done < "$file"
}

config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step() {
  local wanted="${1:-}"
  local state step found=""

  [[ -n "$wanted" ]] || return 2

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r state step; do
    [[ "$step" == "$wanted" ]] || continue

    if [[ "$state" == "next" ]]; then
      found="next"
    elif [[ -z "$found" ]]; then
      found="$state"
    fi
  done < <(config_bootstrap_plan_rows) || return 1

  [[ -n "$found" ]] && printf '%s\n' "$found"
}

config_bootstrap_plan_set_step_state() {
  local step="${1:-}"
  local new_state="${2:-}"
  local file tmp line state row_step extra changed=0 found=0

  [[ -n "$step" ]] || return 2
  case "$new_state" in
    next|pending|skipped) ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Invalid bootstrap plan state: $new_state" >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac

  config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$step" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $step" >&2
    echo "[INFO] See: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan" >&2
    return 2
  }

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 3
  tmp="$(mktemp "$STATE_DIR/.bootstrap.plan.XXXXXX")" || return 1

  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    state=""
    row_step=""
    extra=""

    if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*# || -z "${line//[[:space:]]/}" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$tmp"
      continue
    fi

    read -r state row_step extra <<< "$line"
    if [[ -n "$extra" || -z "$row_step" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$tmp"
      continue
    fi

    case "$state" in
      next|pending|skipped)
        if [[ "$row_step" == "$step" ]]; then
          found=1
          if [[ "$state" != "$new_state" ]]; then
            changed=1
          fi
          printf '%s %s\n' "$new_state" "$step" >> "$tmp"
        else
          printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$tmp"
        fi
        ;;
      *)
        printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$tmp"
        ;;
    esac
  done < "$file"

  if (( ! found )); then
    printf '%s %s\n' "$new_state" "$step" >> "$tmp"
    changed=1
  fi

  mv -f "$tmp" "$file"
  config_chown_target_file "$file" || return 1
  return 0
}

config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step() {
  local step="${1:-}"

  [[ -n "$step" ]] || return 2

  if [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.done" ]]; then
    printf 'done'
  elif [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.failed" ]]; then
    printf 'failed'
  elif [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.running" ]]; then
    printf 'running'
  elif [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.skipped" ]]; then
    printf 'skipped'
  else
    printf 'none'
  fi
}

config_bootstrap_step_skip_reason() {
  local step="${1:-}"

  if [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.skipped" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "marker"
    return 0
  fi

  if [[ "$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)" == "skipped" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "plan"
    return 0
  fi

  printf '%s\n' "none"
}

config_bootstrap_step_status_source() {
  local name="${1:-}"
  local marker_state=""
  local plan_state=""

  marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$name")"

  case "$marker_state" in
    done|failed|running|skipped)
      printf '%s\tmarker:%s\n' "$marker_state" "$marker_state"
      return 0
      ;;
  esac

  plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$name" 2>/dev/null || true)"

  case "$plan_state" in
    next|pending|skipped)
      printf '%s\tplan:%s\n' "$plan_state" "$plan_state"
      ;;
    *)
      printf 'pending\tdefault:pending\n'
      ;;
  esac
}

config_bootstrap_plan_warn_header_mismatch() {
  local file
  local header_user=""
  local header_home=""

  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 0

  header_user="$(sed -n 's/^[[:space:]]*#[[:space:]]*target_user=//p' "$file" | head -n1)"
  header_home="$(sed -n 's/^[[:space:]]*#[[:space:]]*target_home=//p' "$file" | head -n1)"

  if [[ -n "$header_user" && "$header_user" != "$TARGET_USER" ]]; then
    echo "[WARN] Plan header target_user=$header_user does not match active TARGET_USER=$TARGET_USER" >&2
  fi

  if [[ -n "$header_home" && "$header_home" != "$TARGET_HOME" ]]; then
    echo "[WARN] Plan header target_home=$header_home does not match active TARGET_HOME=$TARGET_HOME" >&2
  fi
}

config_bootstrap_plan_warn_marker_divergence() {
  local step plan_state marker_state

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$step")"
    [[ -n "$plan_state" ]] || plan_state="none"

    if config_bootstrap_plan_states_align "$plan_state" "$marker_state"; then
      continue
    fi

    case "$plan_state:$marker_state" in
      skipped:*)
        echo "[WARN] Plan says skipped $step, but marker state is $marker_state" >&2
        ;;
      pending:skipped|next:skipped)
        echo "[WARN] Plan says $plan_state $step, but marker state is skipped" >&2
        ;;
      pending:done|next:done)
        echo "[WARN] Plan says $plan_state $step, but marker state is done" >&2
        ;;
      none:skipped)
        echo "[WARN] Plan has no row for $step, marker state is skipped" >&2
        ;;
    esac
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal() {
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_rows >/dev/null || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_warn_header_mismatch
  config_bootstrap_plan_warn_marker_divergence
}

config_bootstrap_plan_validate() {
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal
}

config_bootstrap_plan_diff_rows() {
  local step plan_state marker_state

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [[ -n "$plan_state" ]] || plan_state="none"
    marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$step")"
    printf "%s\t%s\t%s\n" "$step" "$plan_state" "$marker_state"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_states_align() {
  local plan_state="${1:-none}"
  local marker_state="${2:-none}"

  case "$plan_state:$marker_state" in
    skipped:skipped|skipped:done|pending:none|next:none|none:none)
      return 0
      ;;
  esac

  return 1
}

config_bootstrap_plan_print_diff() {
  local step plan_state marker_state meaning

  echo "Bootstrap plan drift:"
  printf "  %-34s %-9s %-9s %s\n" "Step" "Plan" "Marker" "Meaning"

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step plan_state marker_state; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    meaning=""

    if config_bootstrap_plan_states_align "$plan_state" "$marker_state"; then
      continue
    fi

    case "$plan_state:$marker_state" in
      skipped:none)
        meaning="plan wants skipped, marker not present"
        ;;
      pending:skipped|next:skipped)
        meaning="plan wants runnable, marker blocks it"
        ;;
      pending:done)
        meaning="plan wants runnable, marker says already done"
        ;;
      next:done)
        meaning="plan wants next, marker says already done"
        ;;
      none:done|none:failed|none:running|none:skipped)
        meaning="plan has no row, marker still exists"
        ;;
    esac

    [[ -n "$meaning" ]] || continue
    printf "  %-34s %-9s %-9s %s\n" "$step" "$plan_state" "$marker_state" "$meaning"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_plan_diff_rows)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_has_drift() {
  local step plan_state marker_state

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step plan_state marker_state; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    config_bootstrap_plan_states_align "$plan_state" "$marker_state" || return 0
  done < <(config_bootstrap_plan_diff_rows)

  return 1
}

config_bootstrap_plan_prompt_reconciliation() {
  local selection=""

  if [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
    return 1
  fi

  echo "Plan and marker state diverge for target $TARGET_USER."
  echo
  echo "Choose reconciliation:"
  echo "  [p] plan wins          apply bootstrap.plan to marker state"
  echo "  [m] markers win        rewrite bootstrap.plan from current markers"
  echo "  [s] safe skips only    only sync .skipped markers; never remove .done"
  echo "  [a] abort              make no changes"
  read -r -p "Selection [p/m/s/a, default=a]: " selection
  case "${selection:-a}" in
    p|P) printf '%s\n' "plan-wins" ;;
    m|M) printf '%s\n' "markers-win" ;;
    s|S) printf '%s\n' "skips-only" ;;
    *) printf '%s\n' "abort" ;;
  esac
}

config_bootstrap_plan_diff() {
  local step plan_state marker_state

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal || return 1

  echo "Bootstrap plan diff:"
  printf "  %-34s %-9s %s\n" "Step" "Plan" "Marker"

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step plan_state marker_state; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    printf "  %-34s %-9s %s\n" "$step" "$plan_state" "$marker_state"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_plan_diff_rows)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_apply_skips_only() {
  local step plan_state path

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal || return 1

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    path="$STATE_DIR/$step.skipped"

    case "$plan_state" in
      skipped)
        cat > "$path" <<EOF
name=$step
status=skipped
target_user=$TARGET_USER
target_home=$TARGET_HOME
skipped_at=$(date -Is)
source=bootstrap.plan
EOF
        config_chown_target_file "$path" || return 1
        rm -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.running" "$STATE_DIR/$step.failed"
        echo "[INFO] Applied plan skip: $step"
        ;;
      pending|next)
        if [[ -f "$path" ]]; then
          rm -f "$path"
          echo "[INFO] Removed skipped marker from plan $plan_state step: $step"
        fi
        ;;
      ""|none)
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unexpected plan state for $step: $plan_state" >&2
        return 1
        ;;
      esac
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_apply_plan_wins() {
  local reset_done="${1:-0}"
  local step plan_state path marker_state

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal || return 1

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    plan_state="$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$step")"
    path="$STATE_DIR/$step.skipped"

    case "$plan_state" in
      skipped)
        cat > "$path" <<EOF
name=$step
status=skipped
target_user=$TARGET_USER
target_home=$TARGET_HOME
skipped_at=$(date -Is)
source=bootstrap.plan
EOF
        config_chown_target_file "$path" || return 1
        rm -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.running" "$STATE_DIR/$step.failed"
        if [[ "$marker_state" == "done" ]]; then
          if (( reset_done )); then
            rm -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.done"
            echo "[INFO] Removed done marker to honor plan skip: $step"
          else
            echo "[WARN] Plan says skipped $step, but preserving done marker without --reset-done" >&2
          fi
        fi
        echo "[INFO] Applied plan skip: $step"
        ;;
      pending|next)
        if [[ -f "$path" ]]; then
          rm -f "$path"
          echo "[INFO] Removed skipped marker from plan $plan_state step: $step"
        fi
        if [[ "$marker_state" == "done" ]]; then
          if (( reset_done )); then
            rm -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.done"
            echo "[INFO] Removed done marker to re-enable plan $plan_state step: $step"
          else
            echo "[WARN] Plan says $plan_state $step, but preserving done marker without --reset-done" >&2
          fi
        fi
        ;;
      ""|none)
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unexpected plan state for $step: $plan_state" >&2
        return 1
        ;;
    esac
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_write_observed_state_comments() {
  local outfile="${1:-}"
  local step marker_state
  local wrote_header=0

  [[ -n "$outfile" ]] || return 2

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$step")"

    case "$marker_state" in
      done|failed|running)
        if (( ! wrote_header )); then
          printf '#\n' >> "$outfile"
          printf '# Observed runtime state at last rewrite:\n' >> "$outfile"
          wrote_header=1
        fi
        printf '# %s %s\n' "$marker_state" "$step" >> "$outfile"
        ;;
    esac
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
}

config_bootstrap_plan_write_header() {
  local outfile="${1:-}"

  [[ -n "$outfile" ]] || return 2

  {
    printf '# config bootstrap/install target plan\n'
    printf '# target_user=%s\n' "$TARGET_USER"
    printf '# target_home=%s\n' "$TARGET_HOME"
    printf '#\n'
    printf '# Valid states:\n'
    printf '#   next STEP_NAME\n'
    printf '#   pending STEP_NAME\n'
    printf '#   skipped STEP_NAME\n'
    printf '#\n'
    printf '# done/failed/running are runtime marker states, not declared here.\n'
    printf '# Observed marker state may be recorded below as comments such as:\n'
    printf '#   # done STEP_NAME\n'
    printf '\n'
  } > "$outfile"
}

config_bootstrap_plan_sync_observed_state_comments() {
  local file tmp state step

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || return 0
  tmp="$(mktemp "$STATE_DIR/.bootstrap.plan.XXXXXX")" || return 1

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_header "$tmp" || return 1

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r state step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    printf '%s %s\n' "$state" "$step" >> "$tmp"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_plan_rows) || return 1

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_observed_state_comments "$tmp" || return 1

  mv -f "$tmp" "$file"
  config_chown_target_file "$file" || return 1
}

config_bootstrap_plan_rewrite_from_markers() {
  local file tmp step marker_state declared_state

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  tmp="$(mktemp "$STATE_DIR/.bootstrap.plan.XXXXXX")" || return 1

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_header "$tmp" || return 1

  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    marker_state="$(config_bootstrap_marker_state_for_step "$step")"
    case "$marker_state" in
      done|skipped)
        declared_state="skipped"
        ;;
      failed|running|none)
        declared_state="pending"
        ;;
      *)
        declared_state="pending"
        ;;
    esac
    printf '%s %s\n' "$declared_state" "$step" >> "$tmp"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_observed_state_comments "$tmp" || return 1

  mv -f "$tmp" "$file"
  config_chown_target_file "$file" || return 1
  echo "[INFO] Rewrote bootstrap plan from current markers: $file"
}

config_bootstrap_plan_apply_markers_win() {
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_rewrite_from_markers
}

config_bootstrap_plan_apply() {
  local mode=""
  local reset_done=0
  local has_drift=0
  local selection=""

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_bootstrap_plan_validate_internal || return 1

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --plan-wins)
        [[ -z "$mode" ]] || {
          echo "[ERROR] plan-apply accepts only one reconciliation mode." >&2
          return 2
        }
        mode="plan-wins"
        ;;
      --markers-win)
        [[ -z "$mode" ]] || {
          echo "[ERROR] plan-apply accepts only one reconciliation mode." >&2
          return 2
        }
        mode="markers-win"
        ;;
      --skips-only)
        [[ -z "$mode" ]] || {
          echo "[ERROR] plan-apply accepts only one reconciliation mode." >&2
          return 2
        }
        mode="skips-only"
        ;;
      --reset-done)
        reset_done=1
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unsupported plan-apply mode: $1" >&2
        echo "[INFO] Supported modes: --plan-wins, --markers-win, --skips-only" >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  if (( reset_done )) && [[ "$mode" != "plan-wins" ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] --reset-done is only supported with --plan-wins" >&2
    return 2
  fi

  if config_bootstrap_plan_has_drift; then
    has_drift=1
    config_bootstrap_plan_print_diff
  fi

  if [[ -z "$mode" ]]; then
    if (( has_drift )); then
      mode="markers-win"
      echo "[INFO] Defaulting to markers-win: rewriting bootstrap.plan from current markers." >&2
    else
      mode="skips-only"
    fi
  fi

  case "$mode" in
    plan-wins)
      config_bootstrap_plan_apply_plan_wins "$reset_done" || return 1
      ;;
    markers-win)
      config_bootstrap_plan_apply_markers_win || return 1
      ;;
    skips-only)
      config_bootstrap_plan_apply_skips_only || return 1
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unsupported plan-apply mode: $mode" >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac

  config_bootstrap_plan_sync_observed_state_comments || return 1

  config_bootstrap_plan_diff
}

config_bootstrap_step_plan_skipped() {
  [[ "$(config_bootstrap_plan_state_for_step "${1:-}" 2>/dev/null || true)" == "skipped" ]]
}

config_bootstrap_default_state_for_step() {
  local step="${1:-}"

  case "${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE:-default}" in
    lab)
      printf '%s\n' "skipped"
      ;;
    admin|full|default)
      case "$step" in
        install_gui_support|install_docker|install_terraform|install_kubernets|install_minikube|install_sqlserver_support_2004|install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204)
          printf '%s\n' "skipped"
          ;;
        *)
          printf '%s\n' "pending"
          ;;
      esac
      ;;
    *)
      case "$step" in
        install_gui_support|install_docker|install_terraform|install_kubernets|install_minikube|install_sqlserver_support_2004|install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204)
          printf '%s\n' "skipped"
          ;;
        *)
          printf '%s\n' "pending"
          ;;
      esac
      ;;
  esac
}

config_bootstrap_profile_rows() {
  local file line state step extra lineno=0
  local requested_profile="${1:-${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE:-${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE_DEFAULT:-default}}}"
  local profile

  for profile in "$requested_profile" default; do
    lineno=0
    file="$(config_bootstrap_profile_template_file "$profile")"
    [[ -f "$file" ]] || continue

    while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
      lineno=$((lineno + 1))
      line="${line%%#*}"
      line="$(printf '%s' "$line" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//; s/[[:space:]]*$//')"
      [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue
      read -r state step extra <<< "$line"
      case "$state" in
        next|pending|skipped) ;;
        *)
          echo "[WARN] Invalid profile state at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
          return 1
          ;;
      esac
      [[ -n "${step:-}" && -z "${extra:-}" ]] || {
        echo "[WARN] Invalid profile row at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
        return 1
      }
      config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$step" || {
        echo "[WARN] Unknown profile step at $file:$lineno; using built-in fallback" >&2
        return 1
      }
      printf '%s\t%s\n' "$state" "$step"
    done < "$file"
    return 0
  done

  return 1
}

config_bootstrap_plan_init() {
  local file step state
  file="$(config_bootstrap_plan_file)"
  config_runtime_init || return 1

  if [[ -f "$file" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] Bootstrap plan already exists: $file"
    return 0
  fi

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_header "$file" || return 1
  if config_bootstrap_profile_rows "${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE:-${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE_DEFAULT:-default}}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r state step; do
      [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
      printf '%s %s\n' "$state" "$step" >> "$file"
    done < <(config_bootstrap_profile_rows "${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE:-${BOOTSTRAP_PROFILE_DEFAULT:-default}}")
  else
    while IFS= read -r step; do
      [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
      printf '%s %s\n' "$(config_bootstrap_default_state_for_step "$step")" "$step" >> "$file"
    done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
  fi

  config_bootstrap_plan_write_observed_state_comments "$file" || return 1

  config_chown_target_file "$file" || return 1
  echo "[INFO] Created bootstrap plan: $file"
}

config_bootstrap_step_status() {
  local name="$1"
  config_bootstrap_step_status_source "$name" | awk -F'\t' '{print $1}'
}

config_bootstrap_summary() {
  local step state source

  echo "Bootstrap plan:"
  printf "  %-34s %-10s %s\n" "Step" "State" "Source"
  while IFS= read -r step; do
    [[ -n "$step" ]] || continue
    IFS=$'\t' read -r state source < <(config_bootstrap_step_status_source "$step")
    printf "  %-34s %-10s %s\n" "$step" "$state" "$source"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_steps)
  if [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/install_dev_env.done" ]] && compgen -G "$STATE_DIR/install_dev_env_*.*" > /dev/null; then
    echo "  install_dev_env.done                legacy"
  fi
}

config_bootstrap_steps_summary() {
  local step_name function_name scope network description
  local network_label=""

  echo "Bootstrap steps:"
  printf "  source: %s\n" "$(config_bootstrap_steps_source)"
  printf "  %-34s %-7s %-8s %-32s %s\n" "Step" "Scope" "Network" "Function" "Description"
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r step_name function_name scope network description; do
    [[ -n "$step_name" ]] || continue
    network_label="no"
    [[ "$network" == "1" ]] && network_label="yes"
    printf "  %-34s %-7s %-8s %-32s %s\n" "$step_name" "$scope" "$network_label" "$function_name" "$description"
  done < <(config_bootstrap_step_rows)
}

config_bootstrap_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config bootstrap [run|step STEP_NAME|steps|status|plan|plan-init|plan-file|plan-apply|help]
  config install   [run|step STEP_NAME|steps|status|plan|plan-init|plan-file|plan-apply|help]


Commands:
  run             Run the bootstrap workflow; default when no subcommand is given
  step STEP_NAME  Run one bootstrap step through the normal target-scoped
                  run_once path; honors .done, .skipped, and plan skipped state
  steps           Show known bootstrap steps from manifest metadata
  status          Show resolved step table; non-destructive
  plan            Same resolved step table as status for now; non-destructive
  plan-init       Create target bootstrap.plan if missing
  plan-file       Print target bootstrap.plan path
  plan-apply      Validate, compare with markers, then reconcile
  help            Show this help

Bootstrap model:
  bootstrap.plan is your checklist.
  Marker files are the saved history.
  plan-apply makes state match the checklist.
  bootstrap/install runs allowed steps.

Plan states:
  pending STEP   Allow this step to run
  next STEP      Next intended step; currently treated as pending
  skipped STEP   Do not run this step

Marker states:
  STEP.done      Completed before
  STEP.failed    Failed before
  STEP.running   Running now or interrupted
  STEP.skipped   Blocked from running

Step state commands:
  skip STEP       Block a step by writing STEP.skipped and marking the plan row skipped
  unskip STEP     Allow a step by removing STEP.skipped and changing the plan row to pending
  rm STEP         Remove runtime state/cleanup where supported, then mark the plan row skipped
                  Use unskip STEP later if you want to allow it to run again

status and plan:
  Both show the resolved step table.
  Source tells you where the state came from:
    marker:done      saved runtime marker
    plan:skipped     bootstrap.plan row
    default:pending  no marker and no plan row

steps metadata:
  The steps command shows step scope, network requirement, function allowlist
  target, and description from the bootstrap steps manifest or safe fallback.

plan-apply modes:
  plan-apply
      Default mode. If plan and markers disagree, update the plan from markers.

  plan-apply --skips-only
      Safest mode. Only sync STEP.skipped markers.
      Never removes STEP.done. Never runs bootstrap steps.

  plan-apply --plan-wins
      Make bootstrap.plan control skip/blocking state.
      Keeps STEP.done unless --reset-done is used.

  plan-apply --plan-wins --reset-done
      Allows STEP.done to be removed.
      This can make completed steps run again.

  plan-apply --markers-win
      Rewrite bootstrap.plan from markers:
        done/skipped        -> skipped
        failed/running/none -> pending

Legacy/debug commands:
  plan-validate   Check plan and show warnings; no changes
  plan-diff       Compare plan and markers; no changes
  
Typical safe workflow:
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file
  sudoedit "$(sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-file)"
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan

Execute runnable pending steps:
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap

Run one selected step:
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap step install_dev_env_shell_init
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap step install_dev_env_python_user_tools
  sudo config --target labuser install step install_dev_env_shell_init

Inspect known step metadata:
  config --target labuser bootstrap steps

Make plan fully authoritative and allow reruns:
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins --reset-done
  sudo config --target labuser bootstrap

Safety:
  plan-apply never runs installers.
  bootstrap/install can run apt, downloads, Docker, Kubernetes, SQL tools,
  pyenv, Anaconda, and other installers depending on plan and marker state.
  Use status/plan before broad execution.
EOF
}

config_print_bootstrap_execution_context() {
  echo "Bootstrap execution context:"
  printf "  TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "  TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  STATE_DIR=%s\n" "$STATE_DIR"
  printf "  CURRENT_HOME=%s\n" "$CURRENT_HOME"
}

config_print_bootstrap_step_execution_context() {
  local step="${1:-}"

  echo "Bootstrap step execution context:"
  printf "  TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "  TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  STATE_DIR=%s\n" "$STATE_DIR"
  printf "  CURRENT_HOME=%s\n" "$CURRENT_HOME"
  printf "  STEP_NAME=%s\n" "$step"
}

config_require_sudo_for_bootstrap() {
  if [[ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] config bootstrap/install requires sudo." >&2
    echo "[INFO] Re-run as: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap" >&2
    return 1
  fi
}

config_require_sudo_for_bootstrap_step() {
  local step="${1:-STEP_NAME}"

  if [[ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] config bootstrap/install requires sudo." >&2
    echo "[INFO] Re-run as: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap step ${step}" >&2
    return 1
  fi
}

config_bootstrap_validate_target_context() {
  [[ -n "$TARGET_USER" && -n "$TARGET_HOME" && "$TARGET_HOME" != "/root" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Invalid target context for bootstrap" >&2
    return 1
  }

  [[ -d "$TARGET_HOME" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] TARGET_HOME does not exist: $TARGET_HOME" >&2
    return 1
  }
}

config_bootstrap_run_step_by_name() {
  local step="${1:-}"
  local function_name=""

  function_name="$(config_bootstrap_function_for_step "$step" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  [[ -n "$function_name" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $step" >&2
    return 2
  }

  config_bootstrap_function_allowed "$function_name" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Disallowed bootstrap function for step $step: $function_name" >&2
    return 1
  }

  run_once "$step" "$function_name"
}

config_run_bootstrap_step() {
  local step="${1:-}"
  local current_status=""
  local marker_skipped=0
  local plan_skipped=0

  [[ -n "$step" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Missing bootstrap step name" >&2
    echo "[INFO] Usage: config bootstrap step STEP_NAME" >&2
    return 2
  }

  if (($# > 1)); then
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step argument: $2" >&2
    return 2
  fi

  config_bootstrap_step_is_known "$step" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap step: $step" >&2
    echo "[INFO] See: config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan" >&2
    return 2
  }

  config_require_sudo_for_bootstrap_step "$step" || return 1
  config_bootstrap_validate_target_context || return 1
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_print_bootstrap_step_execution_context "$step"

  [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/$step.skipped" ]] && marker_skipped=1
  if config_bootstrap_step_plan_skipped "$step"; then
    plan_skipped=1
  fi

  current_status="$(config_bootstrap_step_status "$step")"
  if [[ "$current_status" == "done" || "$current_status" == "skipped" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] Skipping network preflight for gated step: $step ($current_status)"
  elif config_bootstrap_step_needs_network "$step"; then
    config_run_preflight_checks || return $?
  else
    echo "[INFO] Skipping network preflight for non-network step: $step"
  fi

  if (( marker_skipped )); then
    echo "[INFO] Step is blocked by skipped marker: $STATE_DIR/$step.skipped"
    echo "[INFO] To allow this step, run: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} unskip ${step}"
  fi

  if (( plan_skipped )); then
    echo "[INFO] Step is blocked by bootstrap.plan"
    echo "[INFO] To allow this step, change it to pending/next in:"
    echo "[INFO]   sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan-file"
    echo "[INFO] Then apply the plan with:"
    echo "[INFO]   sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} bootstrap plan-apply --skips-only"
  fi

  config_bootstrap_run_step_by_name "$step"
}

config_run_mounts() {
  case "${1:-}" in
    help|-h|--help)
      mounts_usage
      return 0
      ;;
  esac

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_refresh_session_context
  config_print_mount_context
  mounts_run "$@" || return 1
}

config_run_bootstrap_execute() {
  config_require_sudo_for_bootstrap || return 1
  config_bootstrap_validate_target_context || return 1
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_print_bootstrap_execution_context
  config_bootstrap_summary
  config_run_preflight_checks || return $?
  run_once update_apt UpdateAPT || return $?
  run_once standard_apps StandardApps || return $?
  run_once install_networking InstallNetworking || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_system_packages InstallDevEnvSystemPackages || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_shell_init InstallDevEnvShellInit || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_dotnet_tools InstallDevEnvDotnetTools || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_python_user_tools InstallDevEnvPythonUserTools || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_pyenv InstallDevEnvPyenv || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_anaconda InstallDevEnvAnaconda || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_azure_cli InstallDevEnvAzureCLI || return $?
  run_once install_dev_env_verify InstallDevEnvVerify || return $?
  run_once install_gui_support InstallGUISupport || return $?
  run_once install_docker InstallDocker || return $?
  run_once install_terraform InstallTerraform || return $?
  run_once install_kubernets InstallKubernetes || return $?
  run_once install_minikube InstallMinikube || return $?
  run_once install_sqlserver_support_2004 InstallSQLServerSupport2004 || return $?
  run_once install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204 InstallSQLServerCLITool2204 || return $?
}

config_run_bootstrap() {
  local subcmd="${1:-run}"

  case "$subcmd" in
    run)
      shift || true
      config_run_bootstrap_execute "$@"
      ;;
    step)
      shift || true
      config_run_bootstrap_step "$@"
      ;;
    steps)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_bootstrap_steps_summary
      ;;
    status|plan)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_state_dir_refresh
      config_bootstrap_summary
      ;;
    plan-init)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_bootstrap_plan_init
      ;;
    plan-file)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_runtime_init || return 1
      config_bootstrap_plan_file
      ;;
    plan-validate)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_bootstrap_plan_validate
      ;;
    plan-diff)
      shift || true
      if (($#)); then
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap ${subcmd} argument: $1" >&2
        config_bootstrap_usage >&2
        return 2
      fi
      config_bootstrap_plan_diff
      ;;
    plan-apply)
      shift || true
      config_bootstrap_plan_apply "$@"
      ;;
    help|-h|--help)
      config_bootstrap_usage
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown bootstrap command: $subcmd" >&2
      config_bootstrap_usage >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac
}

config_print_pull_push_context() {
  local action="${1:-operation}"
  echo "Target ${action} context:"
  printf "  TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "  TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  CURRENT_HOME=%s\n" "$CURRENT_HOME"
  printf "  STATE_DIR=%s\n" "$STATE_DIR"
  printf "  WSL_USER=%s\n" "$WSL_USER"
  printf "  SMB_USER=%s\n" "$SMB_USER"
}

config_print_mount_context() {
  echo "Mount context:"
  printf "  TARGET_USER=%s\n" "$TARGET_USER"
  printf "  TARGET_HOME=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  CURRENT_HOME=%s\n" "$CURRENT_HOME"
  printf "  BASEDIR=%s\n" "$BASEDIR"
  printf "  WSL_USER=%s\n" "$WSL_USER"
  printf "  SMB_USER=%s\n" "$SMB_USER"
  printf "  SMB_USER_INGRESS=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_INGRESS"
  printf "  SMB_USER_EGRESS=%s\n" "$SMB_USER_EGRESS"
  printf "  SMB_USER_RESEARCH=%s\n" "${SMB_USER_RESEARCH:-$SMB_USER}"
  printf "  SMB_USER_PUBLISH=%s\n" "${SMB_USER_PUBLISH:-$SMB_USER}"
}

config_require_mount_path() {
  local path="${1:-}"
  local hint="${2:-Run: sudo config mount or sudo config mount --all}"

  [[ -n "$path" ]] || return 2

  if [[ ! -d "$path" ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] Required mount path is missing: $path" >&2
    echo "[INFO] $hint" >&2
    return 1
  fi
}

config_pull_source_dir() {
  local distrohome="${1:-${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}}"
  printf '%s\n' "$distrohome/.configfiles/$TARGET_USER"
}

config_push_destination_dir() {
  local egress_root="${1:-/mnt/egress}"
  printf '%s\n' "$egress_root/$TARGET_USER"
}

config_require_pull_source() {
  local root="${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}"
  local src

  config_require_mount_path "$root" "Run: sudo config mount or sudo config mount --all" || return 1

  src="$(config_pull_source_dir)"
  [[ -d "$src" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Pull source not found for target $TARGET_USER: $src" >&2
    echo "[INFO] Expected: $root/.configfiles/$TARGET_USER" >&2
    return 1
  }
}

config_require_push_destination_parent() {
  config_require_mount_path "/mnt/egress" "Run: sudo config mount --egress or sudo config mount --all"
}

config_require_sudo_for_admin_sync() {
  local action="${1:-operation}"

  if [[ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] config $action requires sudo." >&2
    echo "[INFO] Re-run as: sudo config --target ${TARGET_USER} $action" >&2
    return 1
  fi
}

config_sync_push_marker_path() {
  printf '%s\n' "$STATE_DIR/last_target_push.meta"
}

config_path_mtime_epoch() {
  local path="${1:-}"
  local stamp=""

  [[ -n "$path" ]] || return 2
  [[ -e "$path" ]] || return 0

  if [[ -d "$path" ]]; then
    stamp="$(find "$path" -type f -printf '%T@\n' 2>/dev/null | sort -nr | head -n1)"
    if [[ -z "$stamp" ]]; then
      stamp="$(stat -c '%Y' "$path" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    else
      stamp="${stamp%.*}"
    fi
  else
    stamp="$(stat -c '%Y' "$path" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  fi

  [[ -n "$stamp" ]] && printf '%s\n' "$stamp"
}

config_last_push_epoch() {
  local marker

  marker="$(config_sync_push_marker_path)"
  [[ -f "$marker" ]] || return 0

  awk -F= '/^pushed_at_epoch=/{print $2; exit}' "$marker" 2>/dev/null
}

config_record_push_marker() {
  local marker now_epoch now_iso

  config_runtime_init || return 1
  marker="$(config_sync_push_marker_path)"
  now_epoch="$(date +%s)"
  now_iso="$(date -Is 2>/dev/null || date)"

  cat >"$marker" <<EOF
target_user=$TARGET_USER
target_home=$TARGET_HOME
pushed_at_epoch=$now_epoch
pushed_at=$now_iso
EOF

  config_chown_target_file "$marker" || return 1
}

config_confirm_pull_if_local_newer() {
  local last_push_epoch=""
  local manifest_output=""
  local kind src dest owner_scope
  local dest_mtime=""
  local newer_paths=()
  local answer=""

  last_push_epoch="$(config_last_push_epoch)"
  [[ -n "$last_push_epoch" ]] || return 0

  manifest_output="$(config_pull_user_items)"
  if config_sync_include_system_items; then
    manifest_output+=$'\n'"$(config_pull_system_items)"
  fi

  while IFS=$'\t' read -r kind src dest owner_scope; do
    [[ -n "${dest:-}" ]] || continue
    dest_mtime="$(config_path_mtime_epoch "$dest")"
    [[ -n "$dest_mtime" ]] || continue
    if (( dest_mtime > last_push_epoch )); then
      newer_paths+=("$dest")
    fi
  done <<< "$manifest_output"

  ((${#newer_paths[@]})) || return 0

  echo "[WARN] Local target files look newer than the last recorded push for $TARGET_USER." >&2
  echo "[WARN] A pull may overwrite newer local changes in:" >&2
  printf '  %s\n' "${newer_paths[@]}" >&2

  if [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] Pull confirmation required, but no interactive terminal is available." >&2
    return 1
  fi

  read -r -p "Continue with pull and overwrite newer local target files? [y/N]: " answer
  case "$answer" in
    [Yy]|[Yy][Ee][Ss]) return 0 ;;
    *)
      echo "[INFO] Pull cancelled." >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

config_run_pull() {
  local marker="$STATE_DIR/copy_config_files.done"

  CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF=0
  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --sync-resolv-conf|--with-resolv-conf)
        CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF=1
        ;;
      help|-h|--help)
        config_pull_push_usage
        return 0
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unsupported pull option: $1" >&2
        echo "[INFO] See \`config help pull\`" >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  config_require_sudo_for_admin_sync pull || return 1
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_print_pull_push_context "pull"

  echo "Pull paths:"
  printf "  source=%s\n" "$(config_pull_source_dir)"
  printf "  target_home=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"

  config_require_pull_source || return 1
  config_confirm_pull_if_local_newer || return 1

  rm -f "$marker"

  mounts_init_session_vars
  config_refresh_session_context
  CopyConfigFiles
}

config_run_push() {
  CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF=0
  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --sync-resolv-conf|--with-resolv-conf)
        CONFIG_SYNC_RESOLV_CONF=1
        ;;
      help|-h|--help)
        config_pull_push_usage
        return 0
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unsupported push option: $1" >&2
        echo "[INFO] See \`config help push\`" >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  config_require_sudo_for_admin_sync push || return 1
  config_runtime_init || return 1
  config_print_pull_push_context "push"

  echo "Push paths:"
  printf "  source_home=%s\n" "$TARGET_HOME"
  printf "  destination=%s\n" "$(config_push_destination_dir)"

  config_require_push_destination_parent || return 1

  mounts_init_session_vars
  config_refresh_session_context
  PushConfigFiles || return 1
  config_record_push_marker
}

config_run_credentials() {
  local subcmd="${1:-help}"
  local helper="$CONFIG_SCRIPT_DIR/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh"

  [[ -x "$helper" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Credential helper not found or not executable: $helper" >&2
    return 1
  }

  case "$subcmd" in
    help|-h|--help)
      config_credentials_usage
      return 0
      ;;
    all|shared|per-share)
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown credentials command: $subcmd" >&2
      config_credentials_usage >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac

  config_refresh_session_context

  target_sudo env \
    HOME="$TARGET_HOME" \
    SMB_USER_DEFAULT="$SMB_USER" \
    SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT="$SMB_USER_INGRESS" \
    SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT="$SMB_USER_EGRESS" \
    SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT="${SMB_USER_RESEARCH:-$SMB_USER}" \
    SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT="${SMB_USER_PUBLISH:-$SMB_USER}" \
    "$helper" "$subcmd"
}

config_help_profiles() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Profiles:

  Account profiles are creation templates under:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/profiles.tsv

SMB profiles choose SMB usernames and default mount identity:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/smb-profiles.tsv

  Mount profiles choose which shares are enabled:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/mount-profiles.tsv

  Python env profiles choose the target Python environment policy:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/install/python-env-profiles.tsv

  Operator defaults:
    SMB profile = operator
    mount profile = all

  Target defaults:
    SMB profile = target or a role-specific SMB profile
    mount profile = target, research, or publish

  Account profiles generate or reconcile per-target env files.
  They do not replace runtime state under state/config-sh.
EOF
}

config_credentials_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config credentials [all|shared|per-share|help]
  config --target USER credentials [all|shared|per-share|help]

Behavior:
  - loads the target policy first
  - runs the SMB credential helper as TARGET_USER with HOME=TARGET_HOME
  - exports resolved SMB usernames for shared, ingress, egress, research, and publish
  - never prints passwords

Examples:
  config credentials help
  sudo config --target labuser credentials per-share
EOF
}

config_account_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config --create-operator [options]
  config --create-target [options]
  config --remove-target [options]
  config --remove-operator [options]
  config create-operator [options]
  config create-target [options]
  config remove-target [options]
  config remove-operator [options]

Options:
  --profile NAME        Use an account profile from accounts/profiles.tsv
  --name USER           Override the Linux username/home basename
  --role-group NAME     Override the role group; required if both Research Scientist groups exist in non-interactive mode
  --interactive         Inspect current state and prompt before applying
  --non-interactive     Do not prompt; derive deterministic defaults
  --expire-password     Expire the password at first login, but only after it was set
  --smb-profile NAME    Override the SMB profile from the account profile
  --mount-profile NAME  Override the mount profile from the account profile
  --dry-run             Show the exact plan without making changes
  --yes                 Apply without confirmation prompts
  help, -h, --help      Show this help

Run `config help profiles` to inspect the profile model.

Lifecycle summary:
  create-target/create-operator reconcile Linux accounts from profile templates
  remove-target removes target accounts conservatively and keeps home/config by default
  remove-operator is more guarded and keeps home/config/state/credentials by default

Examples:
  sudo config --create-target --profile AIEngineer --name aiengineer --dry-run --expire-password
  sudo config --create-operator --profile DefaultOperator --name vmuser --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-operator --name oldoperator --dry-run
EOF
}

config_create_operator_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config --create-operator [options]
  config create-operator [options]

Options:
  --interactive         Inspect current state and prompt before applying
  --non-interactive     Do not prompt; derive deterministic defaults
  --expire-password     After setting a password, force a password change at first login

Defaults:
  profile: DefaultOperator
  mounts: all
  SMB profile: operator
  password prompt: interactive only
  non-interactive: no prompt
  expire at first login: no, unless --expire-password is set

Examples:
  sudo config --create-operator --profile DefaultOperator --name vmuser --dry-run
  sudo config --create-operator --profile DefaultOperator --name vmuser --dry-run --expire-password
  sudo config --create-operator --interactive
EOF
}

config_create_target_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config --create-target [options]
  config create-target [options]

Options:
  --interactive         Inspect current state and prompt before applying
  --non-interactive     Do not prompt; derive deterministic defaults
  --expire-password     After setting a password, force a password change at first login

Defaults:
  profile: DefaultTarget unless --name maps to AIEngineer, ResearchScientist, or Publisher
  mounts: target, research, or publish depending on profile
  SMB profile: target or role-specific profile
  password prompt: interactive only
  non-interactive: no prompt
  expire at first login: no, unless --expire-password is set

Examples:
  sudo config --create-target --profile DefaultTarget --name labuser --dry-run
  sudo config --create-target --profile AIEngineer --name aiengineer --dry-run
  sudo config --create-target --profile AIEngineer --name aiengineer --dry-run --expire-password
  sudo config --create-target --profile ResearchScientist --role-group researchscientist --dry-run
  sudo config --create-target --interactive
EOF
}

config_remove_target_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config --remove-target --name USER [options]
  config remove-target --name USER [options]

Options:
  --name USER           Linux user account to remove
  --dry-run             Show the exact removal plan without making changes or deleting anything
  --keep-home           Keep /home/USER (default)
  --remove-home         Remove /home/USER as well
  --purge-config        Remove the target env file under etc/config-sh/targets
  --non-interactive     Do not prompt; required only to skip destructive confirmations
  help, -h, --help      Show this help

Defaults:
  remove Linux user: yes
  remove home: no
  purge target env: no
  remove credentials directly: no
  remove state directly: no

Examples:
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --keep-home
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --remove-home
  sudo config --remove-target --name aiengineer --purge-config
EOF
}

config_remove_operator_usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage:
  config --remove-operator --name USER [options]
  config remove-operator --name USER [options]

Options:
  --name USER               Operator Linux user account to remove
  --dry-run                 Show the exact removal plan without making changes or deleting anything
  --keep-home               Keep /home/USER (default)
  --force-remove-operator   Required to remove vmuser when it is not the logged-in operator
  --non-interactive         Do not prompt
  help, -h, --help          Show this help

Guardrails:
  - Refuses to remove the currently logged-in operator user
  - Refuses to remove vmuser unless --force-remove-operator is provided
  - Keeps home, config root, target envs, state, and credentials by default
  - Does not implement broad purge behavior in this milestone

Examples:
  sudo config --remove-operator --name oldoperator --dry-run
  sudo config --remove-operator --name oldoperator --keep-home
  sudo config remove-operator --name oldoperator --dry-run
EOF
}

config_print_account_system_state() {
  echo "System state:"
  echo "--- getent passwd ---"
  getent passwd | sed -n '1,20p'
  echo "--- getent group operator ---"
  getent group operator || true
  echo "--- getent group aiengineer ---"
  getent group aiengineer || true
  echo "--- getent group researchscientist ---"
  getent group researchscientist || true
  echo "--- getent group researchassistent ---"
  getent group researchassistent || true
  echo "--- getent group publisher ---"
  getent group publisher || true
  echo "--- account profile files ---"
  ls -1 "$(config_accounts_config_dir)" 2>/dev/null || true
}

config_print_account_plan_actions() {
  local expire_password="${1:-0}"

  echo "Planned actions:"
  if getent group "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "  keep primary group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}"
  else
    echo "  create primary group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" ]]; then
    if getent group "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "  keep role group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}"
    else
      echo "  create role group: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}"
    fi
  fi
  if getent passwd "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "  update Linux user: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
  else
    echo "  create Linux user: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
  fi
  echo "  home: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME}"
  echo "  home mode: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE}"
  echo "  reconcile target env: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ENV_FILE}"
  echo "  password policy:"
  echo "    password prompt: interactive only"
  echo "    non-interactive: no prompt"
  if config_bool_is_true "$expire_password"; then
    echo "    expire at first login: yes"
  else
    echo "    expire at first login: no"
  fi
}

config_confirm_destructive_account_action() {
  local prompt="${1:-Proceed?}"
  local answer=""
  read -r -p "$prompt [y/N] " answer
  case "$answer" in
    [Yy]*) return 0 ;;
    *) echo "[INFO] Aborted account removal."; return 1 ;;
  esac
}

config_remove_account_operator_user() {
  if [[ -n "${SUDO_USER:-}" && "${SUDO_USER}" != "root" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$SUDO_USER"
  else
    id -un
  fi
}

config_print_remove_account_plan() {
  local kind="${1:-target}"
  local user="${2:-}"
  local home="${3:-}"
  local target_env="${4:-}"
  local remove_home="${5:-0}"
  local purge_config="${6:-0}"

  echo "Remove ${kind} account plan:"
  echo "  Linux user: ${user}"
  echo "  account kind: ${kind}"
  if [[ "$kind" == "target" ]]; then
    echo "  home: ${home}"
    echo "  target env: ${target_env}"
  fi
  echo
  if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
    echo "WARNING:"
    echo "  Removing an operator can break config orchestration."
    echo
  fi
  echo "Planned actions:"
  echo "  remove Linux user: yes"
  if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
    echo "  remove home: no"
    echo "  purge config root: no"
    echo "  remove target envs: no"
    echo "  remove state: no"
    echo "  remove credentials: no"
  elif config_bool_is_true "$remove_home"; then
    echo "  remove home: yes"
    echo "  purge target env: $(config_bool_is_true "$purge_config" && printf yes || printf no)"
    echo "  remove credentials: yes, because they are under removed home"
    echo "  remove state: yes, because it is under removed home"
  else
    echo "  remove home: no"
    echo "  purge target env: $(config_bool_is_true "$purge_config" && printf yes || printf no)"
    echo "  remove credentials: no"
    echo "  remove state: no"
  fi
}

config_apply_remove_account_plan() {
  local kind="${1:-target}"
  local user="${2:-}"
  local home="${3:-}"
  local target_env="${4:-}"
  local remove_home="${5:-0}"
  local purge_config="${6:-0}"
  local non_interactive="${7:-0}"

  [[ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Account removal requires sudo/root." >&2
    return 1
  }

  if config_bool_is_true "$remove_home" && ! config_bool_is_true "$non_interactive"; then
    echo "[WARN] Removing home will also remove target-owned state and credential files under ${home}."
    config_confirm_destructive_account_action "Remove ${home} as part of removing ${user}?" || return 1
  fi

  if config_bool_is_true "$purge_config" && ! config_bool_is_true "$non_interactive"; then
    config_confirm_destructive_account_action "Remove target env file ${target_env}?" || return 1
  fi

  if getent passwd "$user" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
      userdel "$user"
    elif config_bool_is_true "$remove_home"; then
      userdel -r "$user"
    else
      userdel "$user"
    fi
  else
    echo "[INFO] Linux user already absent: $user"
  fi

  if config_bool_is_true "$purge_config"; then
    rm -f "$target_env"
  fi
}

config_run_remove_account() {
  local kind="${1:-target}"
  shift || true
  local name="" dry_run=0 keep_home=1 remove_home=0 purge_config=0 non_interactive=0
  local force_remove_operator=0
  local operator_user="" target_env="" home=""

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      help|-h|--help)
        if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
          config_remove_operator_usage
        else
          config_remove_target_usage
        fi
        return 0
        ;;
      --name)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --name" >&2; return 2; }
        name="$1"
        ;;
      --dry-run)
        dry_run=1
        ;;
      --keep-home)
        keep_home=1
        remove_home=0
        ;;
      --remove-home)
        remove_home=1
        keep_home=0
        ;;
      --purge-config)
        purge_config=1
        ;;
      --force-remove-operator)
        force_remove_operator=1
        ;;
      --non-interactive)
        non_interactive=1
        ;;
      *)
        if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
          echo "[ERROR] Unknown remove-operator option: $1" >&2
          config_remove_operator_usage >&2
        else
          echo "[ERROR] Unknown remove-target option: $1" >&2
          config_remove_target_usage >&2
        fi
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  [[ "$kind" == "target" || "$kind" == "operator" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Unsupported account removal kind: $kind" >&2
    return 2
  }
  [[ -n "$name" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] remove-${kind} requires --name USER" >&2
    return 2
  }

  operator_user="$(config_remove_account_operator_user)"
  if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
    [[ "$keep_home" == "1" && "$remove_home" == "0" ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] remove-operator supports only --keep-home in this milestone." >&2
      return 2
    }
    if config_bool_is_true "$purge_config"; then
      echo "[ERROR] remove-operator does not support config purge in this milestone." >&2
      return 2
    fi
    case "$name" in
      ""|root)
        echo "[ERROR] Refusing to remove protected account via remove-operator: $name" >&2
        return 1
        ;;
    esac
    if [[ "$name" == "$operator_user" ]]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Refusing to remove the currently logged-in operator user: $name" >&2
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$name" == "vmuser" && "$force_remove_operator" != "1" ]]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Refusing to remove vmuser without --force-remove-operator." >&2
      return 1
    fi
  else
    case "$name" in
      root|vmuser)
        echo "[ERROR] Refusing to remove protected account via remove-target: $name" >&2
        echo "[INFO] Use remove-operator for operator removal." >&2
        return 1
        ;;
    esac
    if [[ "$name" == "$operator_user" ]]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Refusing to remove the current operator account via remove-target: $name" >&2
      echo "[INFO] Use remove-operator for operator removal." >&2
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  home="/home/$name"
  target_env="$(config_target_config_file_for_user "$name")"

  config_print_remove_account_plan "$kind" "$name" "$home" "$target_env" "$remove_home" "$purge_config"

  if (( dry_run )); then
    return 0
  fi

  config_apply_remove_account_plan "$kind" "$name" "$home" "$target_env" "$remove_home" "$purge_config" "$non_interactive"
}

config_apply_account_plan() {
  local interactive_mode="${1:-0}"
  local expire_password="${2:-0}"
  local created_linux_user=0
  local password_was_set=0
  local answer=""

  [[ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Account creation requires sudo/root." >&2
    return 1
  }

  groupadd -f "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}"
  if [[ -n "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" ]]; then
    groupadd -f "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}"
  fi

  if getent passwd "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    usermod -g "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
  elif config_bool_is_true "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_CREATE_LINUX_USER}"; then
    useradd -m -d "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME}" -s /bin/bash -g "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
    created_linux_user=1
  else
    echo "[ERROR] Linux user does not exist and profile forbids creation: ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" >&2
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -n "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" && "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" != "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}" ]]; then
    usermod -aG "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
  fi
  if config_bool_is_true "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ADD_OPERATOR_TO_ROLE_GROUP}" && [[ -n "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" ]] && getent passwd vmuser >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    usermod -aG "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ROLE_GROUP}" vmuser
  fi

  install -d -m "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME}"
  chown "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}:${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PRIMARY_GROUP}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME}"
  chmod "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME_MODE}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_HOME}"
  config_reconcile_target_env_file "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ENV_FILE}" 0

  if (( created_linux_user )); then
    if (( interactive_mode )); then
      read -r -p "Set Linux password for ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME} now? [y/N] " answer
      case "$answer" in
        [Yy]*)
          passwd "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" || return 1
          password_was_set=1
          ;;
        *) echo "Set it later with: sudo passwd ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" ;;
      esac
    else
      echo "Set it later with: sudo passwd ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
    fi
  fi

  if config_bool_is_true "$expire_password"; then
    if (( password_was_set )); then
      chage -d 0 "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" || return 1
    else
      echo "Password was not set, so first-login expiry was not applied."
      echo "Set the password first:"
      echo "  sudo passwd ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
      echo "Then expire it:"
      echo "  sudo chage -d 0 ${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
    fi
  fi
}

config_validate_account_plan_kind() {
  local expected_kind="${1:-}"
  local actual_kind="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_KIND:-}"
  local profile_name="${ACCOUNT_PLAN_PROFILE_NAME:-unknown}"

  if [[ "$actual_kind" != "$expected_kind" ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] Profile kind mismatch." >&2
    echo "[ERROR] Command kind: $expected_kind" >&2
    echo "[ERROR] Profile $profile_name has account_kind=${actual_kind:-unset}" >&2
    if [[ "$expected_kind" == "operator" ]]; then
      echo "[INFO] Use create-target for target profiles, or choose one of these operator profiles:" >&2
      config_print_account_profiles_by_kind operator
    else
      echo "[INFO] Use create-operator for operator profiles, or choose one of these target profiles:" >&2
      config_print_account_profiles_by_kind target
    fi
    return 1
  fi
}

config_run_create_account() {
  local kind="${1:-target}"
  shift || true
  local profile="" name="" interactive=0 non_interactive=0 dry_run=0 assume_yes=0
  local expire_password=0
  local smb_profile_override="" mount_profile_override="" role_group_override=""
  local resolved_profile=""

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      help|-h|--help)
        if [[ "$kind" == "operator" ]]; then
          config_create_operator_usage
        else
          config_create_target_usage
        fi
        return 0
        ;;
      --profile)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --profile" >&2; return 2; }
        profile="$1"
        ;;
      --name)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --name" >&2; return 2; }
        name="$1"
        ;;
      --role-group)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --role-group" >&2; return 2; }
        role_group_override="$1"
        ;;
      --interactive)
        interactive=1
        ;;
      --non-interactive)
        non_interactive=1
        ;;
      --expire-password)
        expire_password=1
        ;;
      --dry-run)
        dry_run=1
        ;;
      --yes)
        assume_yes=1
        ;;
      --smb-profile)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --smb-profile" >&2; return 2; }
        smb_profile_override="$1"
        ;;
      --mount-profile)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --mount-profile" >&2; return 2; }
        mount_profile_override="$1"
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown account creation option: $1" >&2
        config_account_usage >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  if (( interactive )) && (( non_interactive )); then
    echo "[ERROR] Use either --interactive or --non-interactive, not both." >&2
    return 2
  fi

  resolved_profile="$(config_resolve_account_profile_name "$kind" "$profile" "$name")"

  if (( interactive )); then
    config_print_account_system_state
    config_list_account_profiles
    resolved_profile="$(config_prompt_for_profile_selection "account profile" "$(config_account_profiles_file)" "$resolved_profile" 0)" || return 1
  fi

  config_resolve_account_profile "$resolved_profile" "$name" "$smb_profile_override" "$mount_profile_override" "$role_group_override" "$interactive" || return 1
  config_validate_account_plan_kind "$kind" || return 1

  if (( interactive )); then
    config_list_smb_profiles
    smb_profile_override="$(config_prompt_for_profile_selection "SMB profile" "$(config_smb_profiles_file)" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_SMB_PROFILE}" 1)" || return 1
    config_list_mount_profiles
    mount_profile_override="$(config_prompt_for_profile_selection "mount profile" "$(config_mount_profiles_file)" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_MOUNT_PROFILE}" 1)" || return 1
    config_resolve_account_profile "$resolved_profile" "$name" "$smb_profile_override" "$mount_profile_override" "$role_group_override" "$interactive" || return 1
    config_validate_account_plan_kind "$kind" || return 1
  fi

  config_print_account_plan_summary
  config_print_account_plan_actions "$expire_password"

  if (( dry_run )); then
    config_reconcile_target_env_file "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_ACCOUNT_NAME}" "${ACCOUNT_PLAN_TARGET_ENV_FILE}" 1
    return 0
  fi

  if (( interactive )) && (( !assume_yes )); then
    read -r -p "Apply this account plan? [y/N] " answer
    case "$answer" in
      [Yy]*) ;;
      *) echo "[INFO] Aborted account creation."; return 1 ;;
    esac
  fi

  config_apply_account_plan "$interactive" "$expire_password"
}

config_profiles_command() {
  config_list_account_profiles
  echo
  config_list_smb_profiles
  echo
  config_list_mount_profiles
  echo
  config_list_python_env_profiles
}

config_run_user() {
  local mode=""
  local value=""
  local selected_user="$TARGET_USER"
  local selected_home="$TARGET_HOME"
  local use_init=0

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      help|-h|--help)
        config_user_usage
        return 0
        ;;
      --init)
        use_init=1
        ;;
      --as)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --as" >&2; return 2; }
        selected_user="$1"
        ;;
      --shell-command)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --shell-command" >&2; return 2; }
        mode="shell-command"
        value="$1"
        ;;
      --shell-file)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing value after --shell-file" >&2; return 2; }
        mode="shell-file"
        value="$1"
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown user option: $1" >&2
        config_user_usage >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  selected_home="$(getent passwd "$selected_user" | cut -d: -f6)"
  [[ -n "$selected_home" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve home for user=$selected_user" >&2; return 1; }

  case "$mode" in
    shell-command)
      if ((use_init)); then
        TARGET_USER="$selected_user" TARGET_HOME="$selected_home" run_as_target_shell "$value"
      else
        TARGET_USER="$selected_user" TARGET_HOME="$selected_home" run_as_target bash -c "$value"
      fi
      ;;
    shell-file)
      [[ -f "$value" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Shell file not found: $value" >&2; return 1; }
      if ((use_init)); then
        TARGET_USER="$selected_user" TARGET_HOME="$selected_home" run_as_target_shell "bash \"$value\""
      else
        TARGET_USER="$selected_user" TARGET_HOME="$selected_home" run_as_target bash "$value"
      fi
      ;;
    *)
      config_user_usage >&2
      return 2
      ;;
  esac
}
