# State directory
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CONFIG_STATE_DIR="${CONFIG_STATE_DIR:-${TARGET_HOME}/.local/state/config-sh}"
STATE_DIR="$CONFIG_STATE_DIR"
export CONFIG_STATE_DIR STATE_DIR

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# IMPORTANT:
# - Only execute installer/mount flow when script is run directly.
# - When sourced, only functions/aliases/helpers are loaded.
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# always available when sourced or executed
DISTROHOME="${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}"
export DISTROHOME

if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
  config_parse_global_args "$@" || exit $?
  cmd="$CONFIG_COMMAND"
  set -- "${CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS[@]}"

  case "$cmd" in
    -h|--help)
      config_usage
      ;;
    help)
      config_help "$@"
      ;;
    config-init)
      config_init_example_files "$@"
      ;;
    howto)
      config_help_howto
      ;;
    menu|commands)
      config_help_menu
      ;;
    profiles)
      config_profiles_command
      ;;
    preflight)
      config_run_preflight_checks
      ;;
    config-show)
      config_show
      ;;
    status)
      config_status
      ;;
    skip)
      if [[ -z "${1:-}" ]]; then
        echo "Usage: config skip STEP_NAME" >&2
        exit 1
      fi
      config_skip_step "$1"
      ;;
    unskip)
      if [[ -z "${1:-}" ]]; then
        echo "Usage: config unskip STEP_NAME" >&2
        exit 1
      fi
      config_unskip_step "$1"
      ;;
    rm)
      if [[ -z "${1:-}" ]]; then
        echo "Usage: config rm STEP_NAME" >&2
        exit 1
      fi
      config_rm_step "$1"
      ;;
    mounts|mount)
      config_run_mounts "$@"
      ;;
    bootstrap|install)
      config_run_bootstrap "$@"
      ;;
    pull)
      config_run_pull "$@"
      ;;
    push)
      config_run_push "$@"
      ;;
    credentials|credential|secrets)
      config_run_credentials "$@"
      ;;
    run|user)
      config_run_user "$@"
      ;;
    --create-operator|create-operator)
      config_run_create_account operator "$@"
      ;;
    --create-target|create-target)
      config_run_create_account target "$@"
      ;;
    --remove-target|remove-target)
      config_run_remove_account target "$@"
      ;;
    --remove-operator|remove-operator)
      config_run_remove_account operator "$@"
      ;;
    all|legacy)
      config_run_mounts "$@"
      config_run_bootstrap "$@"
      echo 'To set up the X11 display:'
      echo 'On Windows 10/11, install a Windows X Server. Instructions:'
      echo 'https://thomasward.com/wsl2-x11/'
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown command: $cmd" >&2
      config_usage >&2
      exit 2
      ;;
  esac
fi

#########################################
=== START OF ANALYSIS FOR /home/vmuser/.local/bin/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh ===
#########################################
Overview for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh:
This file is a Bourne-Again shell script, ASCII text executable with a size of 4433 bytes.
---
Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Create CIFS credentials files for mounts.sh with hidden password entry.

set -euo pipefail

CREDENTIALS_DIR="${HOME}/.local/share/wsl-mounts"
DEFAULT_DOMAIN="${DEFAULT_DOMAIN:-}"
DISTROHOME_USER="${DISTROHOME_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-hector}}"
SCRIPTING_USER="${SCRIPTING_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-hector}}"
INGRESS_USER="${INGRESS_USER:-${SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
EGRESS_USER="${EGRESS_USER:-${SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
RESEARCH_USER="${RESEARCH_USER:-${SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-Researcher}}}"
PUBLISH_USER="${PUBLISH_USER:-${SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-labuser}}}"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: create-cifs-credentials-files.sh [all|shared|per-share|help]

Commands:
  all        Create both the shared file and per-share files
  shared     Create a single shared cifs.credentials file
  per-share  Create distrohome/scripting/ingress/egress/research/publish credentials files
  help       Show this help

Files created:
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/cifs.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/distrohome.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/scripting.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/ingress.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/egress.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/research.credentials
  ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts/publish.credentials

Format:
  username=your_smb_username
  password=your_smb_password
  # optional when needed by your SMB server:
  # domain=YOUR_DOMAIN

Defaults in this script:
  distrohome  username=hector
  scripting   username=hector
  ingress     username=labuser
  egress      username=labuser
  research    username=Researcher
  publish     username=labuser
  domain      disabled unless DEFAULT_DOMAIN is set in the script or env

How to use:
  1. Run: create-cifs-credentials-files.sh all
  2. Enter the passwords when prompted.
  3. Then run: config mount
     Or: config mount --all

Why use --credentials-file PATH later:
  Only if you want to override the default files under ~/.local/share/wsl-mounts for one run,
  or if your credentials file lives in a different location.
EOF
}

ensure_dir() {
  install -d -m 700 "$CREDENTIALS_DIR"
}

prompt_password() {
  local label="$1"
  local password confirm

  while true; do
    read -r -s -p "Enter password for $label: " password
    printf '\n' >&2
    [[ -n "$password" ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] Password cannot be empty." >&2
      continue
    }

    read -r -s -p "Confirm password for $label: " confirm
    printf '\n' >&2
    [[ "$password" == "$confirm" ]] && break
    echo "[ERROR] Passwords did not match." >&2
  done

  printf '%s' "$password"
}

write_credentials_file() {
  local file="$1"
  local username="$2"
  local label="$3"
  local password

  password="$(prompt_password "$label")"

  {
    printf 'username=%s\n' "$username"
    printf 'password=%s\n' "$password"
    if [[ -n "$DEFAULT_DOMAIN" ]]; then
      printf 'domain=%s\n' "$DEFAULT_DOMAIN"
    fi
  } > "$file"

  chmod 600 "$file"
  echo "[WROTE] $file"
}

create_shared() {
  local username

  read -r -p "Enter username for shared SMB credentials: " username
  [[ -n "$username" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] Shared SMB username cannot be empty." >&2
    return 1
  }

  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/cifs.credentials" "$username" "shared SMB user $username"
}

create_per_share() {
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/distrohome.credentials" "$DISTROHOME_USER" "distrohome ($DISTROHOME_USER)"
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/scripting.credentials" "$SCRIPTING_USER" "scripting ($SCRIPTING_USER)"
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/ingress.credentials" "$INGRESS_USER" "ingress ($INGRESS_USER)"
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/egress.credentials" "$EGRESS_USER" "egress ($EGRESS_USER)"
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/research.credentials" "$RESEARCH_USER" "research ($RESEARCH_USER)"
  write_credentials_file "$CREDENTIALS_DIR/publish.credentials" "$PUBLISH_USER" "publish ($PUBLISH_USER)"
}

main() {
  local cmd="${1:-all}"

  case "$cmd" in
    help|-h|--help)
      usage
      ;;
    all)
      ensure_dir
      create_shared
      create_per_share
      ;;
    shared)
      ensure_dir
      create_shared
      ;;
    per-share)
      ensure_dir
      create_per_share
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unknown command: $cmd" >&2
      usage >&2
      exit 2
      ;;
  esac
}

main "$@"

#########################################
=== START OF ANALYSIS FOR /home/vmuser/.local/bin/lv.sh ===
#########################################
Overview for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/lv.sh:
This file is a Bourne-Again shell script, ASCII text executable with a size of 26408 bytes.
---
Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/lv.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Quiet lv dashboard/helper library. Safe to source from shell startup.

_lv_reset='\033[0m'
_lv_bold='\033[1m'
_lv_yellow='\033[33m'
_lv_green='\033[32m'
_lv_cyan='\033[36m'
_lv_gray='\033[90m'

_lv_conda_base() { conda info --base 2>/dev/null; }
_lv_workon_home() { printf "%s" "${WORKON_HOME:-$HOME/.virtualenvs}"; }
_lv_ip() { hostname -I 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}'; }

_lv_alias_base() {
  local files=("$HOME/.local/bin/conda.sh" "$HOME/.local/.bash_aliases" "$HOME/.bashrc")
  local out=() f
  for f in "${files[@]}"; do
    [[ -f "$f" ]] && out+=("$f")
  done
  (IFS=' '; printf "%s" "${out[*]}")
}

_lv_clip() {
  local s="$1" w="$2"
  ((${#s}<=w)) && { printf "%s" "$s"; return; }
  ((w<4)) && { printf "%s" "${s:0:w}"; return; }
  printf "%s..." "${s:0:w-3}"
}

_lv_normpath() {
  local p="$1"
  [[ -z "$p" ]] && return 0
  realpath -m "$p" 2>/dev/null || readlink -f "$p" 2>/dev/null || printf "%s" "$p"
}

_lv_shortpath() {
  local p="$1" w="${2:-24}" cb wh pnorm cbnorm whnorm
  [[ -z "$p" ]] && return 0

  cb="$(_lv_conda_base)"
  wh="$(_lv_workon_home)"

  pnorm="$(_lv_normpath "$p")"
  cbnorm="$(_lv_normpath "$cb")"
  whnorm="$(_lv_normpath "$wh")"

  if [[ -n "$cbnorm" && "$pnorm" == "$cbnorm/envs/"* ]]; then
    p="base/envs/${pnorm#"$cbnorm/envs/"}"
  elif [[ -n "$whnorm" && "$pnorm" == "$whnorm/"* ]]; then
    p="base/${pnorm#"$whnorm/"}"
  elif [[ "$pnorm" == "$HOME/"* ]]; then
    p="~/${pnorm#"$HOME/"}"
  else
    p="$pnorm"
  fi

  ((${#p}>w)) && p="...${p: -$((w-3))}"
  printf "%s" "$p"
}

_lv_capture_alias_rows() {
  local f line name body comment pending_comment
  declare -A seen=()
  local files=("$HOME/.local/bin/conda.sh" "$HOME/.local/.bash_aliases" "$HOME/.bashrc")

  for f in "${files[@]}"; do
    [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
    pending_comment=""

    while IFS= read -r line; do
      [[ "$line" == '#!'* ]] && { pending_comment=""; continue; }

      if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*#[[:space:]]*(.+)$ ]]; then
        comment="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
        if [[ -n "$comment" && "$comment" != '>>>'* && "$comment" != '!!'* ]]; then
          pending_comment="$comment"
        else
          pending_comment=""
        fi
        continue
      fi

      [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*$ ]] && continue

      if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*alias[[:space:]]+([^=[:space:]]+)= ]]; then
        name="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
        [[ -n "$name" && -z "${seen[$name]}" ]] || continue

        if [[ -n "$pending_comment" ]]; then
          printf "sep\t%s\t\n" "$pending_comment"
          pending_comment=""
        fi

        if alias "$name" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
          body="$(alias "$name" | sed -n "s/^alias [^=]*='\(.*\)'$/\1/p")"
          printf "data\t%s\t%s\n" "$name" "$body"
          seen["$name"]=1
        fi
        continue
      fi

      pending_comment=""
    done < "$f"
  done

  while IFS= read -r line; do
    name="${line#alias }"
    name="${name%%=*}"
    [[ -n "$name" && -z "${seen[$name]}" ]] || continue
    body="$(printf "%s\n" "$line" | sed -n "s/^alias [^=]*='\(.*\)'$/\1/p")"
    printf "data\t%s\t%s\n" "$name" "$body"
    seen["$name"]=1
  done < <(alias -p 2>/dev/null)
}

_lv_capture_conda_rows() {
  command -v conda >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  local base
  base="$(conda info --base 2>/dev/null)"
  [[ -n "$base" ]] && printf "base\t%s\n" "$base"

  conda info --envs 2>/dev/null | awk '
    NF && $1 !~ /^#/ {
      n=$1
      p=($2=="*" ? $3 : $2)
      if (n!="base") print n "\t" p
    }'
}

_lv_capture_venv_rows() {
  local d w="$(_lv_workon_home)"
  declare -A seen=()

  [[ -d "$w" ]] && printf "base\t%s\n" "$w"

  if [[ -n "$VIRTUAL_ENV" && -d "$VIRTUAL_ENV" ]]; then
    printf "%s\t%s\n" "$(basename "$VIRTUAL_ENV")" "$VIRTUAL_ENV"
    seen["$VIRTUAL_ENV"]=1
  fi

  if [[ -d "$w" ]]; then
    for d in "$w"/*; do
      [[ -d "$d" ]] || continue
      [[ -n "${seen[$d]}" ]] && continue
      if [[ -f "$d/bin/activate" || -f "$d/pyvenv.cfg" ]]; then
        printf "%s\t%s\n" "$(basename "$d")" "$d"
        seen["$d"]=1
      fi
    done
  fi

  for d in "$PWD/.venv" "$PWD/venv" "$PWD/env"; do
    [[ -d "$d" ]] || continue
    [[ -n "${seen[$d]}" ]] && continue
    if [[ -f "$d/bin/activate" || -f "$d/pyvenv.cfg" ]]; then
      printf "%s\t%s\n" "$(basename "$d")" "$d"
      seen["$d"]=1
    fi
  done
}

_lv_find_conda_path() { _lv_capture_conda_rows | awk -F'\t' -v n="$1" '$1==n{print $2;exit}'; }
_lv_find_venv_path()  { _lv_capture_venv_rows  | awk -F'\t' -v n="$1" 'tolower($1)==tolower(n){print $2;exit}'; }
_lv_find_alias_body() { alias "$1" 2>/dev/null | sed -n "s/^alias [^=]*='\(.*\)'$/\1/p"; }

_lv_show_important_pkgs() {
  local run_cmd="$1" p v found=0
  local pkgs=(numpy pandas scipy scikit-learn matplotlib seaborn torch tensorflow jupyter ipykernel requests fastapi flask django)
  for p in "${pkgs[@]}"; do
    v=$(eval "$run_cmd python -m pip show \"$p\" 2>/dev/null" | awk -F': ' '/^Version:/{print $2; exit}')
    [[ -n "$v" ]] && { printf "  %-14s %s\n" "$p" "$v"; found=1; }
  done
  ((found==0)) && echo "  (none of the common data/web ML packages found)"
}

_lv_print_key_envs() {
  local src="$1" line k v p
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
    k="${line%%=*}"
    v="${line#*=}"

    case "$k" in
      PATH|PYTHONPATH)
        echo "  $k:"
        IFS=':' read -r -a _lv_parts <<< "$v"
        for p in "${_lv_parts[@]}"; do
          [[ -n "$p" ]] && echo "    - $p"
        done
        ;;
      *)
        if ((${#v} > 96)); then
          echo "  $k:"
          printf "%s\n" "$v" | fold -s -w 92 | sed 's/^/    /'
        else
          echo "  $k=$v"
        fi
        ;;
    esac
  done <<< "$src"
}

_lv_help() {
  cat <<'EOF'
lv usage:
  lv                         Show compact dashboard
  lv -help                   Show this help
  lv alias <name>            Inspect alias by name
  lv conda <name>            Inspect conda env by name
  lv venv <name>             Inspect venv by name
  lv alias <name> -new       Create alias (prompts for command)
  lv conda <name> -new       Create conda env
  lv venv <name> -new        Create venv env
  lv alias <name> -del       Delete alias
  lv conda <name> -del       Delete conda env
  lv venv <name> -del        Delete venv directory
  lv <env> <name> --clone --target <env> <name> --packages <-pkg1 -pkg2 ...>  Clone existing conda|venv environment
EOF
}

_lv_inspect() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2" cpath vpath a vars

  case "$kind" in
    conda)
      cpath="$(_lv_find_conda_path "$name")"
      if [[ -n "$cpath" ]]; then
        echo -e "${_lv_bold}${_lv_green}Conda Environment:${_lv_reset} $name"
        echo "Path: $(_lv_shortpath "$cpath" 70)"
        echo "Python: $(conda run -n "$name" python -V 2>/dev/null)"
        echo "Pip:    $(conda run -n "$name" python -m pip --version 2>/dev/null)"
        echo "Key env vars:"
        vars="$(conda run -n "$name" env 2>/dev/null | grep -E '^(CONDA_PREFIX|PATH|PYTHONPATH|PIP_INDEX_URL|HTTP_PROXY|HTTPS_PROXY)=')"
        _lv_print_key_envs "$vars"
        echo "Important packages:"
        _lv_show_important_pkgs "conda run -n \"$name\""
      else
        echo "Conda env '$name' not found."
      fi
      ;;
    venv)
      vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$name")"
      if [[ -n "$vpath" ]]; then
        echo -e "${_lv_bold}${_lv_cyan}Venv Environment:${_lv_reset} $name"
        echo "Path: $(_lv_shortpath "$vpath" 70)"
        (
          source "$vpath/bin/activate"
          echo "Python: $(python -V 2>/dev/null)"
          echo "Pip:    $(python -m pip --version 2>/dev/null)"
          echo "Key env vars:"
          vars="$(env | grep -E '^(VIRTUAL_ENV|PATH|PYTHONPATH|PIP_INDEX_URL|HTTP_PROXY|HTTPS_PROXY)=')"
          _lv_print_key_envs "$vars"
          echo "Important packages:"
          _lv_show_important_pkgs ""
        )
      else
        echo "Venv '$name' not found."
      fi
      ;;
    alias)
      a="$(_lv_find_alias_body "$name")"
      [[ -n "$a" ]] && echo "Alias '$name': $a" || echo "Alias '$name' not found."
      ;;
    *)
      echo "Unknown focus '$kind'. Use: alias | conda | venv"
      ;;
  esac
}

_lv_create_new() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2"
  case "$kind" in
    conda)
      command -v conda >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "conda not found."; return 1; }
      conda create -n "$name" python=3.11 -y
      ;;
    venv)
      local w p
      w="$(_lv_workon_home)"
      p="$w/$name"
      mkdir -p "$w"
      python3 -m venv "$p"
      echo "Created venv: $p"
      ;;
    alias)
      local cmd
      read -r -p "Command for alias '$name': " cmd
      [[ -z "$cmd" ]] && { echo "No command provided."; return 1; }
      alias "$name=$cmd"
      printf "alias %s='%s'\n" "$name" "$cmd" >> "$HOME/.local/.bash_aliases"
      echo "Created alias: $name"
      ;;
    *)
      echo "Unknown focus '$kind'. Use: alias | conda | venv"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

_lv_delete() {
  local kind="$1" n="$2" c v

  case "$kind" in
    conda)
      c="$(_lv_find_conda_path "$n")"
      [[ -z "$c" ]] && { echo "Conda env '$n' not found."; return; }
      echo "Will delete conda env: $n ($(_lv_shortpath "$c" 70))"
      read -r -p "Continue? [y/N] " ans
      [[ "$ans" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Cancelled."; return; }
      conda env remove -n "$n" -y
      echo "Done."
      ;;
    venv)
      v="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$n")"
      [[ -z "$v" ]] && { echo "Venv '$n' not found."; return; }
      echo "Will delete venv dir: $(_lv_shortpath "$v" 70)"
      read -r -p "Continue? [y/N] " ans
      [[ "$ans" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Cancelled."; return; }
      rm -rf -- "$v"
      echo "Done."
      ;;
    alias)
      if ! alias "$n" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "Alias '$n' not found."
        return
      fi
      echo "Will delete alias: $n"
      read -r -p "Continue? [y/N] " ans
      [[ "$ans" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Cancelled."; return; }
      unalias "$n"
      sed -i.bak "/^[[:space:]]*alias[[:space:]]\+$n=/d" "$HOME/.local/.bash_aliases" 2>/dev/null
      echo "Done."
      ;;
    *)
      echo "Unknown focus '$kind'. Use: alias | conda | venv"
      ;;
  esac
}

_lv_alias_file() { printf "%s" "$HOME/.local/bin/conda.sh"; }

_lv_alias_edit_file() {
  [[ -n "${DISTROHOME:-}" && -n "${DISTRONAME:-}" ]] || { echo ""; return 1; }
  printf "%s" "$HOME/.local/bin/conda.sh"
}

_lv_alias_cmd_for_env() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2"
  case "$kind" in
    conda) printf "conda activate %s" "$name" ;;
    venv)  printf 'source "${WORKON_HOME:-$HOME/.virtualenvs}/%s/bin/activate"' "$name" ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

_lv_alias_set() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2"
  local file header cmd alias_line tmp

  file="$(_lv_alias_edit_file)"
  header="# ${kind} aliases"
  cmd="$(_lv_alias_cmd_for_env "$kind" "$name")" || { echo "Unknown kind: $kind"; return 1; }
  alias_line="alias ${name}='${cmd}'"

  [[ -n "$file" && -f "$file" ]] || { echo "Alias target file not found."; return 1; }
  tmp="$(mktemp)" || { echo "Could not create temp file."; return 1; }

  awk -v header="$header" -v name="$name" -v alias_line="$alias_line" '
    BEGIN { in_block=0; found_header=0; added=0; exists=0; }
    {
      if ($0 == header) { found_header=1; in_block=1; print $0; next; }
      if (in_block) {
        if ($0 ~ /^#[[:space:]]+/) {
          if (!exists && !added) { print alias_line; added=1; }
          in_block=0; print $0; next;
        }
        if ($0 ~ ("^[[:space:]]*alias[[:space:]]+" name "=")) exists=1;
        print $0; next;
      }
      print $0;
    }
    END {
      if (!found_header) {
        print ""; print header; print alias_line;
      } else if (in_block && !exists && !added) {
        print alias_line;
      }
    }
  ' "$file" > "$tmp" || { rm -f "$tmp"; echo "Failed to prepare update."; return 1; }

  if cat "$tmp" > "$file"; then
    rm -f "$tmp"
    echo "Alias added/kept: $name -> $cmd"
  else
    rm -f "$tmp"
    echo "Permission denied writing alias target."
    return 1
  fi
}

_lv_alias_unset() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2" file tmp
  file="$(_lv_alias_edit_file)"
  [[ -n "$file" && -f "$file" ]] || { echo "Alias target file not found."; return 1; }

  tmp="$(mktemp)" || { echo "Could not create temp file."; return 1; }
  awk -v name="$name" '
    $0 ~ ("^[[:space:]]*alias[[:space:]]+" name "=") { next }
    { print $0 }
  ' "$file" > "$tmp" || { rm -f "$tmp"; echo "Failed to prepare update."; return 1; }

  if cat "$tmp" > "$file"; then
    rm -f "$tmp"
    echo "Alias removed (if existed): $name"
  else
    rm -f "$tmp"
    echo "Permission denied writing alias target."
    return 1
  fi
}

_lv_clone_preflight() {
  local src_kind="$1" src_name="$2" tgt_kind="$3" tgt_name="$4"
  local src_vpath="" tgt_vpath="" w=""

  command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "Missing python3. Install python3 first."; return 1; }

  if [[ "$src_kind" == "conda" || "$tgt_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
    command -v conda >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
      echo "Conda required for conda source/target. Install/initialize conda first."
      return 1
    }
  fi

  if [[ "$src_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
    [[ -n "$(_lv_find_conda_path "$src_name")" ]] || { echo "Source conda env not found: $src_name"; return 1; }
    conda run -n "$src_name" python -V >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "Source conda env python not usable: $src_name"; return 1; }
    conda run -n "$src_name" python -m pip --version >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
      echo "Source conda env missing pip: $src_name"
      echo "Run: conda run -n \"$src_name\" python -m ensurepip --upgrade"
      return 1
    }
  else
    src_vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$src_name")"
    [[ -n "$src_vpath" && -x "$src_vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Source venv python missing: $src_name"; return 1; }
    "$src_vpath/bin/python" -m pip --version >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
      echo "Source venv missing pip: $src_name"
      echo "Run: \"$src_vpath/bin/python\" -m ensurepip --upgrade"
      return 1
    }
  fi

  if [[ "$tgt_kind" == "venv" ]]; then
    w="$(_lv_workon_home)"
    mkdir -p "$w" >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "Cannot create WORKON_HOME: $w"; return 1; }

    tgt_vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$tgt_name")"
    if [[ -n "$tgt_vpath" && -x "$tgt_vpath/bin/python" ]]; then
      "$tgt_vpath/bin/python" -m pip --version >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        echo "Target venv exists but pip missing: $tgt_name"
        echo "Install prerequisites: sudo apt install -y python3-venv python3-pip"
        echo "Then recreate: rm -rf \"$tgt_vpath\" && python3 -m venv \"$tgt_vpath\""
        return 1
      }
    fi
  else
    if [[ -n "$(_lv_find_conda_path "$tgt_name")" ]]; then
      conda run -n "$tgt_name" python -V >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "Target conda env python not usable: $tgt_name"; return 1; }
      conda run -n "$tgt_name" python -m pip --version >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        echo "Target conda env missing pip: $tgt_name"
        echo "Run: conda run -n \"$tgt_name\" python -m ensurepip --upgrade"
        return 1
      }
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

_lv_clone_write_manifests() {
  local src_kind="$1" src_name="$2" tgt_kind="$3" tgt_name="$4" pyver="$5" wd="$6"
  shift 6
  local pkgs=("$@")

  local req="$wd/requirements.txt"
  local yml="$wd/environment.yml"
  : > "$req" || return 1

  local p pip_ver conda_ver
  local -a conda_specs=() pip_specs=()

  for p in "${pkgs[@]}"; do
    p="${p#-}"
    [[ "$p" == "jupyther" ]] && p="jupyter"
    [[ "$p" == "python" ]] && continue
    if [[ "$p" == "pip" ]]; then
      pip_specs+=("pip")
      continue
    fi
    [[ -z "$p" ]] && continue

    pip_ver=""
    conda_ver=""

    if [[ "$src_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
      conda_ver="$(conda list -n "$src_name" "$p" 2>/dev/null | awk 'NF && $1 !~ /^#/ && $1==pkg {print $2; exit}' pkg="$p")"
      pip_ver="$(conda run -n "$src_name" python -m pip show "$p" 2>/dev/null | awk -F': ' '/^Version:/{print $2; exit}')"
    else
      local sv="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$src_name")"
      [[ -n "$sv" && -x "$sv/bin/python" ]] || return 1
      pip_ver="$("$sv/bin/python" -m pip show "$p" 2>/dev/null | awk -F': ' '/^Version:/{print $2; exit}')"
    fi

    if [[ -n "$conda_ver" ]]; then
      conda_specs+=("$p=$conda_ver")
      pip_specs+=("$p==$conda_ver")
    elif [[ -n "$pip_ver" ]]; then
      pip_specs+=("$p==$pip_ver")
    else
      pip_specs+=("$p")
    fi
  done

  printf "%s\n" "${pip_specs[@]}" > "$req"

  {
    echo "name: $tgt_name"
    echo "dependencies:"
    echo "  - python=$pyver"
    echo "  - pip"
    for p in "${conda_specs[@]}"; do
      echo "  - $p"
    done
    if ((${#pip_specs[@]})); then
      echo "  - pip:"
      for p in "${pip_specs[@]}"; do
        echo "    - $p"
      done
    fi
  } > "$yml" || return 1

  printf '%s|%s\n' "$req" "$yml"
}

_lv_clone() {
  local src_kind="$1" src_name="$2"
  shift 2

  local tgt_kind="" tgt_name=""
  local -a req_pkgs=()
  local src_py="3.11"
  local src_vpath="" tgt_vpath="" workon_home=""
  local i

  [[ "$src_kind" =~ ^(conda|venv)$ ]] || { echo "Source kind must be conda|venv"; return 1; }
  [[ -n "$src_name" ]] || { echo "Source name is required."; return 1; }
  [[ "${1:-}" == "--clone" ]] || {
    echo "Usage: lv <env> <name> --clone --target <env> <name> --packages -python -numpy ..."
    return 1
  }
  shift

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --target)
        tgt_kind="$2"
        tgt_name="$3"
        shift 3
        ;;
      --packages)
        shift
        while (($#)); do
          [[ "$1" == --* ]] && break
          req_pkgs+=("${1#-}")
          shift
        done
        ;;
      *)
        shift
        ;;
    esac
  done

  [[ "$tgt_kind" =~ ^(conda|venv)$ ]] || { echo "Target kind must be conda|venv"; return 1; }
  [[ -n "$tgt_name" ]] || { echo "Missing target env name."; return 1; }

  _lv_clone_preflight "$src_kind" "$src_name" "$tgt_kind" "$tgt_name" || return 1

  if [[ "$src_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
    src_py="$(conda run -n "$src_name" python -c 'import sys; print(f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}")' 2>/dev/null)"
  else
    src_vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$src_name")"
    [[ -n "$src_vpath" && -x "$src_vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Source venv python not found: $src_name"; return 1; }
    src_py="$("$src_vpath/bin/python" -c 'import sys; print(f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}")' 2>/dev/null)"
  fi
  [[ -n "$src_py" ]] || src_py="3.11"

  if [[ "$tgt_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
    [[ -n "$(_lv_find_conda_path "$tgt_name")" ]] || {
      conda create -n "$tgt_name" "python=$src_py" -y || { echo "Failed creating target conda env: $tgt_name"; return 1; }
    }
  else
    workon_home="$(_lv_workon_home)"
    mkdir -p "$workon_home" || { echo "Cannot create WORKON_HOME: $workon_home"; return 1; }
    tgt_vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$tgt_name")"
    if [[ -z "$tgt_vpath" ]]; then
      python3 -m venv "$workon_home/$tgt_name" || { echo "Failed creating target venv: $workon_home/$tgt_name"; return 1; }
      tgt_vpath="$workon_home/$tgt_name"
    fi
    [[ -x "$tgt_vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Target venv python missing: $tgt_vpath/bin/python"; return 1; }
  fi

  for i in "${!req_pkgs[@]}"; do
    [[ "${req_pkgs[$i]}" == "jupyther" ]] && req_pkgs[$i]="jupyter"
  done

  local wd out req yml tv
  wd="$(mktemp -d)" || { echo "Failed to create temp workdir."; return 1; }

  out="$(_lv_clone_write_manifests "$src_kind" "$src_name" "$tgt_kind" "$tgt_name" "$src_py" "$wd" "${req_pkgs[@]}" | tail -n 1)"
  req="${out%%|*}"
  yml="${out##*|}"

  if [[ -z "$req" || -z "$yml" || ! -f "$req" || ! -f "$yml" ]]; then
    rm -rf "$wd"
    echo "Failed to generate manifest files."
    return 1
  fi

  echo "Generated manifests:"
  echo "  requirements: $req"
  echo "  environment:  $yml"

  if [[ "$tgt_kind" == "conda" ]]; then
    conda env update -n "$tgt_name" -f "$yml" || {
      rm -rf "$wd"
      echo "conda env update failed."
      return 1
    }
  else
    tv="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$tgt_name")"
    [[ -n "$tv" && -x "$tv/bin/python" ]] || {
      rm -rf "$wd"
      echo "Target venv python not found: $tgt_name"
      return 1
    }

    _lv_pip_install_safe "$tv/bin/python" "$req" || {
      rm -rf "$wd"
      echo "pip install from requirements failed."
      return 1
    }
  fi

  rm -rf "$wd"
  echo "Clone completed: $src_kind/$src_name -> $tgt_kind/$tgt_name"
  _lv_post_clone_update_prompt "$tgt_kind" "$tgt_name"
}

_lv_post_clone_update_prompt() {
  local kind="$1" name="$2" vpath ans pkg_line
  local -a pkgs

  echo
  echo "Clone completed: $kind/$name"
  read -r -p "Post-clone update? [s]pecific packages / [o]utdated (venv) / [a]ll / [n]one: " ans

  case "${ans,,}" in
    s|specific)
      read -r -p "Enter packages (space-separated): " pkg_line
      [[ -z "$pkg_line" ]] && { echo "No packages provided. Skipping."; return 0; }
      read -r -a pkgs <<< "$pkg_line"

      if [[ "$kind" == "conda" ]]; then
        conda update -n "$name" -y "${pkgs[@]}" || {
          echo "Conda update failed, trying pip in conda env..."
          conda run -n "$name" python -m pip install -U "${pkgs[@]}"
        }
      else
        vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$name")"
        [[ -n "$vpath" && -x "$vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Venv not found: $name"; return 1; }
        "$vpath/bin/python" -m pip install -U "${pkgs[@]}"
      fi
      ;;
    o|outdated)
      if [[ "$kind" != "venv" ]]; then
        echo "Outdated-only flow is for venv. For conda use [a]ll or [s]pecific."
        return 0
      fi
      vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$name")"
      [[ -n "$vpath" && -x "$vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Venv not found: $name"; return 1; }
      "$vpath/bin/python" -m pip list --outdated --format=freeze | cut -d= -f1 | xargs -r "$vpath/bin/python" -m pip install -U
      ;;
    a|all)
      if [[ "$kind" == "conda" ]]; then
        conda update -n "$name" --all -y
      else
        vpath="$(_lv_find_venv_path "$name")"
        [[ -n "$vpath" && -x "$vpath/bin/python" ]] || { echo "Venv not found: $name"; return 1; }
        "$vpath/bin/python" -m pip list --outdated --format=freeze | cut -d= -f1 | xargs -r "$vpath/bin/python" -m pip install -U
      fi
      ;;
    ""|n|no|none)
      echo "Skipped post-clone update."
      ;;
    *)
      echo "Unknown option. Skipped post-clone update."
      ;;
  esac
}

_lv_pip_install_safe() {
  local py="$1" req="$2"
  local spec
  local -a specs=()

  [[ -x "$py" ]] || { echo "Python not executable: $py"; return 1; }
  [[ -f "$req" ]] || { echo "Requirements file not found: $req"; return 1; }

  mapfile -t specs < <(grep -E -v '^[[:space:]]*($|#)' "$req")

  for spec in "${specs[@]}"; do
    echo "Installing: $spec"
    "$py" -m pip install --progress-bar off "$spec" || {
      echo "Failed package: $spec"
      return 1
    }
  done
}

lv() {
  case "${1:-}" in
    -help|--help|-h ) _lv_help; return ;;
  esac

  if [[ -n "$1" ]]; then
    case "$1" in
      alias|conda|venv)
        if [[ -z "${2:-}" ]]; then
          echo "Usage: lv {alias|conda|venv} <name> [--clone --target <conda|venv> <name> --packages -python -pip <-pkg1 -pkg2 ...> | -new | -del]"
          return
        fi

        if [[ "${3:-}" == "--clone" ]]; then
          _lv_clone "$@"
        elif [[ "${3:-}" == "-new" || "${3:-}" == "--new" ]]; then
          _lv_create_new "$1" "$2"
        elif [[ "${3:-}" == "-del" || "${3:-}" == "--del" ]]; then
          _lv_delete "$1" "$2"
        else
          _lv_inspect "$1" "$2"
        fi
        ;;
      *)
        echo "Please specify focus: lv {alias|conda|venv} <name> [--clone --target <conda|venv> <name> --packages -python -pip <-pkg1 -pkg2 ...> | -new | -del]"
        ;;
    esac
    return
  fi

  local ip cb cb_short wb wb_short ab sep tablew
  local WA=16 WB=14 WC=10 WD=22 WE=10 WF=26
  local i max
  local kind a b c d e f
  local -a alias_rows conda_rows venv_rows

  mapfile -t alias_rows < <(_lv_capture_alias_rows)
  mapfile -t conda_rows < <(_lv_capture_conda_rows)
  mapfile -t venv_rows  < <(_lv_capture_venv_rows)

  max=${#alias_rows[@]}
  (( ${#conda_rows[@]} > max )) && max=${#conda_rows[@]}
  (( ${#venv_rows[@]}  > max )) && max=${#venv_rows[@]}

  ip="$(_lv_ip)"
  cb="$(_lv_conda_base)"
  wb="$(_lv_workon_home)"
  ab="$(_lv_alias_base)"

  tablew=$((WA+1+WB+3+WC+1+WD+3+WE+1+WF))
  sep="$(printf '%*s' "$tablew" '' | tr ' ' '-')"

  printf "Distro: ${_lv_bold}${_lv_yellow}%s${_lv_reset}   IP: ${_lv_bold}${_lv_yellow}@%s${_lv_reset}\n" "${DISTRONAME:-n/a}" "${ip:-n/a}"
  if [[ -n "$cb" ]]; then
    cb_short="${cb/#$HOME/~}"
    printf "%bConda base: %s (%s)%b\n" "${_lv_gray}" "$cb" "$cb_short" "${_lv_reset}"
  fi
  if [[ -n "$wb" ]]; then
    wb_short="${wb/#$HOME/~}"
    printf "%bVenv base:  %s (%s)%b\n" "${_lv_gray}" "$wb" "$wb_short" "${_lv_reset}"
  fi
  if [[ -n "$ab" ]]; then
    printf "%bAlias base: %s%b\n" "${_lv_gray}" "$ab" "${_lv_reset}"
  fi

  printf "${_lv_gray}%-${WA}s %-${WB}s | %-${WC}s %-${WD}s | %-${WE}s %-${WF}s${_lv_reset}\n" "ALIAS" "ACTION" "CONDA" "PATH" "VENV" "PATH"
  printf "%s\n" "$sep"

  for ((i=0; i<max; i++)); do
    kind="data"; a=""; b=""; c=""; d=""; e=""; f=""

    if (( i < ${#alias_rows[@]} )); then
      IFS=$'\t' read -r kind a b <<< "${alias_rows[i]}"
    fi
    if (( i < ${#conda_rows[@]} )); then
      IFS=$'\t' read -r c d <<< "${conda_rows[i]}"
    fi
    if (( i < ${#venv_rows[@]} )); then
      IFS=$'\t' read -r e f <<< "${venv_rows[i]}"
    fi

    d="$(_lv_shortpath "$d" "$WD")"
    f="$(_lv_shortpath "$f" "$WF")"

    if [[ "$kind" == "sep" ]]; then
      printf "%b%-${WA}s %-${WB}s${_lv_reset} | ${_lv_green}%-${WC}s %-${WD}s${_lv_reset} | ${_lv_cyan}%-${WE}s %-${WF}s${_lv_reset}\n" \
        "${_lv_gray}" "$(_lv_clip "# $a" "$WA")" "" \
        "$(_lv_clip "$c" "$WC")" "$(_lv_clip "$d" "$WD")" \
        "$(_lv_clip "$e" "$WE")" "$(_lv_clip "$f" "$WF")"
    else
      printf "%-${WA}s %-${WB}s | ${_lv_green}%-${WC}s %-${WD}s${_lv_reset} | ${_lv_cyan}%-${WE}s %-${WF}s${_lv_reset}\n" \
        "$(_lv_clip "$a" "$WA")" "$(_lv_clip "$b" "$WB")" \
        "$(_lv_clip "$c" "$WC")" "$(_lv_clip "$d" "$WD")" \
        "$(_lv_clip "$e" "$WE")" "$(_lv_clip "$f" "$WF")"
    fi
  done

  printf "%s\n" "$sep"
  echo "Try: lv -help | lv alias <name> | lv conda <name> | lv venv <name> | lv venv <name> -new | lv conda <name> -del"
  printf "%s\n" "$sep"
}

if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
  lv "$@"
fi

Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/bin/mounts.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Mount/fstab/SMB workflow. Safe to source.

CURRENT_HOME="${CURRENT_HOME:-${TARGET_HOME:-${HOME:-$(getent passwd "$(whoami)" | cut -d: -f6)}}}"
BASEDIR="${BASEDIR:-${CURRENT_HOME}/.local/wsl-mounts}"
ENVFILE="$BASEDIR/mount-env"
FSTAB_TEMPLATE="$BASEDIR/fstab.wsltmpl"
FSTAB_RENDERED="$BASEDIR/fstab.wsl"
DISTROHOME="${DISTROHOME:-/mnt/distrohome}"

LOCAL_WSL_USER="${WSL_USER:-${TARGET_USER:-$(whoami)}}"
WSL_USER="$LOCAL_WSL_USER"
SMB_USER="${SMB_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-$(case "$LOCAL_WSL_USER" in vmuser) echo hector ;; labuser) echo labuser ;; *) echo "$LOCAL_WSL_USER" ;; esac)}}"

export CURRENT_HOME BASEDIR WSL_USER SMB_USER DISTROHOME

mounts_refresh_paths() {
  CURRENT_HOME="${CURRENT_HOME:-${TARGET_HOME:-${HOME:-$(getent passwd "$(whoami)" | cut -d: -f6)}}}"
  BASEDIR="${BASEDIR:-${CURRENT_HOME}/.local/wsl-mounts}"
  ENVFILE="$BASEDIR/mount-env"
  FSTAB_TEMPLATE="$BASEDIR/fstab.wsltmpl"
  FSTAB_RENDERED="$BASEDIR/fstab.wsl"
  export CURRENT_HOME BASEDIR ENVFILE FSTAB_TEMPLATE FSTAB_RENDERED DISTROHOME
}

mounts_bool_is_true() {
  case "${1,,}" in
    1|yes|true|on) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

mounts_prompt_yesno() {
  local msg="$1" ans
  while true; do
    read -rp "$msg [y/N]: " ans
    case "$ans" in
      [Yy]*) return 0 ;;
      [Nn]*|'') return 1 ;;
      *) echo "Please answer y or n." ;;
    esac
  done
}

mounts_parse_args() {
  MOUNTS_NON_INTERACTIVE="${MOUNTS_NON_INTERACTIVE:-1}"
  MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING="${MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING:-${MOUNT_SCRIPTING:-0}}"
  MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS="${MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS:-${MOUNT_INGRESS:-0}}"
  MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS="${MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS:-${MOUNT_EGRESS:-0}}"
  MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH="${MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH:-${MOUNT_RESEARCH:-0}}"
  MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH="${MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH:-${MOUNT_PUBLISH:-0}}"
  MOUNTS_SKIP_DISTROHOME="${MOUNTS_SKIP_DISTROHOME:-$(case "${MOUNT_DISTROHOME:-1}" in 0|no|false|off) echo 1 ;; *) echo 0 ;; esac)}"
  MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE="${MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE:-}"

  if mounts_bool_is_true "${MOUNTS_MOUNT_ALL:-0}"; then
    MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING=1
    MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS=1
    MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS=1
    MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH=1
  fi

  if [[ -z "$MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE" ]]; then
    local default_credentials_file
    default_credentials_file="$(mounts_credentials_file_default)"
    if [[ -f "$default_credentials_file" ]]; then
      MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE="$default_credentials_file"
    fi
  fi

  while (($#)); do
    case "$1" in
      --credentials-file|--credentials)
        shift
        [[ -n "${1:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Missing path after --credentials-file" >&2; return 2; }
        MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE="$1"
        ;;
      --no-credentials-file|--no-credentials)
        MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE=""
        ;;
      --scripting)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING=1
        ;;
      --ingress)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS=1
        ;;
      --egress)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS=1
        ;;
      --research)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH=1
        ;;
      --publish)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH=1
        ;;
      --all)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING=1
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS=1
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS=1
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH=1
        ;;
      --skip-distrohome)
        MOUNTS_SKIP_DISTROHOME=1
        ;;
      --no-scripting)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING=0
        ;;
      --no-ingress)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS=0
        ;;
      --no-egress)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS=0
        ;;
      --no-research)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH=0
        ;;
      --no-publish)
        MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH=0
        ;;
      --)
        shift
        break
        ;;
      *)
        echo "[ERROR] Unknown mounts option: $1" >&2
        return 2
        ;;
    esac
    shift
  done

  export MOUNTS_NON_INTERACTIVE MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH MOUNTS_SKIP_DISTROHOME MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE
}

mounts_credentials_file_default() {
  printf '%s' "${CURRENT_HOME}/.local/share/wsl-mounts/cifs.credentials"
}

mounts_validate_credentials_file() {
  local file="$1"
  local perms

  [[ -n "$file" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Empty credentials file path" >&2; return 1; }
  [[ "$file" != *,* ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Credentials file path must not contain commas: $file" >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Credentials file not found: $file" >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -r "$file" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Credentials file is not readable: $file" >&2; return 1; }

  perms="$(stat -c '%a' "$file" 2>/dev/null)" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not read credentials file permissions: $file" >&2
    return 1
  }
  (( 8#$perms & 077 )) && {
    echo "[ERROR] Credentials file is too permissive; run: chmod 600 '$file'" >&2
    return 1
  }

  grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*username=' "$file" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Credentials file missing username= line: $file" >&2
    return 1
  }
  grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*password=' "$file" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Credentials file missing password= line: $file" >&2
    return 1
  }
}

mounts_share_credentials_file() {
  local share="$1"
  local var_name
  local share_file=""
  local default_share_file=""

  case "$share" in
    distrohome) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_DISTROHOME" ;;
    scripting) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_SCRIPTING" ;;
    ingress) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_INGRESS" ;;
    egress) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_EGRESS" ;;
    research) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_RESEARCH" ;;
    publish) var_name="MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_PUBLISH" ;;
    *) var_name="" ;;
  esac

  if [[ -n "$var_name" ]]; then
    # shellcheck disable=SC1083
    share_file="${!var_name:-}"
  fi

  default_share_file="${CURRENT_HOME}/.local/share/wsl-mounts/${share}.credentials"

  if [[ -n "$share_file" ]]; then
    printf '%s' "$share_file"
  elif [[ -f "$default_share_file" ]]; then
    printf '%s' "$default_share_file"
  else
    printf '%s' "$MOUNTS_CREDENTIALS_FILE"
  fi
}

mounts_cifs_auth_option() {
  local fallback_user="$1"
  local credentials_file="${2:-}"

  if [[ -n "$credentials_file" ]]; then
    mounts_validate_credentials_file "$credentials_file" || return 1
    printf 'credentials=%s' "$credentials_file"
  else
    printf 'username=%s' "$fallback_user"
  fi
}

mounts_should_mount_optional() {
  local label="$1"
  local enabled="$2"
  local prompt="$3"
  local enable_hint="$4"

  if mounts_bool_is_true "$enabled"; then
    return 0
  fi

  if mounts_bool_is_true "$MOUNTS_NON_INTERACTIVE" || [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] Skipping $label; enable with $enable_hint"
    return 1
  fi

  mounts_prompt_yesno "$prompt"
}

mounts_runtime_init() {
  mounts_refresh_paths
  mkdir -p "$BASEDIR"
  umask 077
  export DISTROHOME
}

mounts_write_generic_fstab_template() {
  cat > "$FSTAB_TEMPLATE" <<'EOF'
# Generic WSL/DrvFs and bind mounts template (user-local)
# Drive mounts (drvfs)
# C: /c drvfs,uid=CURRENT_UID,gid=CURRENT_GID,umask=677,fmask=277 0 0
# V: /mnt/v drvfs metadata,uid=CURRENT_UID,gid=CURRENT_GID,umask=677,fmask=277 0 0

# Bind mount example for VSCodium (use WSL_USER placeholder)
#/c/Users/WSL_USER/AppData/Local/Programs/VSCodium /opt/vscodium none bind 0 0
EOF
  chmod 600 "$FSTAB_TEMPLATE"
  echo "Wrote template to $FSTAB_TEMPLATE"
}

mounts_write_current_envfile() {
  local uid gid
  uid=$(id -u "$WSL_USER" 2>/dev/null || id -u)
  gid=$(id -g "$WSL_USER" 2>/dev/null || id -g)

  local DISTRO_RAW
  DISTRO_RAW=$(awk -F= '/^NAME=/{print $2}' /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null || true)
  DISTRO_RAW=${DISTRO_RAW//\"/}
  DISTRONAME="${DISTRONAME:-${DISTRO_RAW:-wsl}}"
  DISTRONAME="$(echo "$DISTRONAME" | sed 's/[\/\\ ]/_/g')"

  cat > "$ENVFILE" <<EOM
# Auto-generated mount environment (user-local)
WSL_USER='$WSL_USER'
CURRENT_UID='$uid'
CURRENT_GID='$gid'
DISTRONAME='$DISTRONAME'
EOM
  chmod 600 "$ENVFILE"
  echo "Wrote env to $ENVFILE"
}

mounts_render_fstab_from_template() {
  if [[ ! -f "$FSTAB_TEMPLATE" ]]; then
    echo "Template missing: $FSTAB_TEMPLATE" >&2
    return 1
  fi

  # shellcheck disable=SC1090
  source "$ENVFILE"

  sed -e "s|CURRENT_UID|$CURRENT_UID|g" \
      -e "s|CURRENT_GID|$CURRENT_GID|g" \
      -e "s|WSL_USER|$WSL_USER|g" \
      -e "s|DISTRONAME|$DISTRONAME|g" \
      "$FSTAB_TEMPLATE" > "$FSTAB_RENDERED"

  chmod 600 "$FSTAB_RENDERED"
  echo "Rendered fstab to $FSTAB_RENDERED"
}

mounts_apply_rendered_fstab() {
  if [[ ! -f "$FSTAB_RENDERED" ]]; then
    echo "Rendered fstab missing: $FSTAB_RENDERED" >&2
    return 1
  fi

  grep -E -v '^\s*#|^\s*$' "$FSTAB_RENDERED" | while read -r src dst fstype opts dump pass; do
    [[ -n "$dst" ]] || continue

    mkdir -p "$dst" || echo "mkdir failed for $dst"
    sudo umount "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true

    case "$fstype" in
      drvfs)
        sudo mount -t drvfs "$src" "$dst" -o "$opts" || echo "drvfs mount failed: $src -> $dst"
        ;;
      none)
        sudo mount --bind "$src" "$dst" || echo "bind mount failed: $src -> $dst"
        ;;
      cifs|smb|smbfs)
        sudo mount -t cifs "$src" "$dst" -o "$opts" || echo "cifs mount failed: $src -> $dst"
        ;;
      *)
        sudo mount -t "$fstype" "$src" "$dst" -o "$opts" || echo "mount failed: $src -> $dst (fstype=$fstype)"
        ;;
    esac
  done
}

mounts_cleanup_artifacts() {
  rm -f "$FSTAB_TEMPLATE" "$FSTAB_RENDERED" "$ENVFILE"
  rmdir --ignore-fail-on-non-empty "$BASEDIR" 2>/dev/null || true
  echo "Cleaned up artifacts in $BASEDIR"
}

mounts_ensure_shared_group() {
  local shared_group="${SHARED_MOUNT_GROUP:-wslshare}"
  sudo groupadd -f "$shared_group"
  id -nG vmuser 2>/dev/null | grep -qw "$shared_group" || sudo usermod -aG "$shared_group" vmuser
  id -nG "${TARGET_USER}" 2>/dev/null | grep -qw "$shared_group" || sudo usermod -aG "$shared_group" "${TARGET_USER}"
}

mounts_mount_smb_folders() {
  local shared_group="${SHARED_MOUNT_GROUP:-wslshare}"
  local shared_gid
  shared_gid="$(getent group "$shared_group" | cut -d: -f3)"
  [[ -n "$shared_gid" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Shared group '$shared_group' does not exist."; return 1; }
  local SMB_USER_MAIN="${SMB_USER}"
  local SMB_USER_INGRESS="${SMB_USER_INGRESS:-$SMB_USER_MAIN}"
  local SMB_USER_EGRESS="${SMB_USER_EGRESS:-$SMB_USER_MAIN}"
  local uid gid
  uid=$(id -u "$WSL_USER" 2>/dev/null || id -u)
  gid=$(id -g "$WSL_USER" 2>/dev/null || id -g)

  local distrohome_mount=/mnt/distrohome
  local ingress_mount=/mnt/ingress
  local egress_mount=/mnt/egress
  local mount_scripting=/mnt/scripting
  local research_mount=/mnt/research
  local publish_mount=/mnt/publish

  local src_distrohome="//172.27.240.1/distrohome/${DISTRONAME}"
  local src_ingress="//172.27.240.1/ingress"
  local src_egress="//172.27.240.1/egress"
  local src_scripting="//172.27.240.1/scripting"
  local src_research="//172.27.240.1/research"
  local src_publish="//172.27.240.1/publish"

  ensure_dir() {
    local d="$1"
    [[ -d "$d" ]] || sudo mkdir -p "$d"
  }

  is_mounted() { mountpoint -q "$1"; }

  mount_cifs_opts() {
    local src="$1" dst="$2" extra="$3" fmode="$4" dmode="$5" smb_user="${6:-$SMB_USER}" credentials_file="${7:-}" mount_uid="${8:-$uid}" mount_gid="${9:-$shared_gid}"
    local auth_opt

    auth_opt="$(mounts_cifs_auth_option "$smb_user" "$credentials_file")" || return 1
    sudo mount -t cifs "$src" "$dst" -o "$auth_opt",uid=$mount_uid,gid=$mount_gid,$extra,file_mode=$fmode,dir_mode=$dmode,vers=3.0,sec=ntlmssp
  }

  research_mount_ids() {
    local owner_user="${RESEARCH_MOUNT_OWNER_USER:-researchscientist}"
    local group_name="${RESEARCH_MOUNT_GROUP:-aiengineer}"
    local owner_uid group_gid

    owner_uid="$(id -u "$owner_user" 2>/dev/null)" || {
      echo "[ERROR] Research mount owner user '$owner_user' does not exist." >&2
      return 1
    }
    group_gid="$(getent group "$group_name" | cut -d: -f3)"
    [[ -n "$group_gid" ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] Research mount group '$group_name' does not exist." >&2
      return 1
    }

    printf '%s:%s' "$owner_uid" "$group_gid"
  }

  mount_optional_share() {
    local label="$1" dst="$2" src="$3" fmode="$4" dmode="$5" smb_user="$6" enable_hint="$7" prompt="$8" credentials_file="${10:-}" mount_uid="${11:-$uid}" mount_gid="${12:-$shared_gid}"

    if mounts_should_mount_optional "$label" "${9}" "$prompt" "$enable_hint"; then
      if is_mounted "$dst"; then
        echo "$label already mounted at $dst, remounting ..."
        sudo umount "$dst"
      elif ! ls -ld "$dst" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "$label mountpoint at $dst is stale, cleaning up ..."
        sudo umount "$dst" 2>/dev/null || sudo umount -l "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true
      fi
      ensure_dir "$dst"
      mount_cifs_opts "$src" "$dst" "" "$fmode" "$dmode" "$smb_user" "$credentials_file" "$mount_uid" "$mount_gid" || echo "Failed to mount $dst"
    elif ! ls -ld "$dst" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "$label mountpoint at $dst is stale, cleaning up skipped mountpoint ..."
      sudo umount "$dst" 2>/dev/null || sudo umount -l "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
  }

  mount_research_share() {
    local label="research"
    local dst="$research_mount"
    local src="$src_research"
    local fmode="0770"
    local dmode="0770"
    local smb_user="$SMB_USER_RESEARCH"
    local enable_hint="--research or MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH=1"
    local prompt="Mount research at $research_mount with owner=researchscientist, group=aiengineer, mode=0770?"
    local enabled="$MOUNTS_MOUNT_RESEARCH"
    local credentials_file
    local research_owner_group research_uid research_gid

    if mounts_should_mount_optional "$label" "$enabled" "$prompt" "$enable_hint"; then
      research_owner_group="$(research_mount_ids)" || return 1
      research_uid="${research_owner_group%%:*}"
      research_gid="${research_owner_group##*:}"
      credentials_file="$(mounts_share_credentials_file research)"

      if is_mounted "$dst"; then
        echo "$label already mounted at $dst, remounting ..."
        sudo umount "$dst"
      elif ! ls -ld "$dst" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "$label mountpoint at $dst is stale, cleaning up ..."
        sudo umount "$dst" 2>/dev/null || sudo umount -l "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true
      fi
      ensure_dir "$dst"
      mount_cifs_opts "$src" "$dst" "" "$fmode" "$dmode" "$smb_user" "$credentials_file" "$research_uid" "$research_gid" || echo "Failed to mount $dst"
    elif ! ls -ld "$dst" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "$label mountpoint at $dst is stale, cleaning up skipped mountpoint ..."
      sudo umount "$dst" 2>/dev/null || sudo umount -l "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
  }

  if mounts_bool_is_true "$MOUNTS_SKIP_DISTROHOME"; then
    echo "Skipping distrohome"
  else
    local distrohome_credentials
    distrohome_credentials="$(mounts_share_credentials_file distrohome)"
    ensure_dir "$distrohome_mount"
    if is_mounted "$distrohome_mount"; then
      echo "distrohome already mounted at $distrohome_mount, remounting ..."
      sudo umount "$distrohome_mount"
      mount_cifs_opts "$src_distrohome" "$distrohome_mount" "" "0660" "0770" "$SMB_USER_MAIN" "$distrohome_credentials" || echo "Failed to re-mount $distrohome_mount"
    else
      mount_cifs_opts "$src_distrohome" "$distrohome_mount" "" "0660" "0770" "$SMB_USER_MAIN" "$distrohome_credentials" || echo "Failed to mount $distrohome_mount"
    fi
  fi

  mount_optional_share "scripting" "$mount_scripting" "$src_scripting" "0770" "0770" "$SMB_USER_MAIN" "--scripting or MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING=1" "Mount scripting at $mount_scripting with owner=rwx,group=rwx,others=---?" "$MOUNTS_MOUNT_SCRIPTING" "$(mounts_share_credentials_file scripting)"
  mount_optional_share "ingress" "$ingress_mount" "$src_ingress" "0440" "0550" "$SMB_USER_INGRESS" "--ingress or MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS=1" "Mount ingress at $ingress_mount as owner=read-only, no group/other access?" "$MOUNTS_MOUNT_INGRESS" "$(mounts_share_credentials_file ingress)"
  mount_optional_share "egress" "$egress_mount" "$src_egress" "0220" "0330" "$SMB_USER_EGRESS" "--egress or MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS=1" "Mount egress at $egress_mount as owner=write-only, no group/other access?" "$MOUNTS_MOUNT_EGRESS" "$(mounts_share_credentials_file egress)"
  mount_research_share
  mount_optional_share "publish" "$publish_mount" "$src_publish" "0770" "0770" "$SMB_USER_PUBLISH" "--publish or MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH=1" "Mount publish at $publish_mount as owner,group=full, no other access?" "$MOUNTS_MOUNT_PUBLISH" "$(mounts_share_credentials_file publish)"

  echo "[INFO] All mounts processed. Verify with: ls -ld $distrohome_mount $mount_scripting $ingress_mount $egress_mount $research_mount $publish_mount"
}

mounts_init_session_vars() {
  DISTRONAME="${DISTRONAME:-${MOUNTS_DISTRONAME_DEFAULT:-jepabio-Ubuntu-22.04}}"

  WSL_USER="${WSL_USER:-${TARGET_USER:-$(id -un)}}"

  case "$WSL_USER" in
    vmuser) SMB_USER="${SMB_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-hector}}" ;;
    labuser) SMB_USER="${SMB_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-labuser}}" ;;
    *) SMB_USER="${SMB_USER:-${SMB_USER_DEFAULT:-$WSL_USER}}" ;;
  esac

  SMB_USER_INGRESS="${SMB_USER_INGRESS:-${SMB_USER_INGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
  SMB_USER_EGRESS="${SMB_USER_EGRESS:-${SMB_USER_EGRESS_DEFAULT:-labuser}}"
  SMB_USER_RESEARCH="${SMB_USER_RESEARCH:-${SMB_USER_RESEARCH_DEFAULT:-$SMB_USER}}"
  SMB_USER_PUBLISH="${SMB_USER_PUBLISH:-${SMB_USER_PUBLISH_DEFAULT:-$SMB_USER}}"

  export DISTRONAME WSL_USER SMB_USER SMB_USER_INGRESS SMB_USER_EGRESS SMB_USER_RESEARCH SMB_USER_PUBLISH
}

mounts_cleanup_empty_unmounted_mnt() {
  local d
  [[ -d /mnt ]] || {
    echo "[WARN] /mnt does not exist."
    return 0
  }

  for d in /mnt/*; do
    [[ -e "$d" ]] || continue
    [[ -d "$d" ]] || continue

    if mountpoint -q "$d"; then
      echo "[SKIP] Mounted: $d"
      continue
    fi

    if sudo rmdir "$d" 2>/dev/null; then
      echo "[REMOVED] Empty and unmounted: $d"
    else
      echo "[SKIP] Not empty or not removable: $d"
    fi
  done
}

mounts_run() {
  mounts_parse_args "$@" || return $?
  mounts_runtime_init
  mounts_cleanup_empty_unmounted_mnt
  mounts_ensure_shared_group
  cd "$HOME" || return 1
  mounts_init_session_vars
  mounts_write_generic_fstab_template
  mounts_write_current_envfile
  mounts_render_fstab_from_template
  mounts_apply_rendered_fstab
  mounts_cleanup_artifacts
  mounts_mount_smb_folders

  echo "Done."
  echo "Session variables:"
  echo "[INFO] DISTROHOME=$DISTROHOME"
  echo "[INFO] DISTRONAME=$DISTRONAME"
  echo "[INFO] WSL_USER=$WSL_USER"
  echo "[INFO] SMB_USER=$SMB_USER"
  echo "[INFO] SMB_USER_INGRESS=$SMB_USER_INGRESS"
  echo "[INFO] SMB_USER_EGRESS=$SMB_USER_EGRESS"
  echo "[INFO] SMB_USER_RESEARCH=$SMB_USER_RESEARCH"
  echo "[INFO] SMB_USER_PUBLISH=$SMB_USER_PUBLISH"
}

mounts_usage() {
  cat <<'USAGE'
Usage: config mount [help] [options]

Notes:
  --target USER selects the Linux account being configured.
  mount manages WSL/session mount points and SMB identity.
  Use MOUNTS_* and SMB_USER_* overrides for mount credentials/identity.
  The effective mount context is printed before mount work starts.
  Operator profile defaults to all supported mounts.
  Target profile defaults to ingress and egress only unless a role profile enables research or publish.

Commands:
  help      Show this help

Options:
  --credentials-file PATH  Use it only for a non-default or one-off credentials file.
  --credentials PATH       Same as --credentials-file
  --no-credentials-file    Disable credentials-file auth and use username-only fallback
  --scripting        Mount /mnt/scripting
  --ingress          Mount /mnt/ingress
  --egress           Mount /mnt/egress
  --research         Mount /mnt/research
  --publish          Mount /mnt/publish
  --all              Mount scripting, ingress, egress, and publish
  --skip-distrohome  Skip /mnt/distrohome
  --no-scripting     Force skip /mnt/scripting
  --no-ingress       Force skip /mnt/ingress
  --no-egress        Force skip /mnt/egress
  --no-research      Force skip /mnt/research
  --no-publish       Force skip /mnt/publish

Create Credential files:
  "$HOME/.local/bin/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh" help        Show setup and usage.
  "$HOME/.local/bin/create-cifs-credentials-files.sh" per-share   Create/update per-share files.
    clear

Profile-aware workflow:
  config --target USER config-show
  sudo config --target USER credentials per-share
  sudo config --target USER mount
  Research mount stays manual unless the profile is AIEngineer or ResearchScientist.
  When mounted, research uses owner/group `researchscientist:aiengineer` and mode `0770`.

Examples:
  config mount                               Mount /mnt/distrohome and skip optional shares.
  config mount --all                         Mount /mnt/distrohome plus scripting, ingress, egress, and publish.
  config mount --research --no-publish       Mount research and skip publish.
  config mount --skip-distrohome --ingress   Skip distrohome and mount ingress only.
    
USAGE
}

if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
  cmd="${1:-run}"
  shift || true
  case "$cmd" in
    run|mount|mounts) mounts_run "$@" ;;
    help|-h|--help) mounts_usage ;;
    *) echo "[ERROR] Unknown command: $cmd" >&2; mounts_usage >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
fi

Overview for /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/smb-profiles.tsv:

Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/smb-profiles.tsv:

#
# columns:

#########################################
=== START OF ANALYSIS FOR /home/vmuser/.local/labuser/fix-crlf.sh ===
#########################################
Overview for /home/vmuser/.local/labuser/fix-crlf.sh:
This file is a Bourne-Again shell script, ASCII text executable with a size of 627 bytes.
---
Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/labuser/fix-crlf.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Normalize CRLF to LF for the known labuser shell files.

set -euo pipefail

LABUSER_HOME="${LABUSER_HOME:-/home/labuser}"

FILES=(
  "$LABUSER_HOME/.bashrc"
  "$LABUSER_HOME/.local/bin/env.sh"
  "$LABUSER_HOME/.local/bin/config.sh"
  "$LABUSER_HOME/.local/bin/lab-env.sh"
  "$LABUSER_HOME/.local/bin/ml-env.sh"
)

fix_file() {
  local file="$1"
  if [[ ! -f "$file" ]]; then
    echo "[SKIP] Missing: $file"
    return 0
  fi

  if grep -q $'\r' "$file"; then
    sed -i 's/\r$//' "$file"
    echo "[FIXED] $file"
  else
    echo "[OK] $file"
  fi
}

for file in "${FILES[@]}"; do
  fix_file "$file"
done

=== START OF ANALYSIS FOR /home/vmuser/.local/lib/config-sh/installers.sh ===
#########################################
Overview for /home/vmuser/.local/lib/config-sh/installers.sh:
This file is a Bourne-Again shell script, ASCII text executable, with very long lines (493) with a size of 39950 bytes.
---
Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/lib/config-sh/installers.sh:
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Trusted installer implementation library for config.sh.
#
# This file is source-only. Do not execute it directly.
#
# Responsibilities:
#   - install/update functions called by bootstrap steps
#   - installer-specific package/repo/version helpers
#   - uninstall cleanup behavior used by config rm
#
# Not responsibilities:
#   - CLI parsing
#   - target selection
#   - bootstrap plan/marker state
#   - manifest parsing
#   - arbitrary command execution from config files

DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER="${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER:-Anaconda3-2025.12-2-Linux-x86_64.sh}"
DEVENV_AZURE_REPO_FILE="${DEVENV_AZURE_REPO_FILE:-/etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.sources}"
DEVENV_MS_KEYRING="${DEVENV_MS_KEYRING:-/etc/apt/keyrings/microsoft.gpg}"

resolve_target_python_env_run_target() {
  (($#)) || return 2
  if declare -F run_as_target >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    run_as_target "$@"
  else
    sudo -u "${TARGET_USER}" -H env HOME="${TARGET_HOME}" "$@"
  fi
}

resolve_target_python_env_conda_bin() {
  local target_conda="${TARGET_HOME}/anaconda3/bin/conda"
  if [[ -x "$target_conda" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$target_conda"
  elif command -v conda >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    command -v conda
  else
    return 1
  fi
}

resolve_target_python_env_find_conda_path() {
  local env_name="${1:-}"
  local conda_bin="${2:-}"
  local line name marker path
  local helper_path=""
  local conda_dir=""

  [[ -n "$env_name" && -n "$conda_bin" ]] || return 2

  if declare -F _lv_find_conda_path >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    conda_dir="$(dirname "$conda_bin")"
    helper_path="$(HOME="$TARGET_HOME" PATH="${conda_dir}:$PATH" _lv_find_conda_path "$env_name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [[ -n "$helper_path" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$helper_path"
      return 0
    fi
  fi

  while IFS= read -r line; do
    [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue
    [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    set -- $line
    name="$1"
    if [[ "${2:-}" == "*" ]]; then
      path="${3:-}"
    else
      path="${2:-}"
    fi
    [[ "$name" == "$env_name" ]] || continue
    [[ -n "$path" ]] || continue
    printf '%s\n' "$path"
    return 0
  done < <(resolve_target_python_env_run_target "$conda_bin" info --envs 2>/dev/null)

  return 1
}

resolve_target_python_env_find_venv_path() {
  local env_name="${1:-}"
  local candidate=""
  local workon_home=""
  local helper_path=""

  [[ -n "$env_name" ]] || return 2

  if declare -F _lv_workon_home >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    workon_home="$(HOME="$TARGET_HOME" WORKON_HOME="${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs" _lv_workon_home 2>/dev/null || true)"
  fi

  if declare -F _lv_find_venv_path >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    helper_path="$(HOME="$TARGET_HOME" WORKON_HOME="${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs" _lv_find_venv_path "$env_name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [[ -n "$helper_path" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$helper_path"
      return 0
    fi
  fi

  for candidate in \
    "${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs/${env_name}" \
    "${workon_home:-${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs}/${env_name}" \
    "${TARGET_HOME}/.local/venvs/${env_name}"
  do
    [[ -x "$candidate/bin/python" ]] || continue
    if [[ -f "$candidate/bin/activate" || -f "$candidate/pyvenv.cfg" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$candidate"
      return 0
    fi
  done

  return 1
}

resolve_target_python_env_create_conda() {
  local env_name="${1:-}"
  local conda_bin="${2:-}"

  [[ -n "$env_name" && -n "$conda_bin" ]] || return 2

  echo "[INFO] Creating missing conda env: ${env_name}"
  resolve_target_python_env_run_target "$conda_bin" create -n "$env_name" python=3.11 -y
}

resolve_target_python_env_create_venv() {
  local env_name="${1:-}"
  local workon_home=""
  local venv_path=""

  [[ -n "$env_name" ]] || return 2

  if declare -F _lv_workon_home >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    workon_home="$(HOME="$TARGET_HOME" WORKON_HOME="${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs" _lv_workon_home 2>/dev/null || true)"
  fi
  venv_path="${workon_home:-${TARGET_HOME}/.virtualenvs}/${env_name}"

  echo "[INFO] Creating missing venv: ${venv_path}"
  resolve_target_python_env_run_target mkdir -p "$(dirname "$venv_path")" || return 1
  resolve_target_python_env_run_target python3 -m venv "$venv_path"
}

ResolveTargetPythonEnv() {
  local explicit_env="${1:-}"
  local env_name=""
  local env_manager="${PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER:-auto}"
  local create_missing="${PYTHON_ENV_CREATE_MISSING:-0}"
  local allow_system="${PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM:-0}"
  local resolved_mode=""
  local python_command=""
  local conda_bin=""
  local conda_path=""
  local venv_path=""

  env_name="${explicit_env:-${PYTHON_ENV:-${CONFIG_PYTHON_ENV_DEFAULT:-}}}"
  [[ -n "${TARGET_USER:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] TARGET_USER is not set." >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -n "${TARGET_HOME:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] TARGET_HOME is not set." >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -n "$env_name" ]] || {
    echo "[ERROR] No target Python env was provided." >&2
    echo "[ERROR] Resolution order is: explicit argument > PYTHON_ENV > CONFIG_PYTHON_ENV_DEFAULT" >&2
    return 1
  }

  case "$env_manager" in
    auto|conda|venv) ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unsupported PYTHON_ENV_MANAGER: $env_manager" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac

  if [[ "$allow_system" == "1" && "$env_name" == "system" ]]; then
    resolved_mode="system"
    python_command="python3"
  else
    if [[ "$env_manager" == "auto" || "$env_manager" == "conda" ]]; then
      conda_bin="$(resolve_target_python_env_conda_bin 2>/dev/null || true)"
      if [[ -n "$conda_bin" ]]; then
        conda_path="$(resolve_target_python_env_find_conda_path "$env_name" "$conda_bin" 2>/dev/null || true)"
        if [[ -n "$conda_path" ]]; then
          resolved_mode="conda"
          python_command="$conda_bin run -n $env_name python"
        fi
      fi
    fi

    if [[ -z "$resolved_mode" && ( "$env_manager" == "auto" || "$env_manager" == "venv" ) ]]; then
      venv_path="$(resolve_target_python_env_find_venv_path "$env_name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
      if [[ -n "$venv_path" ]]; then
        resolved_mode="venv"
        python_command="$venv_path/bin/python"
      fi
    fi

    if [[ -z "$resolved_mode" ]]; then
      if [[ "$create_missing" == "1" ]]; then
        if [[ "$env_manager" == "conda" || ( "$env_manager" == "auto" && -n "$conda_bin" ) ]]; then
          [[ -n "$conda_bin" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Conda is required to create env '$env_name', but it was not found." >&2; return 1; }
          resolve_target_python_env_create_conda "$env_name" "$conda_bin" || return 1
          conda_path="$(resolve_target_python_env_find_conda_path "$env_name" "$conda_bin" 2>/dev/null || true)"
          [[ -n "$conda_path" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Created conda env '$env_name' but could not resolve it afterward." >&2; return 1; }
          resolved_mode="conda"
          python_command="$conda_bin run -n $env_name python"
        else
          resolve_target_python_env_create_venv "$env_name" || return 1
          venv_path="$(resolve_target_python_env_find_venv_path "$env_name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
          [[ -n "$venv_path" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Created venv '$env_name' but could not resolve it afterward." >&2; return 1; }
          resolved_mode="venv"
          python_command="$venv_path/bin/python"
        fi
      else
        echo "[ERROR] Target Python env not found: $env_name" >&2
        echo "[ERROR] env_manager=$env_manager create_missing=$create_missing allow_system=$allow_system" >&2
        echo "[ERROR] Refusing to fall back to the active shell env or system Python." >&2
        return 1
      fi
    fi
  fi

  if [[ "$resolved_mode" == "system" && "$allow_system" != "1" ]]; then
    echo "[ERROR] System Python is not allowed for env '$env_name'." >&2
    return 1
  fi

  RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV="$env_name"
  RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE="$resolved_mode"
  RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_COMMAND="$python_command"
  RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH="$venv_path"
  RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_CONDA_BIN="$conda_bin"
  RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND="$resolved_mode"
  RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME="$env_name"
  if [[ "$resolved_mode" == "conda" ]]; then
    RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH="$conda_path"
  elif [[ "$resolved_mode" == "venv" ]]; then
    RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH="$venv_path"
  else
    RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH="python3"
  fi
  RESOLVED_PYTHON_COMMAND="$python_command"
  export RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_COMMAND
  export RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_CONDA_BIN
  export RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH RESOLVED_PYTHON_COMMAND

  echo "Python environment context:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target Python env: ${env_name}"
  echo "  env manager: ${env_manager}"
  echo "  create missing: ${create_missing}"
  echo "  allow system: ${allow_system}"
  echo "  resolved mode: ${resolved_mode}"
  echo "  python command: ${python_command}"
}

InstallPythonPackagesIntoTargetEnv() {
  local requested_env="${1:-}"
  local package_var_name="${2:-}"
  local label="${3:-Python packages}"
  local package_value=""
  local -a packages=()

  [[ -n "${TARGET_USER:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] TARGET_USER is not set." >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -n "${TARGET_HOME:-}" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] TARGET_HOME is not set." >&2; return 1; }
  [[ -n "$package_var_name" ]] || { echo "[ERROR] Package variable name is required." >&2; return 2; }

  ResolveTargetPythonEnv "$requested_env" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve target Python env for package installation." >&2
    return 1
  }

  package_value="${!package_var_name:-}"
  if [[ -z "$package_value" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] No packages configured for ${package_var_name}; skipping ${label}."
    return 0
  fi
  read -r -a packages <<< "$package_value"
  ((${#packages[@]})) || {
    echo "[INFO] No packages configured for ${package_var_name}; skipping ${label}."
    return 0
  }

  echo "Python package installation context:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER}"
  echo "  target env: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved env kind: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved path: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved command: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_COMMAND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_COMMAND:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  package variable: ${package_var_name}"
  echo "  label: ${label}"

  case "${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-}}" in
    conda)
      [[ -n "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_CONDA_BIN:-}" ]] || {
        echo "[ERROR] Conda env was resolved but no conda binary was recorded." >&2
        return 1
      }
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_CONDA_BIN}" run -n "${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV}}" \
        python -m pip install --progress-bar off "${packages[@]}"
      ;;
    venv)
      [[ -n "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH:-}" && -x "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH}/bin/python" ]] || {
        echo "[ERROR] Venv was resolved but python executable is missing." >&2
        return 1
      }
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH}/bin/python" -m pip install --progress-bar off "${packages[@]}"
      ;;
    system)
      if [[ "${PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM:-0}" != "1" ]]; then
        echo "[ERROR] Refusing to install into system Python because PYTHON_ENV_ALLOW_SYSTEM is not 1." >&2
        return 1
      fi
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target python3 -m pip install --user --progress-bar off "${packages[@]}"
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unsupported resolved Python env kind: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-unset}}" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}
