InstallPandocObsidianTools() {
  echo "Publisher Pandoc/Obsidian tooling check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"

  if command -v pandoc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[OK] pandoc is available: $(command -v pandoc)"
  else
    echo "[INFO] pandoc is not installed."
    echo "[INFO] This step is currently a non-mutating check/stub."
    echo "[INFO] If safe apt-backed behavior is approved later, this step can be upgraded."
  fi

  echo "[INFO] Obsidian desktop presence is not checked automatically."
  echo "[INFO] No vault paths or manuscript paths are assumed by this step."
  return 0
}

CheckOpenClawBaseRequirements() {
  echo "OpenClaw base requirements check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  openclaw base packages var: PIP_OPENCLAW_BASE"
  echo "  openclaw full stack var: PIP_OPENCLAW_FULL_STACK"

  ResolveTargetPythonEnv "${1:-}" || {
    echo "[WARN] Target Python env could not be resolved for OpenClaw checks." >&2
    echo "[INFO] Configure the target Python env before running install_openclaw_full_stack."
    return 1
  }

  echo "[OK] Target Python env resolved for OpenClaw package installation."
  if command -v git >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[OK] git is available."
  else
    echo "[WARN] git is not available."
  fi
}

InstallOpenClawFullStack() {
  InstallPythonPackagesIntoTargetEnv "${1:-}" "PIP_OPENCLAW_FULL_STACK" "OpenClaw full Python stack"
}

CheckOpenClawAgentWorkspace() {
  local workspace="${OPENCLAW_WORKSPACE:-}"

  echo "OpenClaw agent workspace check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"

  if [[ -z "$workspace" ]]; then
    echo "[SKIP] OPENCLAW_WORKSPACE is not configured."
    echo "[INFO] Configure OPENCLAW_WORKSPACE explicitly before checking agent workspace state."
    return 0
  fi

  if [[ ! -d "$workspace" ]]; then
    echo "[WARN] OPENCLAW_WORKSPACE is configured, but the directory is not present."
    return 1
  fi

  echo "[OK] OPENCLAW_WORKSPACE is configured and the directory exists."
  if [[ -f "$workspace/main.py" ]]; then
    echo "[INFO] main.py is present in the configured workspace."
  else
    echo "[INFO] main.py is not present in the configured workspace."
  fi
  echo "[INFO] This check does not run an agent or execute main.py."
}

CheckDockerAccess() {
  echo "Docker access check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"

  if ! command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[WARN] docker is not installed or not in PATH."
    echo "[INFO] Use install_docker if Docker support is required."
    return 1
  fi

  echo "[OK] docker CLI is available: $(command -v docker)"
  if docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[OK] docker daemon is reachable by the current user."
  else
    echo "[WARN] docker CLI is present, but daemon access failed."
    echo "[INFO] This check does not create containers or run hello-world."
    return 1
  fi
}

CheckTerraformInstallation() {
  echo "Terraform installation check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"

  if ! command -v terraform >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[WARN] terraform is not installed or not in PATH."
    echo "[INFO] Use install_terraform if Terraform support is required."
    return 1
  fi

  echo "[OK] terraform is available: $(command -v terraform)"
  terraform version
}

CheckKubernetesAccess() {
  echo "Kubernetes access check:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"

  if ! command -v kubectl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[WARN] kubectl is not installed or not in PATH."
    echo "[INFO] Use install_kubernets if Kubernetes tooling is required."
    return 1
  fi

  echo "[OK] kubectl is available: $(command -v kubectl)"
  if kubectl version --client >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    kubectl version --client
  else
    echo "[WARN] kubectl client version check failed."
    return 1
  fi

  if kubectl config current-context >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[OK] kube context is configured: $(kubectl config current-context)"
  else
    echo "[INFO] No current kube context is configured."
  fi
  echo "[INFO] This check does not modify kube context or create clusters."
}

CheckGPUAvailability() {
  echo "GPU availability context:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"
  if command -v nvidia-smi >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    nvidia-smi
    return 0
  fi

  echo "[WARN] nvidia-smi is not available on this system."
  echo "[INFO] If GPU support is expected, verify NVIDIA drivers and runtime integration."
  return 1
}

CheckPytorchCuda() {
  local torch_check_code=$'import importlib.util\nimport sys\n\nspec = importlib.util.find_spec("torch")\nif spec is None:\n    print("[WARN] torch is not installed in the resolved target env.")\n    print("[INFO] Run install_pytorch_stack to add it.")\n    sys.exit(1)\n\nimport torch\nprint(f"torch_version={torch.__version__}")\nprint(f"cuda_available={torch.cuda.is_available()}")\nprint(f"cuda_device_count={torch.cuda.device_count()}")\nsys.exit(0 if torch.cuda.is_available() else 1)\n'

  ResolveTargetPythonEnv "${1:-}" || {
    echo "[ERROR] Could not resolve target Python env for PyTorch CUDA check." >&2
    return 1
  }

  echo "PyTorch CUDA check context:"
  echo "  target user: ${TARGET_USER}"
  echo "  target role: ${TARGET_ROLE:-"(unset)"}"
  echo "  target env: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved env kind: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved path: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_PATH:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH:-"(unset)"}}"
  echo "  resolved command: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_COMMAND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_COMMAND:-"(unset)"}}"

  case "${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-}}" in
    conda)
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_CONDA_BIN}" run -n "${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_NAME:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV}}" python -c \
        "$torch_check_code"
      ;;
    venv)
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target "${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_VENV_PATH}/bin/python" -c \
        "$torch_check_code"
      ;;
    system)
      resolve_target_python_env_run_target python3 -c \
        "$torch_check_code"
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unsupported resolved Python env kind for PyTorch CUDA check: ${RESOLVED_PYTHON_ENV_KIND:-${RESOLVED_TARGET_PYTHON_ENV_MODE:-unset}}" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

config_apt_lists_fresh() {
  local ttl="${CONFIG_APT_REFRESH_TTL_SECONDS:-14400}"
  local stamp

  [[ -d /var/lib/apt/lists ]] || return 1
  stamp="$(find /var/lib/apt/lists -type f -printf '%T@\n' 2>/dev/null | sort -nr | head -n1)"
  [[ -n "$stamp" ]] || return 1

  awk -v now="$(date +%s)" -v stamp="${stamp%.*}" -v ttl="$ttl" 'BEGIN { exit !((now - stamp) < ttl) }'
}

config_apt_refresh_if_stale() {
  if config_apt_lists_fresh; then
    echo "[SKIP] apt package lists are still fresh"
    return 0
  fi
  sudo apt-get update
}

config_retry() {
  local attempts="${CONFIG_RETRY_ATTEMPTS:-3}"
  local delay="${CONFIG_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS:-2}"
  local try=1
  local exit_code=0

  while (( try <= attempts )); do
    "$@"
    exit_code=$?
    if (( exit_code == 0 )); then
      return 0
    fi
    if (( try >= attempts )); then
      break
    fi

    echo "[WARN] Command failed (attempt ${try}/${attempts}): $*"
    echo "[INFO] Retrying in ${delay}s..."
    sleep "$delay"
    try=$((try + 1))
  done

  return "$exit_code"
}

config_curl_retry() {
  config_retry curl --fail --location --show-error "$@"
}

config_wget_retry() {
  config_retry wget "$@"
}

config_apt_install() {
  config_retry sudo apt-get install -y "$@"
}

config_validate_package_list() {
  local var_name="${1:-}"
  shift || true
  local pkg

  for pkg in "$@"; do
    [[ "$pkg" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9.+:_-]+$ ]] || {
      echo "[ERROR] Invalid package token in ${var_name}: $pkg" >&2
      return 1
    }
  done
}

config_apt_install_list() {
  local var_name="${1:-}"
  local fallback="${2:-}"
  local value=""
  local -a packages=()

  [[ -n "$var_name" ]] || return 2
  value="${!var_name:-$fallback}"
  read -r -a packages <<< "$value"
  ((${#packages[@]})) || return 0
  config_validate_package_list "$var_name" "${packages[@]}" || return 1
  config_apt_install "${packages[@]}"
}

config_apt_remove_if_installed() {
  if [[ $# -eq 0 ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  config_retry sudo apt-get remove -y "$@" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  config_retry sudo apt-get autoremove -y >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}

config_normalize_azure_cli_repo() {
  if [[ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.list && -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.sources ]]; then
    sudo rm -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/azure-cli.list
  fi
}

InstallMicrosoftPackagesRepo() {
  local ubuntu_version
  local debfile

  ubuntu_version="$(. /etc/os-release && printf '%s' "${VERSION_ID:-}")"
  case "$ubuntu_version" in
    20.04|22.04|24.04)
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[FAIL] Unsupported Ubuntu version for Microsoft packages: $ubuntu_version" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac

  if [[ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/microsoft-prod.list || -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/microsoft-prod.sources ]]; then
    return 0
  fi

  config_apt_install curl gpg ca-certificates apt-transport-https || return $?

  debfile="$(mktemp --suffix=.deb)"
  config_curl_retry -o "$debfile" "https://packages.microsoft.com/config/ubuntu/${ubuntu_version}/packages-microsoft-prod.deb" || {
    rm -f "$debfile"
    return 1
  }

  sudo dpkg -i "$debfile" || {
    rm -f "$debfile"
    return 1
  }
  rm -f "$debfile"

  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
}

UpdateAPT() {
  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
  sudo apt-get install -y software-properties-common wget
  sudo apt-get upgrade -y

  echo "##########################################"
  echo "# apt packages were updated successfully. #"
  echo "# Verify: apt --version                   #"
  echo "##########################################"
}

StandardApps() {
  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
  config_apt_install_list APT_STANDARD_APPS "cmatrix dos2unix ccze rs reminiscence xfce4 xfce4-terminal zsh" || return 1

  echo "############################################################"
  echo "# standard apps were installed successfully.                #"
  echo "# cmatrix, dos2unix, ccze, rs, reminiscence, xfce4, zsh     #"
  echo "############################################################"
}

InstallNetworking() {
  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
  config_apt_install_list APT_NETWORKING "ethtool net-tools wslu cifs-utils" || return 1

  echo "############################################################"
  echo "# networking tools were installed successfully.             #"
  echo "# ethtool, net-tools, wslu, cifs-utils                      #"
  echo "############################################################"
}

InstallDevEnvSystemPackages() {
  InstallMicrosoftPackagesRepo || return 1
  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
  config_apt_install_list APT_DEVENV_SYSTEM "dotnet-sdk-8.0 powershell python3-pip python3-venv make build-essential libssl-dev zlib1g-dev libbz2-dev libreadline-dev libsqlite3-dev wget curl llvm libncurses5-dev libncursesw5-dev xz-utils tk-dev libffi-dev liblzma-dev git" || return 1
}

InstallDevEnvShellInit() {
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.bashrc" 'export PYENV_ROOT="$HOME/.pyenv"'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.bashrc" 'export PATH="$PYENV_ROOT/bin:$HOME/.dotnet/tools:$PATH"'
  append_block_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.bashrc" '# >>> pyenv initialize >>>' '# >>> pyenv initialize >>>
if command -v pyenv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  eval "$(pyenv init --path)"
  eval "$(pyenv init -)"
fi
# <<< pyenv initialize <<<'
}

InstallDevEnvDotnetTools() {
  local dotnet_tools_log
  local dotnet_interactive_version="${CONFIG_DOTNET_INTERACTIVE_VERSION:-1.0.522904}"
  local pester_version="${CONFIG_PESTER_VERSION:-5.7.1}"
  dotnet_tools_log="$(mktemp)"

  if config_retry run_as_target_shell "dotnet tool update -g Microsoft.dotnet-interactive --version ${dotnet_interactive_version} || dotnet tool install -g Microsoft.dotnet-interactive --version ${dotnet_interactive_version}" >"$dotnet_tools_log" 2>&1; then
    cat "$dotnet_tools_log"
    rm -f "$dotnet_tools_log"
    run_as_target_shell 'dotnet interactive --help >/dev/null 2>&1 || true'

    if ! run_as_target_shell 'command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1'; then
      InstallMicrosoftPackagesRepo || return 1
      config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
      config_apt_install powershell || return 1
    fi

    if run_as_target_shell 'command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1'; then
      local pester_log
      local pester_bootstrap
      pester_log="$(mktemp)"
      pester_bootstrap="${TARGET_HOME}/.local/state/config-sh/install-pester.ps1"

      mkdir -p "$(dirname "$pester_bootstrap")" || return 1
      cat >"$pester_bootstrap" <<'EOF'
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$requiredVersion = [version]'__PESTER_VERSION__'
$installed = Get-Module -ListAvailable -Name Pester | Sort-Object Version -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
if ($installed -and $installed.Version -ge $requiredVersion) {
  Write-Host "Pester $($installed.Version) already available"
  exit 0
}
Install-Module Pester -Scope CurrentUser -Force -SkipPublisherCheck -MinimumVersion __PESTER_VERSION__
EOF
      sed -i "s/__PESTER_VERSION__/${pester_version}/g" "$pester_bootstrap"
      chown "$TARGET_USER":"$TARGET_USER" "$pester_bootstrap" 2>/dev/null || true

      if config_retry run_as_target_shell "pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -File \"$pester_bootstrap\"" >"$pester_log" 2>&1; then
        cat "$pester_log"
        rm -f "$pester_log" "$pester_bootstrap"
      else
        cat "$pester_log"
        rm -f "$pester_log" "$pester_bootstrap"
        return 1
      fi
    else
      echo "[FAIL] pwsh is still unavailable after attempting installation" >&2
      return 1
    fi

    return 0
  fi

  cat "$dotnet_tools_log"
  rm -f "$dotnet_tools_log"
  return 1
}

InstallDevEnvPythonUserTools() {
  run_as_target_shell 'python3 -m pip install --upgrade pip'
  run_as_target_shell 'python3 -m pip install --user virtualenv'
}

InstallDevEnvPyenv() {
  if [[ ! -d "${TARGET_HOME}/.pyenv" ]]; then
    run_as_target_shell 'curl -fsSL https://pyenv.run | bash'
  else
    echo "[INFO] pyenv already exists at ${TARGET_HOME}/.pyenv"
  fi
}

InstallDevEnvAnaconda() {
  local anaconda_root="${TARGET_HOME}/anaconda3"

  if [[ -x "$anaconda_root/bin/conda" ]]; then
    run_as_target_shell '"$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" update -n base -c defaults conda -y'
  else
    run_as_target_shell "wget -O \"\$HOME/${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER}\" \"https://repo.anaconda.com/archive/${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER}\"" || return 1
    run_as_target_shell "bash \"\$HOME/${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER}\" -b -p \"\$HOME/anaconda3\"" || return 1
    run_as_target_shell "rm -f \"\$HOME/${DEVENV_ANACONDA_INSTALLER}\""
  fi

  run_as_target_shell '"$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" config --set auto_activate_base false'
  run_as_target_shell '"$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" init bash'
}

InstallDevEnvAzureCLI() {
  config_normalize_azure_cli_repo
  local az_codename

  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return 1
  sudo apt-get install -y ca-certificates curl apt-transport-https lsb-release gnupg
  sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/keyrings

  curl -fsSL https://packages.microsoft.com/keys/microsoft.asc | gpg --dearmor | sudo tee "$DEVENV_MS_KEYRING" >/dev/null
  sudo chmod go+r "$DEVENV_MS_KEYRING"

  az_codename="$(lsb_release -cs)"

  sudo tee "$DEVENV_AZURE_REPO_FILE" >/dev/null <<EOF_AZURE_CLI_REPO
Types: deb
URIs: https://packages.microsoft.com/repos/azure-cli/
Suites: ${az_codename}
Components: main
Architectures: $(dpkg --print-architecture)
Signed-by: ${DEVENV_MS_KEYRING}
EOF_AZURE_CLI_REPO

  config_retry sudo apt-get update
  sudo apt-get install -y azure-cli
}

InstallDevEnvVerify() {
  local azure_verify_dir="${TARGET_HOME}/.local/state/azure-cli"
  mkdir -p "$azure_verify_dir"
  export AZURE_CONFIG_DIR="$azure_verify_dir"
  echo "##########################################"
  echo "# Dev environment status.                 #"
  echo "# system: dotnet sdk, python3, azure-cli  #"
  echo "# user: conda, pyenv, dotnet tool, pip    #"
  echo "##########################################"
  echo "[INFO] Verifying ..."

  dotnet --list-sdks || true
  python3 --version || true
  run_as_target_shell 'pyenv --version' 2>/dev/null || echo "[INFO] pyenv shell init not active yet"
  run_as_target_shell '"$HOME/anaconda3/bin/conda" --version' || true
  az version || true
}

InstallDevEnv() {
  InstallDevEnvSystemPackages
  InstallDevEnvShellInit
  InstallDevEnvDotnetTools
  InstallDevEnvPythonUserTools
  InstallDevEnvPyenv
  InstallDevEnvAnaconda
  InstallDevEnvAzureCLI
  InstallDevEnvVerify
}

InstallGUISupport() {
  config_apt_install_list APT_GUI_SUPPORT "mesa-utils libglu1-mesa-dev freeglut3-dev mesa-common-dev" || return 1
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" '# set DISPLAY to Windows X11 server'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" 'DISPLAY="$WINDOWS_IP:0"'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" '# Tell X11 programs to render on Windows, not linux, side'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" '# docs: https://docs.mesa3d.org/envvars.html'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" 'LIBGL_ALWAYS_INDIRECT=1'
  append_once_target "${TARGET_HOME}/.profile" 'export DISPLAY LIBGL_ALWAYS_INDIRECT'
}

InstallKubernetes() {
  local k8s_minor="${CONFIG_KUBERNETES_MINOR_VERSION:-v1.36}"
  local keyring="/etc/apt/keyrings/kubernetes-apt-keyring.gpg"
  local source_list="/etc/apt/sources.list.d/kubernetes.list"
  local release_url="https://pkgs.k8s.io/core:/stable:/${k8s_minor}/deb"

  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return $?
  config_apt_install apt-transport-https ca-certificates curl gpg || return $?
  sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/keyrings || return $?
  config_curl_retry "${release_url}/Release.key" \
    | sudo gpg --dearmor -o "$keyring" || return $?
  echo "deb [signed-by=${keyring}] ${release_url}/ /" \
    | sudo tee "$source_list" >/dev/null || return $?

  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
  config_apt_install kubectl kubelet kubeadm || return $?
  sudo apt-mark hold kubectl kubelet kubeadm >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}

install-kubernets() {
  InstallKubernetes "$@"
}

InstallMinikube() {
  local arch
  local asset
  local tmpfile

  case "$(uname -m)" in
    x86_64|amd64) arch="amd64" ;;
    aarch64|arm64) arch="arm64" ;;
    *)
      echo "[FAIL] Unsupported minikube architecture: $(uname -m)" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac

  asset="minikube-linux-${arch}"
  tmpfile="$(mktemp)"

  config_curl_retry -o "$tmpfile" "https://github.com/kubernetes/minikube/releases/latest/download/${asset}" || {
    rm -f "$tmpfile"
    return 1
  }

  sudo install "$tmpfile" /usr/local/bin/minikube || {
    rm -f "$tmpfile"
    return 1
  }

  rm -f "$tmpfile"
}

install-minikube() {
  InstallMinikube "$@"
}

InstallSQLServerSupport2004() {
  local ubuntu_version
  local debfile

  ubuntu_version="$(. /etc/os-release && printf '%s' "${VERSION_ID:-}")"
  case "$ubuntu_version" in
    20.04|22.04|24.04)
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[FAIL] Unsupported Ubuntu version for Microsoft SQL packages: $ubuntu_version" >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac

  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return $?
  config_apt_install curl gpg ca-certificates apt-transport-https || return $?

  debfile="$(mktemp --suffix=.deb)"
  config_curl_retry -o "$debfile" "https://packages.microsoft.com/config/ubuntu/${ubuntu_version}/packages-microsoft-prod.deb" || {
    rm -f "$debfile"
    return 1
  }

  sudo dpkg -i "$debfile" || {
    rm -f "$debfile"
    return 1
  }
  rm -f "$debfile"

  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
  config_retry sudo ACCEPT_EULA=Y apt-get install -y msodbcsql18 unixodbc-dev || return $?
}

InstallSQLServerCLITool2204() {
  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
  config_retry sudo ACCEPT_EULA=Y apt-get install -y mssql-tools18 || return $?
  append_once_target "$TARGET_HOME/.bashrc" 'export PATH="$PATH:/opt/mssql-tools18/bin"'
}

InstallDocker() {
  local arch
  local codename
  local keyring="/etc/apt/keyrings/docker.asc"
  local source_list="/etc/apt/sources.list.d/docker.list"

  arch="$(dpkg --print-architecture)"
  codename="$(. /etc/os-release && printf '%s' "${VERSION_CODENAME:-}")"

  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return $?
  config_apt_install ca-certificates curl || return $?

  sudo install -m 0755 -d /etc/apt/keyrings || return $?
  config_curl_retry -o /tmp/docker.asc https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu/gpg || return $?
  sudo install -m 0644 /tmp/docker.asc "$keyring" || return $?
  rm -f /tmp/docker.asc

  echo "deb [arch=${arch} signed-by=${keyring}] https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu ${codename} stable" \
    | sudo tee "$source_list" >/dev/null || return $?

  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
  config_apt_install docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin || return $?
  sudo usermod -aG docker "$TARGET_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}

InstallTerraform() {
  local arch
  local codename
  local keyring="/usr/share/keyrings/hashicorp-archive-keyring.gpg"
  local source_list="/etc/apt/sources.list.d/hashicorp.list"
  local tmpkey

  arch="$(dpkg --print-architecture)"
  codename="$(grep -oP '(?<=UBUNTU_CODENAME=).*' /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null || true)"
  if [[ -z "$codename" ]]; then
    codename="$(lsb_release -cs)"
  fi

  config_apt_refresh_if_stale || return $?
  config_apt_install gnupg curl ca-certificates || return $?
  sudo mkdir -p /usr/share/keyrings || return $?

  tmpkey="$(mktemp)" || return $?
  config_curl_retry https://apt.releases.hashicorp.com/gpg \
    | gpg --dearmor >"$tmpkey" || {
      rm -f "$tmpkey"
      return 1
    }
  sudo install -m 0644 "$tmpkey" "$keyring" || {
    rm -f "$tmpkey"
    return 1
  }
  rm -f "$tmpkey"
  echo "deb [arch=${arch} signed-by=${keyring}] https://apt.releases.hashicorp.com ${codename} main" \
    | sudo tee "$source_list" >/dev/null || return $?

  config_retry sudo apt-get update || return $?
  config_apt_install terraform || return $?
}

config_uninstall_step() {
  local name="$1"

  case "$name" in
    update_apt)
      return 0
      ;;
    standard_apps)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed \
        htop jq tree unzip zip ripgrep fd-find build-essential curl wget git
      ;;
    install_networking)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed \
        net-tools dnsutils iputils-ping traceroute nmap openssh-client
      ;;
    install_dev_env_system_packages)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed \
        dotnet-sdk-8.0 powershell dotnet-templates-8.0 dotnet-targeting-pack-8.0 \
        dotnet-apphost-pack-8.0 aspnetcore-targeting-pack-8.0 \
        aspnetcore-runtime-8.0 dotnet-runtime-8.0 dotnet-hostfxr-8.0 \
        dotnet-host-8.0 netstandard-targeting-pack-2.1-8.0 \
        python3-venv python3-pip llvm llvm-14 llvm-14-dev llvm-14-tools \
        llvm-14-runtime llvm-14-linker-tools libclang-cpp14 libllvm14 \
        libz3-dev libz3-4 libxss-dev libxml2-dev libxft-dev libxrender-dev \
        libxext-dev libx11-dev libxcb1-dev libxdmcp-dev libxau-dev \
        x11proto-dev x11proto-core-dev xorg-sgml-doctools xtrans-dev \
        libtk8.6 libtcl8.6 tk tk8.6 tk8.6-dev tk-dev tcl tcl8.6 \
        tcl8.6-dev tcl-dev libsqlite3-dev libssl-dev libreadline-dev \
        libpthread-stubs0-dev libpng-tools libpng-dev libncurses-dev \
        libncurses5-dev libncursesw5-dev libicu-dev libfontconfig-dev \
        libfontconfig1-dev libfreetype-dev libfreetype6-dev libffi-dev \
        libbz2-dev libbrotli-dev bzip2-doc icu-devtools pkg-config \
        uuid-dev liblzma-dev libpfm4 binfmt-support
      ;;
    install_dev_env_shell_init)
      return 0
      ;;
    install_dev_env_dotnet_tools)
      run_as_target_shell 'dotnet tool uninstall -g microsoft.dotnet-interactive >/dev/null 2>&1 || true'
      ;;
    install_dev_env_python_user_tools)
      run_as_target_shell 'python3 -m pip uninstall -y pipx virtualenv >/dev/null 2>&1 || true'
      run_as_target_shell 'rm -rf "$HOME/.local/pipx" "$HOME/.cache/pipx"'
      ;;
    install_dev_env_azure_cli)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed azure-cli apt-transport-https
      ;;
    install_dev_env_anaconda)
      rm -rf "$TARGET_HOME/anaconda3" "$TARGET_HOME/miniconda3"
      ;;
    install_dev_env_pyenv)
      rm -rf "$TARGET_HOME/.pyenv"
      ;;
    install_dev_env_verify)
      return 0
      ;;
    install_gui_support)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed x11-apps mesa-utils
      ;;
    install_docker)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed \
        docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin
      sudo rm -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/docker.list
      sudo rm -f /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.asc
      ;;
    install_terraform)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed terraform
      sudo rm -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/hashicorp.list
      sudo rm -f /etc/apt/keyrings/hashicorp-archive-keyring.gpg
      ;;
    install_kubernets)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed kubectl kubelet kubeadm
      ;;
    install_minikube)
      sudo rm -f /usr/local/bin/minikube
      run_as_target_shell 'rm -rf "$HOME/.minikube" "$HOME/.kube"'
      ;;
    install_sqlserver_support_2004)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed unixodbc unixodbc-dev msodbcsql17 msodbcsql18 mssql-tools mssql-tools18
      ;;
    install_sqlserver_cli_tool_2204)
      config_apt_remove_if_installed mssql-tools mssql-tools18
      ;;
    *)
      echo "[ERROR] Unsupported rm target: $name" >&2
      echo "[ERROR] Use 'config unskip $name' or remove the marker manually only if you really mean state cleanup." >&2
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
  echo "[ERROR] installers.sh is a source-only library. Use config instead." >&2
  exit 2
fi

# AN1-01 Postlog

Date: 2026-05-19
Milestone: `AN1-01 — Add Global Target Option Parsing`
File changed: `/home/vmuser/.local/bin/config.sh`

## Summary

Implemented milestone-scoped global option parsing and target application before command dispatch.

## Patch Details

1. Added parser state variables:
- `CONFIG_COMMAND`
- `CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS`
- `CONFIG_TARGET_USER`

2. Added minimal target helpers:
- `config_resolve_home_for_user()`
- `config_apply_target_user()`

`config_apply_target_user()` now:
- Validates non-empty user input.
- Resolves target home via `getent passwd`.
- Sets and exports:
  - `TARGET_USER`
  - `TARGET_HOME`
  - `CONFIG_STATE_DIR`
  - `STATE_DIR`

3. Added global parser:
- `config_parse_global_args()`

Supported pre-command globals:
- `--target USER`
- `-t USER`
- `--user USER`
- `--`

Behavior added:
- Missing value after target option returns exit `2` with clear error.
- Parsed command goes to `CONFIG_COMMAND`.
- Remaining arguments go to `CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS`.

4. Updated dispatch bootstrap:
- Replaced:
  - `cmd="${1:-help}"`
  - `shift || true`
- With:
  - `config_parse_global_args "$@" || exit $?`
  - `cmd="$CONFIG_COMMAND"`
  - `set -- "${CONFIG_COMMAND_ARGS[@]}"`

Full content for /home/vmuser/.local/patches/AN1_07C_plan_file_authority_postcheck.log:
AN1-07C postcheck — 2026-06-11

Validated with real sudo commands against target `labuser`.

Observed behavior:
- bare `sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply`
  - detected drift for `install_dev_env_dotnet_tools` (`plan=skipped`, `marker=none`)
  - defaulted to `markers-win`
  - rewrote `bootstrap.plan` from current markers
  - resulting state: `install_dev_env_dotnet_tools` became `pending`, completed steps remained effectively adopted from markers

- `sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan`
  - showed `done` for `update_apt`, `standard_apps`, `install_networking`
  - showed `pending` for `install_dev_env_dotnet_tools`
  - showed `skipped` for the remaining skipped steps

- `sudo config --target labuser bootstrap status`
  - matched `plan` output after bare apply

- explicit `sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan-apply --plan-wins`
  - removed conflicting `.done` markers for `update_apt`, `standard_apps`, `install_networking`
  - applied `.skipped` markers so marker state matched the plan
  - final diff showed `skipped/skipped` alignment for skipped steps and `pending/none` for `install_dev_env_dotnet_tools`

- `sudo config --target labuser bootstrap plan` after `--plan-wins`
  - showed skipped for the three previously-done steps, confirming marker reset to plan authority

Result:
- PASS: bare `plan-apply` now defaults to adopting marker state into the plan file
- PASS: explicit `--plan-wins` now resets markers to match plan authority
- PASS: `status` now reflects marker precedence correctly

Notes:
- current drift handling still prints a warning before markers-win rewrites the plan when a step is `skipped/none`; this is acceptable but could be softened later if desired.

--- config help profiles ---
Profiles:

  Account profiles are creation templates under:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/profiles.tsv

SMB profiles choose SMB usernames and default mount identity:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/smb-profiles.tsv

  Mount profiles choose which shares are enabled:
    /home/vmuser/.local/etc/config-sh/accounts/mount-profiles.tsv

  Operator defaults:
    SMB profile = operator
    mount profile = all

  Target defaults:
    SMB profile = target or a role-specific SMB profile
    mount profile = target, research, or publish

  Account profiles generate or reconcile per-target env files.
  They do not replace runtime state under state/config-sh.
